# 1: Chapter 1: D

He opened his eyes.

Everything around was quiet.

The pendulum of the old-fashioned clock hanging on the wall swung silently.

D suddenly sat up from the bed, his chest rising and falling, breathing heavily.

What is this place?

D instinctively observed the surroundings, feeling confused and puzzled.

This was a small log cabin.

A single bed was placed against the wall inside the room, and opposite the bed was a desk with a natural wood finish, casually adorned with a bulging backpack.

A yellowed calendar, looking aged, hung on the wall beside it, and on the other side of the room stood a wooden cabinet, next to which the stove was piled with coal-like things and kitchenware such as pots and pans.

Staring at this unfamiliar scene, D felt even more bewildered.

What is going on?

Shouldn't he be working overtime at the company right now? Why is he here?

"Ow... my head hurts!"

Suddenly, something extra appeared in his mind, causing D's brain to swell.

A flood of memories rushed in like a tide, and once he digested it, D's face instantly showed an expression of disbelief.

Time travel?

After a brief moment of astonishment, D slowly sorted out his current situation.

He had indeed traveled through time.

Fatigue from five consecutive days of overtime had led to his sudden death, bringing him to this world.

This was a world entirely different from Earth.

Besides humans, it was inhabited by creatures known as "Pokemon." Whether in the sky, the ocean, or on land, you could see them in every corner of this world.

"It's actually the Pokemon world!" D was somewhat delighted.

He couldn't claim to know everything about Pokemon, but he had played and watched both the games and the anime.

In his youth, he had once yearned to live in this world.

As everyone knows, this world is famously relaxed and leisurely; as long as a person is ten years old, they can receive an initial Pokemon and travel around the world with it.

Since he had traversed it, could he also travel freely like Ash in the anime?

The thought of escaping the 9-to-9 work schedule, the housing and car loans, of no longer being a useless corporate drone stirred something within D.

But soon, he calmed down again.

Reality is often bitter compared to one's dreams.

The person D crossed into shared his name and was originally from the Paldea Region, having moved with his parents to the Hoenn Region when he was young.

Shortly after that, both of the original's parents passed away due to illness—their move to the Hoenn Region was in pursuit of recovery.

After their passing, the original sold off property, seemingly disillusioned with worldly affairs, and wandered without attachments for many years.

Finally, at the age of twenty, he chose to return to his hometown in the Paldea Region, to the ranch left by his grandfather who died early and ended his life there.

Although this was the Pokemon world, the original never captured any Pokemon, as he did not want to form attachments.

These memories surfaced in his mind, leaving D a bit sentimental, prompting him to sigh.

"Gurgle, gurgle..."

His empty stomach protested loudly, and D glanced at the time.

2:00AM

Two in the morning, and from the looks of it, the original had probably not had dinner.

D sighed, whispered an apology in his heart, then got out of bed. Based on his memories, he opened the original's backpack, took out a packet of instant noodles, fetched some water into a pot, and set it to boil on the coal stove.

While waiting for the water to boil, D searched around but found nothing edible aside from some basic seasonings.

Though this was a ranch, it had been abandoned for years. The fields of the ranch were already overrun by weeds and stones, making it impossible to have any vegetables or fruits, let alone eggs.

Memories of the ranch's prosperity still lingered in his mind.

At that time, the ranch was lively and thriving, with orderly fields growing various crops, and the branches of fruit trees laden with heavy fruit.

The Pokemon House housed a group of Miltank imported from the Johto Region and Wooloo from the Galar Region, with rows of beehives placed on the grass outside the house.

Combee would collect nectar from outside and produce honey in the hives, yielding jars of crystal-clear, fragrant, rich, golden, and sticky honey every few days.

At this point, the water was boiling.

Just as D tore open the ramen package and was about to drop it into the water, a piece of information suddenly appeared in his mind.

[Instant Ramen: A basic food of low quality with little nutritional value, but due to its convenience in cooking, it is favored by many outdoor workers and travelers, effect unknown.]

Huh? What is this...

D inexplicably felt this was somewhat familiar.

When the ramen was placed into the water, with the boiling water, the once hard noodles quickly softened.

D added some seasonings to it.

He wasn't exactly skilled in cooking, only knowing a little bit.

Due to living alone, he prepared most of his meals himself because it was more economical, eventually learning to cook over time.

Soon, the ramen was ready.

D didn't think much of it, poured it into a bowl, then sat at the desk ready to fill his belly.

But just at that moment, the familiar information reappeared in his mind.

[Salt-Overloaded Clear Soup Noodles (D-): A low-tier dish, whose added excessive salt has removed its original bonus effects. It merely fills the belly, but consuming too much of it will cause thirst. Pokemon that like salty flavors might enjoy it, Evaluation: Terrible, but not bad enough to cause Pokemon to faint after eating.]

D's brow twitched.

Good grief!

He finally remembered where this familiar feeling came from!

Isn't this from the cooking introduction interface in a game called [Culinary Master] developed by their game company?

It was a trash game marketed under the guise of gourmet food to scam money.

The game was a nurturing type, with settings where players possessed magical abilities.

The dishes they made could provide various attributes and bonuses to the characters inside, along with other strange and wonderful magical abilities.

The range and quality of bonuses linked to the players' cooking levels—like most junk games, the cooking levels in it ranged from F- to SSS.

The level of dishes also depended on the rarity of the ingredients, the quality, and the player's cooking skills.

D- could only be described as mediocre; if it tasted worse, like F-, it might even apply debuffs to the game characters, reducing their attributes and causing them to die quickly in battles with enemies...

"What's going on?" D was slightly dazed, recalling the web novels he had read in the past, "Could this be my Golden Finger?"

D, staring at the Pokemon mentioned in the description above, picked up the bowl and began eating the noodles.

As the noodles entered his mouth, a salty taste filled his entire palate.

D: "..."

Sure enough, there's too much salt.

Though he wasn't used to eating it, it was the only food left in the house.

D endured, eating while continuing to explore the situation with his Golden Finger.

It seems that the dishes he now made could grant various magical abilities and bonuses to the Pokemon that consumed them.

Probably?

---

---

---

---

# 2: Chapter 2: Past Days at the Ranch

Although D had a rough guess about the effects of the "Golden Finger," due to some real-world reasons, he couldn't attempt to use it.

First, he didn't have any Pokemon around him, and secondly, the original owner was just too poor...

At three in the morning, D, having barely filled his stomach, began pondering his current predicament.

For him, traversing into this world was indeed a joyful event, but regardless of which world it was, or whatever he wanted to do, he needed to fill his stomach first.

D cleaned the dishes and then sat at the desk to sort out the original owner's belongings.

As a wanderer away from home, the original owner's backpack was very simple.

Apart from a few changes of clothes, there were just three packs of instant noodles and a pile of scattered coins and notes.

D counted, the total amount was around 2300.

This amount of money wouldn't last him a travel journey; he'd likely starve to death in less than a month.

After doing some mental calculations about the conversion of this world's prices, D couldn't help but sigh into his hands.

Sigh, it was too difficult for him...

But thinking of the cute and majestic Pokemon in anime, D revived his spirits and lifted himself up.

"Take it step by step!"

Maybe from not having a good rest before traveling or due to this body being overly exhausted, after filling his stomach, D quickly felt an overwhelming drowsiness come upon him.

He did not resist, thinking he could finally have a good sleep, not needing to worry about being late for work tomorrow, nor having to skimp and save to repay the mortgage. Tomorrow would be a new day.

Turning off the light and lying on the wooden bed, D soon fell into a deep sleep.

Early the next morning, he was awakened by a knocking sound at the door.

D opened his eyes abruptly, sitting up in tension, and upon observing his surroundings, he suddenly realized the fact that he had already crossed over to another world.

He breathed a sigh of relief, with the knocking sound outside continuing.

D was a bit puzzled, got out of bed with messy hair, opened the door, and saw an elderly man wearing a brown vest, slightly pudgy, with a black gentleman's hat standing outside.

The elderly man appeared to be between sixty and seventy years old; seeing him come out, he displayed a warm and kind smile.

Beside him stood a creature that stood on two feet, resembling a lizard.

The creature was green, shiny, with a smooth sheen under the Morning Sun, a tire-like structure around its neck, and a sturdy, dark green resilient tail gently swaying behind it, with the tip of the tail a translucent white.

It opened its big eyes, curiously examining him.

D was instantly drawn to it and recognized this Pokemon immediately — Cyclizar.

And the elderly man before him was the mayor of Porto Marinada Town.

The old man spoke, "I heard yesterday you said you plan to stay and manage this ranch, and I thought you might be unfamiliar with ranch work, moreover, cleaning alone can be tiring, so I brought this child over."

D was slightly taken aback; after a moment's recollection, a memory surfaced in his mind:

Yesterday morning, when the original owner returned to the ranch, he happened to encounter the old mayor, who recognized him and after some niceties, said:

"Here is the inheritance your grandfather left you. I knew one day you would return, so I come by periodically to help clean it. Although it has been abandoned for many years, the house is still livable. Feel free to stay here!"

The original owner decently thanked him.

The old mayor showed a smile, "When I was young, I was friends with your grandfather. When you were little, I held you! It's something your grandfather entrusted me with before he passed! I must hand over the ranch to you!"

— The old mayor apparently thought the original owner returned to take care of the ranch, D thought.

Realizing this, D instantly understood the situation, but looking at this Cyclizar, he was still a bit puzzled.

Seeing the tall and lanky youth before him, the old mayor couldn't help but feel a bit of sympathy and regret.

Such a pitiful child! All his family has passed away, leaving him alone in this world.

After seeing his state yesterday, the old mayor went home and brought over the child of his own Cyclizar.

One reason was for Cyclizar to stay by his side so they'd have company, and secondly, Cyclizar could also help along with the ranch work.

The old mayor sighed in his heart, but outwardly, he gently asked, "Can you help me take care of it?"

D: "..."

As an adult, he immediately understood what the old mayor meant.

D looked at Cyclizar.

Cyclizar also gazed back with wide eyes, innocent and curious.

Cyclizar, a Pokemon of Normal and Dragon Type, has lived alongside households in the Paldea Region since ancient times.

They're gentle by nature and, due to their aversion to cold, can use human body heat to warm their backs, allowing humans to ride on their backs.

Therefore, in this particular region of Paldea, Cyclizar also serves as a mode of transportation.

D couldn't think of a reason to refuse because he wanted to have his own Pokemon as well.

Receiving a starter Pokemon from the local professor, like Ash in the anime, to travel together seemed impossible.

He didn't have any Poke Balls on him, and it was even less possible to capture wild Pokemon with just his fists and feet.

---

"Thank you, I'll take good care of it," D said.

Hearing this, the old mayor felt relieved and smiled: "Since that's the case, I'll leave this child to you. Oh by the way, since it's your first day at the ranch, you're probably not familiar with the work here, are you?"

Used to the 996 work culture in his previous life, everything in front of D was indeed somewhat unfamiliar.

"Yes." D nodded, at this moment, the questions he pondered over last night also found answers.

Now with nowhere else to go, his understanding of this world was limited to what he had seen in anime and games before.

Given his current situation, staying in this place was the best choice, at least it provided him a shelter from the elements.

The old mayor laughed and said: "Since that's the case, let me show you around this ranch! But speaking of which, you left Paldea when you were ten and settled in Hoenn, right?"

D: "Yes."

The old mayor sighed: "In the blink of an eye, ten years have passed, time really flies!"

The once prosperous and lively scenes were still vivid in his mind, but now, after the hustle and bustle, the place was deserted, leaving behind a desolate and dilapidated ranch.

The farmland was overrun by weeds and stones, the Pokemon House eroded by time, leaving only a framework, its shape barely recognizable.

The two walked through the ranch, Cyclizar obediently following behind, sneaking glances at the human youth.

D hadn't noticed, having decided to stay and manage the ranch, he listened intently to what the old mayor said.

The old mayor's eyes were filled with nostalgia, and with every spot they visited, memories of the past surfaced in his mind.

"The piece of land before us is the farm in the ranch. Long ago, various crops were grown here, and every autumn, a variety of fruits would be harvested."

"That house is where the rancher lives, and the big box next to it is the shipping box. You can put harvested crops and milk in there."

"To the east of the ranch is a valley, where many small, mild-mannered Pokemon reside. You can gather mountain produce and berries there, and put what's not needed into the shipping box."

"Every day at 5 PM, someone from town comes to take what's in the box and settle the accounts. The collected goods are sent to Porto Marinada Market to be sold."

"When you develop further, a special vehicle will come to transport goods! They'll deliver the ranch's produce to every corner of the Paldea Region, selling to different people."

D: "..."

What is this, Harvest Moon?

Fortunately, having played farming games before, he was quite familiar with this kind of content and quickly accepted these matters.

At this moment, D caught sight of an abandoned building surrounded by large trees and weeds.

Curiously, he asked: "What's that?"

The old mayor took a glance and said regretfully: "That's the Pokemon House in the ranch. Your grandpa used to import about a dozen Miltank from the Johto Region to raise there."

Upon saying this, the old mayor furrowed his brows and let out a sigh, continuing:

"After he passed away, those Miltanks were sold by your parents, and the Pokemon House gradually fell into disrepair."

"But you don't have to worry, once you get the hang of things, you can commission the carpentry shop to help expand the residence and repair the Pokemon House."

"Oh, by the way, I've put the tools you'll need in the ranch over there."

The old mayor pointed to the storage room behind the house and said: "An axe for cutting trees, a hammer for smashing stones, a hoe for farming, a watering can for irrigation, a sickle for cutting grass, and some crop seeds suitable for spring planting, in short, this ranch is entrusted to you!"

"I hope you can take good care of the ranch and let it regain its former glory. If that day comes, your grandpa would definitely be very happy!"

D remained silent for a moment, then nodded and said: "I'll try my best."

Hearing his response, a smile appeared on the old mayor's face: "Since that's the case, I'll say no more, Cyclizar is entrusted to your care, it's quite strong and will surely help you manage the ranch."

Upon hearing this, Cyclizar let out a happy growl: "Gah-oo!"

The old mayor smiled and prepared to turn and leave.

But before he could walk a few meters, he suddenly turned back: "One more thing!"

D hurriedly said: "Please tell me."

The old mayor: "You can keep the wood and stone obtained from chopping trees and smashing rocks, and once you've saved enough money, take them to the carpentry shop to help expand your residence and the Pokemon House, saving a lot on materials!"

D made a note of this.

The old mayor, from a distance, took off his hat and said warmly: "I live in Porto Marinada Town. If you need anything, come find me. Cyclizar knows the way, you can ride it there directly."

"Okay." D nodded.

With that, the old mayor left.

In the year 198 of the League Calendar, spring had just arrived in the Paldea Region, and the temperature hadn't fully warmed up yet.

D and Cyclizar stood at the doorway of the cabin, watching the figure of the old mayor gradually disappear.

Only after the old mayor completely vanished did D turn his head to look at the shiny Cyclizar beside him.

Although he was an outsider, judging from Cyclizar's appearance and demeanor, it was easy to tell that this Cyclizar was very healthy and well cared for.

Thinking about the magical setting of Pokemon, D's curiosity was piqued, and he couldn't help but say: "Cyclizar?"

"Gah-oo?" Cyclizar turned its head to look over.

Sure enough, it knew it was a Cyclizar.

D asked again: "Can you understand what I'm saying? If you can, nod your head."

"Gah-oo~"

Cyclizar indeed nodded its head obediently.

Just like in the anime, Pokemon could directly understand human language!

D's heart was thoroughly replaced by novelty.

According to the setting, Pokemon are extraordinary creatures with intelligence and emotions far beyond ordinary animals.

Even some Pokemon's intelligence and emotions are said to be on par with humans.

Looking at Cyclizar's shiny skin, D couldn't resist extending his hand to touch it.

The texture was different than he imagined, not as terrifying as a real-life lizard, but rather like leather, similar to a motorcycle.

Cyclizar showed no signs of displeasure at being touched; instead, it closed its eyes contentedly and seemed quite pleased.

Imitating Ash from the anime, D said to Cyclizar: "Cyclizar, let's take good care of each other in the future!"

"Gah-oo~"

After that, D didn't know what else to say.

He withdrew his hand, and his stomach suddenly growled.

Cyclizar heard this sound and looked at D's stomach.

Feeling somewhat embarrassed, D asked: "Have you eaten yet? I'm about to cook, want to join me?"

"Gah-oo~"

Although Cyclizar had already eaten breakfast, it was still very happy about the new owner's invitation.

It nodded and followed D back to the wooden cabin.

Standing in front of the cabin, at this moment, a different feeling emerged in D's heart.

He took a deep breath, thinking that this would be his home from now on!

Having worked the 996 life in his past existence, being a leisurely farmer in this life seemed not bad at all.

Being a rancher might be tiring, but at least he was working for himself here.

A new life was about to begin, D took a deep breath and glanced at Cyclizar beside him.

Speak of the devil, last night he was worrying about not having a Pokemon to test his golden finger on, and this morning he got a Cyclizar.

If that's so, then it's decided!

D started to heat oil, no, boil water, preparing to cook a bowl of clear broth noodles, to see what effect it might have and if it would provide various magical benefits to Pokemon, just like in the game.

---

---

---

---

# 3: Chapter 3: Hot Soup Noodles

As the most basic dish, clear soup noodle is very simple to make.

Boil water in a pot until it boils, then add instant noodles.

If the ingredients are sufficient, D would add chopped green onions and lard to let the noodles absorb the soup. When the noodles are served in a bowl, the green onions float on top of the soup, adding a hint of green to the dish, making it more appetizing.

Finally, a drop of sesame oil is added, and a soft-boiled egg that was prepared in advance is put in. When pierced with chopsticks, the golden yolk slowly flows out and blends with the noodle soup.

Just thinking about it makes D salivate.

However, returning to reality, only the basic seasonings were in front of him.

Sighing silently in his heart, D learned from yesterday's lesson and added a suitable amount of salt and chicken powder into the pot.

Soon, a simple bowl of soup noodles was ready.

D served two bowls, and as he picked them up, he looked down, and the information about the soup noodles appeared in his mind.

[Hot Soup Noodles (C-): A low-grade dish, slightly lacking in flavor due to missing key ingredients but a good choice for warming the stomach on a cold winter's day.

Cooking Effect: Cold Resistance lv1, with a resistance effect to endure severe cold, making the body warm after drinking, duration 30 minutes.

Evaluation: Although the quality of the ingredients is low, the skilled cooking technique gives it a touch of unique flavor, and when players become wealthy and can cook master-level dishes in the future, they may recall these days fondly.]

D: "..."

At this time, Cyclizar sniffed the scent wafting through the air.

"Raaaw?"

D looked at it, then brought the two bowls to the table, inviting Cyclizar: "Want to try it?"

"Raaaw…"

Cyclizar wrinkled its brow uneasily. To be honest, as a Cyclizar, it wasn't very fond of that smell.

Moreover, apart from the plain white noodles, there was nothing else in the bowl, making the dish seem unappealing from the outside.

Seeing this, D immediately understood Cyclizar's thoughts, sighed helplessly in his heart, didn't insist, and instead comforted: "Forget it, if you don't want to eat, then don't!"

Saying this, he animatedly fantasized: "When I make money later and buy other ingredients, I'll make you the food you like!"

D was filled with anticipation for the upcoming ranch life.

Hearing this, Cyclizar felt guilty instead.

This was the food specially prepared for it by its new owner. Wasn't it not so great if it didn't at least try the taste?

Thinking about this, Cyclizar lowered its head to look at the soup noodles in front of it, hesitated, and said: "Raaaw!"

Then it decided to eat this bowl of noodles!

Cyclizar stretched out its two chubby paws, picked up the hot soup noodles in front of it, and gulped them down.

The imagined bad situation did not occur. The taste of the noodles was quite plain but, after drinking it, Cyclizar instantly felt its body warm up as if it had returned to its mother's embrace.

"Raaaw…"

Seeing Cyclizar's sudden action, D was first slightly surprised but then quickly calmed down.

Looking at Cyclizar, thinking of the effect of the hot soup noodles, he asked with a smile: "How does it feel?"

Hearing D's voice, Cyclizar snapped back to reality: "Raaaw!"

Then it confusedly lowered its head, looking at its own body.

Clearly, no human was riding on its back, so why did it suddenly feel warm?

Seeing its action, D suddenly got a general idea in his heart.

"It seems that the dish indeed brings benefits to the Pokemon."

D thought, he continued to ask: "Did you feel your body getting warmer, not as cold as before?"

Although it was spring, the temperature hadn't fully risen yet, especially in the morning, it was still chilly.

Cyclizar raised its head in surprise, looking over, as if saying: How did you know?

"Raaaw?"

D laughed: "That's the power of food."

"Raaaw…"

Cyclizar looked puzzled; this statement was too advanced for it.

Because it's just a Cyclizar.

D did not continue to explain, anyway, living together in the future, Cyclizar would understand sooner or later.

Having confirmed that his dish had effects on Pokemon, D was in a great mood, quickly finished his breakfast, cleaned the dishes, and then walked out of the cabin to the outside.

The overgrown ranch was full of lush vegetation, and above, from time to time, a bird-type Pokemon flew overhead, out to forage.

Breathing in the fresh air around, D took a deep breath and then prepared to take a walk around.

He intended to first become familiar with the place.

Walking through the ranch with Cyclizar, D found that the size of this ranch was larger than he had imagined.

The edge of the ranch was surrounded by wooden fences, and the middle area was very large, with a thriving valley on the east side, and standing in the middle of the ranch, one could hear the growl of wild Pokemon coming from there.

The farmland area occupied one-third of the ranch, the southwest corner had a lake with a surrounding river, and there was a wooden bridge over the river connecting the ranch to the opposite bank.

D stood on the bridge, looking at the decayed and broken wooden fence along the riverbank, thinking that he had a lot of work to do.

Clearing weeds, rocks, cutting down excess trees, and re-cultivating land to prepare for planting.

As for the Pokemon House meant for raising Miltank, D didn't plan to repair it now.

First, there wasn't enough wood, and secondly, he lacked the funds.

After all, whether rebuilding the Pokemon House or purchasing Miltank and those Pokemon required a considerable amount of funds.

Standing by the lake at the edge of the farmland, D squatted down, observing the scale and depth of the lake, thinking: "Raising fish here would be a very good choice."

According to the original owner's memory, besides Pokemon, this world also had ordinary animals.

However, instead of regular fish, D wanted to raise some Water Type Pokemon.

Like Magikarp, Goldeen, Feebas, and Marshtomp, for example.

Or even bolder, like Gyarados, Milotic, Vaporeon, Primarina.

Although these fish can't be eaten, maybe because of the obsession of being a collecting player, just keeping them here and watching them made D very satisfied.

D organized these matters in his mind and realized there were many, many things waiting for him to do.

Far from things that could be completed in a short time.

Thinking of those ranch management games he used to play, D was momentarily speechless.

He had just escaped the 996 (work schedule), was he going to 007 now?

---

---

---

---

# 4: Chapter 4: Koraidon

After strolling around the ranch, D had imprinted the entire terrain map of the ranch in his mind.

While rushing back with Cyclizar, he suddenly recalled the former town mayor saying that he had left something for him in the storeroom behind the house.

D walked around the wooden cabin and arrived at the storeroom. Pushing open the door, he saw various tools placed atop a large box.

Hammer, sickle, hoe, sprayer, axe, a pack of crop seeds wrapped in a red packaging bag, and a brown leather book titled "Pasture Encyclopedia."

Most of these tools were made of iron, and they appeared quite new, likely purchased specially by the town mayor to be gifted.

In his heart, D silently expressed gratitude as he was currently quite short on funds.

He stepped forward to collect these tools and then picked up the pack of seeds.

Information about the seeds immediately popped into his mind.

[Turnip Seeds: Spring crop, originally requiring three months to mature, but in this world, it takes only about a month to harvest, very suitable for novice ranchers to plant. If attended by Grass Type Pokemon, its growth cycle will be further shortened.]

"Eh?" D was somewhat shocked: "They grow so fast?"

He carefully recalled the original owner's memory, and after quite a while he finally found relevant information from deep within those memories.

—This is how the world naturally is, with fast-growing crops and short cycles.

Because of this, humans here have hardly ever experienced food shortages.

"No wonder the Pokemon World feels like a utopia..." D muttered in his heart.

Humans need not worry about food issues, coupled with the existence of Pokemon, productivity has been greatly liberated, enabling young people to freely travel and pursue what they wish to do.

However, from the original owner's memory, D learned that not all crops grow this quickly.

There are some crops with very long growth cycles, maturing almost once every season.

D nodded silently, then picked up the book, opened the flyleaf, and found it recorded methods for planting and nurturing crops.

Every page contained different crops, detailing how to plow fields, timing for crop planting, fertilization methods, watering cycles, and other content.

After casually flipping through a few pages, D cherishingly stored it away and left the storeroom with Cyclizar.

Opposite the storeroom was a cave, D walked inside and discovered that the cave's internal space was not large.

From childhood memories of the original owner, it could be known that this cave had two main functions.

One was for mushroom cultivation, as such dark and damp places are ideally suited for mushrooms to grow.

The second was for raising Noibat. The Noibat residing here would head outside after sunset, foraging and bringing back various berries and normal fruits for the rancher.

This magical Pokemon is naturally a gourmet.

They use their ears to emit supersonic waves of 200,000 Hz to find ripe fruit, and blast at the fruit using supersonic waves, exclusively selecting matured ones to consume.

"Interesting." D thought to himself.

After understanding the cave's uses, D didn't linger there.

He returned to the front with the tools, and looking at a weed-covered field, decided to proceed step by step, first clearing a small area for planting turnips.

Seeing his actions, the nearby Cyclizar eagerly volunteered to assist: "Gyaa-oh!"

With a swing of its tail, accompanied by the heavy descent of the tail, the stone seemed heavily struck, shattering into pieces.

D: "..."

He glanced down at the hammer in his hand, and at the stone he had painstakingly cracked, unsure what to say for a moment.

Cyclizar seemed pleased that it could be of help.

Looking at its demeanor, D sighed helplessly and said with relief, "Let's work together then!"

"Gyaa-oh!"

————

Meanwhile, in the central region of Paldea, within a basin surrounded by towering and steep mountains.

This is the Paldea Giant Pit, a mysterious area known as "Zone Zero."

Below this area sits a modernized, advanced laboratory.

Deep within the laboratory, Pokemon Doctor Professor Sada and her husband, Professor Turo, gazed expectantly and excitedly at the tall, mysterious advanced machine before them.

"The Time Machine is finally successfully developed!"

Professor Sada, clad in a white lab coat and wearing a necklace resembling ancient beast teeth as decoration, exclaimed excitedly.

"Finally, we can see those Pokemon from the future and past recorded in the Book of Scarlet and Violet!" Professor Turo was equally excited.

He and his wife spent many years stationed in the deepest part of the Paldea Giant Pit conducting this research.

They specialized respectively in ancient and future research and finally, utilizing the special area deep within the Paldea Giant Pit, developed this device.

It can transmit Poke Balls, capture Pokemon from different timelines, and summon them to modern-day.

"Let's try it now! I want to witness those Pokemon from different eras with my own eyes!" Professor Sada declared.

Professor Turo nodded, and had a moment of tenderness, suddenly asking, "Isn't Arven's eighth birthday coming soon?"

Professor Sada nodded, "Coincidentally, we can bring those era-crossing Pokemon back to accompany Arven and share life together."

"Then let's begin!" Professor Turo also shared this thought.

He took a deep breath and began looking forward to seeing what expression Arven would make upon seeing those Pokemon.

The child always loved Pokemon; if he saw Pokemon from the future and past, he would surely be delighted, right?

There are no parents who don't love their child, and thinking about it, they've already been away from him for quite some time.

This time, they planned to return and take a vacation to spend quality time with Arven.

Professor Turo thought silently while he and his wife used ID codes to start the Time Machine.

"Wong!"

With a peculiar humming sound, the Time Machine quietly started.

The space around the laboratory began to undergo strange changes, the walls rhythmically glowing with red and purple hues like mirrors.

The duo stared unblinkingly at the Time Machine, excited and nervous.

Soon, the rhythm around slowed down.

Subsequently, a dazzling red light flashed from the center of the Time Machine.

Both their hearts were tense to the extreme.

As the light gradually faded, a Pokemon they had never seen appeared below the Time Machine.

It was a tall, strong, bipedal mysterious Pokemon.

Its physique crimson red, with white accents on its face, abdomen, thick legs, and tail, and a slender, long antenna-like tendril atop its head adorned with a cluster of mesmerizing blue-purple feathers, appearing formidable and flamboyant.

It had a pair of golden eyes, gazing fiercely at the two humans opposite.

Success!

Professor Sada and Professor Turo were relieved upon seeing the Pokemon.

Finally! Their efforts hadn't gone to waste!

"Quickly, use the Poke Ball to contain it!" Professor Turo calmly suggested.

Professor Sada nodded, raised the pre-prepared Poke Ball, and tossed it towards the Pokemon.

However, as soon as the Pokemon saw the Poke Ball, its golden eyes suddenly narrowed into a slit.

With an indistinguishable roar from its mouth, it casually swung its right claw, sending the Poke Ball flying away.

Professors Sada and Turo felt a hint of hostility.

They tensed up, about to release the Pokemon beside them.

But they noticed that the mysterious Pokemon from the past suddenly turned its head to survey the surroundings, seemingly confirming something.

Soon, it seemed to have the answer.

In the next moment, emotions appeared in the Pokemon's eyes that were unreadable; with powerful thrusts from its legs, it ignored the two professors, striding out of the laboratory.

Its speed was remarkable; before Professors Sada and Turo could react, it had dashed outside the lab in one breath.

Then it looked upwards, its head feathers unfolding into wing-like structures, and it darted upward.

How long had it waited?

Koraidon no longer remembered.

The imagery floated back in its mind from when it fell into the Time Machine, the expression on that person's face.

Worry, anxiety, panic, and the hand reaching out to try and grab it.

On that day, it was still just a Cyclizar.

On that day, it fell into the Time Machine, returning to ancient times, affected by unknown forces, transformed into its current form.

---

---

---

---

# 5: Chapter 5: The Back Hill

As noon approached, D and Cyclizar cleared a small piece of land suitable for planting and stored the chopped wood and stones in the storage room.

"Gruu gru~"

After a busy morning, Cyclizar's stomach made rumbling noises.

Looking down, it muttered, "Grrr..."

"Hungry?" D heard the sound and laughed, "Hold on a moment, I'll go make lunch."

Cyclizar immediately perked up with joy, "Grrr~"

They washed their hands at the outdoor sink and returned to the wooden house.

Cyclizar cooled off under the eaves while D took out the ingredients and got busy in the kitchen.

Today's lunch was still hot soup noodles, but with a twist—D added some wild leeks found in the pasture to enhance the flavor.

With practice making perfect, this time it only took a few minutes to finish.

D served the noodles into bowls and looked down, noticing that the hot soup noodles had improved from a grade of C- to C, and the cold resistance duration increased by five minutes.

"As expected, ingredients do affect the dish's rating."

D placed the bowl in front of Cyclizar, who had been waiting at the table.

Cyclizar looked down at the bowl of strange noodles and instantly displayed a pitiful expression.

"Grrr..."

Impoverished D: "..."

He sighed, thinking of the little money left from the previous owner, and consoled, "Hang in there. I'll take you to town for some good food this afternoon."

"Grrr~"

Fortunately, Cyclizar wasn't bothered by D's poverty. Being a simple optimist, it got excited upon hearing D's words, looking forward to the evening's delicious offerings.

Watching Cyclizar tilt its head back to drink the noodles, D began to eat his portion with a worried expression.

"The remaining money will be spent quickly after buying food. The land was just tilled and seeds can only be planted tomorrow, taking a month to mature..."

The farmland covered in weeds was hard and unsuitable for crop growth. D had tilled it this morning and watered it, making the soil soft.

Although there was no fertilizer, fortunately, turnips didn't require high soil quality.

"Besides, I need to find other ways to make money until the turnips are harvested."

With that thought, D found himself in deep thought.

Ways to make money...

He remembered what he used to do playing farming simulator games.

One was fishing, and the other was going to the back mountain to collect mushrooms and fruits to get through the early stages of the game.

But now, without a fishing rod, his only option was to try his luck in the back mountain and see if he could find any mountain goods.

Without hesitation, after lunch, D prepared to head to the back mountain.

Getting wind of his plan, Cyclizar, who was initially standing upright, suddenly crouched down on all fours and shouted back to him, "Grrr!"

D was slightly startled, seeing Cyclizar in ride mode, he asked, "Are you suggesting I ride you?"

Cyclizar nodded its head, "Grrr!"

Curious, D climbed onto its back.

Once he was settled, Cyclizar began to run.

Its pace was neither too fast nor too slow, very steady, as it carried D towards the back mountain.

Feeling the breeze brushing past his hair, D thought, "No wonder Cyclizar became a means of transportation in the Paldea Region. This feeling isn't much different from a regular motorcycle."

After passing through a country lane, a lush forest appeared ahead.

Lush sunlight poured down from above, leaving mottled patterns on the ground through the gaps in the leaves.

In the bushes, Caterpie crawled, while Fletchling stood on branches, and in the dense thicket came rustling sounds, or perhaps ripe and juicy fruits hung heavily on branches.

Everywhere was a scene full of vitality.

D got off Cyclizar and raised his eyebrows, looking at the small Pokemon.

The Pokemon were also curiously watching this unfamiliar human who entered the forest.

"Caterpie... Evolves into Metapod at level 7, and into Butterfree at level 10. For a new Trainer, this Pokemon is quite a good choice."

"Fletchling? If I remember correctly, this seems to be the regional bird of Kalos, and its final evolution, Talonflame, is a Fire and Flying Type Pokemon, which seems really cool!"

"Huh? Could that white little mouse be a Maushold from the Paldea Region?"

D's gaze suddenly caught sight of three small figures in a patch of grass.

Tentatively, two slightly larger white mice poked their heads out, protecting a smaller white mouse behind them.

"A family of three..." D looked around but didn't find a fourth mouse.

Noticing his gaze, the three Maushold shivered, then quickly turned and disappeared into the grass, vanishing without a trace.

D: "..."

Nonetheless, one has to admit that this Pokémon, Maushold, is quite cute.

Prior to this, D didn't think of himself as someone who loved fluffy things, but seeing the cute appearance of Maushold, coupled with their fine, pure white fur, he couldn't help but be tempted.

"I wonder what it feels like to touch them."

There were many Pokémon in the forest, and after walking around with Cyclizar, D couldn't help but feel excited.

If he were a Trainer, he'd definitely take out a Poke Ball and have an epic battle with them, winning their recognition before capturing them.

Both the rapidly growing Caterpie and the promising Fletchling were perfect!

Unfortunately, from the memories of the original owner, he knew that an ordinary red-white ball cost two hundred League Coins, and he simply couldn't afford it now.

D sighed, then shifted his attention from those Pokémon to the mountain goods growing in the forest.

He walked up to a lush fruit tree and reached out to pick a fruit from it.

As he looked down, the information about the fruit appeared in his mind.

[Pecha Berry: A miraculous fruit that can cure poisoning, with a slightly sweet taste, usable for making jams, Poffins, fruitcakes, or added to yogurt to become fruit yogurt, its unique flavor is favored by many Pokémon.]

A Berry?

D picked up the Pecha Berry, bringing it to his nose for a sniff, catching a faint peach fragrance.

He held the Berry in front of Cyclizar and asked, "Want a bite?"

Cyclizar glanced at it, then opened its mouth wide and swallowed the Pecha Berry whole.

"Ga-ow~"

Judging from its expression, it seemed the taste was quite good.

"It seems that just like in the animations, Berries are also a primary food source for Pokémon."

D formed a rough idea in his heart, quietly nodding as he picked a few more Pecha Berries from the tree, planning to take them back to the ranch.

However, he only took a small portion.

Because if Berries are a food source for Pokémon, then other Pokémon in this forest would also need these Berries.

If he took all these Pecha Berries, surely many Pokémon would be left hungry.

After packing the Pecha Berries, D took Cyclizar and headed deeper into the forest.

This forest was vast, perhaps they took the wrong direction, as along the way, besides the initial Pecha Berries, D encountered no other fruit trees.

Without realizing it, time slipped into the evening, and feeling tired, D sat under a big tree to rest, glancing at the sky and thinking it was time to head back.

Just then, Cyclizar's nose twitched.

"What's up?" D turned his head to look.

"Ga-ow..."

Cyclizar turned its head, surveying its surroundings, as if trying to determine a direction.

Soon, it pinpointed a spot and looked towards it.

Following its gaze, D looked at the seemingly empty patch of ground, initially puzzled, but soon he noticed something odd.

Centered around the roots of a big tree, all the grass in a circular area had withered.

"Ga-ow!"

Cyclizar barked in that direction.

D stood up, filled with curiosity and walked forward with Cyclizar to observe the strange area.

He then used his hand to brush away the decaying fallen leaves and yellowing grass, uncovering a black, unknown object underneath.

At that moment, D also caught a whiff of the scent.

It was an incredibly unique smell, indescribable with words.

His mind was still puzzling over what this could be, but in the next second, the information about it automatically appeared in his mind.

[Black Truffle: A top-quality, gourmet ingredient, this rare and exquisite delicacy can be paired with eggs to make black truffle steamed eggs or blended with butter for creamy soup. Although it doesn't inherently provide enhancements, it can significantly improve the effects of a dish.]

---

# 6: Chapter 6: The First Earnings

Black Truffle...

Maybe it's the lack of money that made D's first thought upon seeing it: This must be expensive, right?

But soon, he noticed the enhancement effects mentioned after.

[Although it has no inherent enhancement effects, it can greatly boost the effects of cooking.]

What is this? A high-grade synthesizing material?

Regarding this, D could vaguely judge based on the settings of a game from his past life.

That game also featured ingredients of this type.

Adding it to a dish could upgrade an lv1 effect to lv3, and even turn a temporary buff into a permanent one.

With this, the value of the Black Truffle had increased another level.

A good thing indeed!

D cautiously dug out the entire black truffle whole.

Seeing his actions, Cyclizar lay down beside him, holding its breath, until the palm-sized truffle was packed into D's backpack, then it raised its head and curiously asked, "Gao?"

"This is something that can improve our quality of life!"

D smiled at Cyclizar, weighing the truffle in his hand, estimating it to be about a kilogram.

As an outsider, he didn't know the price of a black truffle, but based on its shape and size, he could sense its price wouldn't be low.

He looked up at the sky, noticing it was getting late, so D mounted Cyclizar, urging it to head towards the ranch.

At five in the afternoon, just as D returned to the ranch and was about to pour himself a glass of water, he heard a honking sound outside.

"Beep beep beep!"

He stepped outside, spotting a van with a wave logo driving in from outside.

A brawny driver with muscular arms and a buzz cut stuck his head out the window and warmly said:

"You must be the new owner of this ranch, D, right? Mayor Thomas told me about you. I'm Zack from Porto Marinada Market, responsible for collecting goods from nearby ranches. Please take good care of me in the future!"

"Hello, Mr. Zack." D greeted politely.

Zack turned off the engine, jumped out, and glanced around, seeing the farmland not far away that had been cleared. He grinned and said: "Looks like you've already gotten the hang of working on the ranch! Do you have anything to sell?"

Zack only asked casually, well aware that a new rancher wouldn't have mature crops or wool and milk to sell.

After all, the development of a ranch takes time.

But in the next moment, he saw the new rancher take out a large, black object from who knows where.

The well-informed Zack recognized the name of that item at a glance.

He widened his eyes in astonishment, "Black Truffle? Wow! Truffles of this size are extremely rare, where did you find it?!"

"The back mountain," D replied, "Can it be sold?"

Zack nodded quickly, "Of course, this thing sells for an outrageous price! Normal quality black truffles go for about five thousand league coins per pound, but the quality and size of the one you have is practically top-notch, the kind you rarely come across on the market. The price is about fifteen thousand league coins per pound."

Its unique flavor is sought after by many, especially the high society families and nobles.

They certainly have no shortage of money.

Fifteen thousand per pound?

D's heart stirred, meaning the black truffle in his hand was worth a total of thirty thousand league coins?

At this price, D couldn't help but feel tempted.

Should he sell it?

Such a rare ingredient could greatly enhance cooking for Pokemon.

But...

If he sold it, he would have enough money to buy seeds, renovate the ranch, and prepare food.

In an instant, D fell into hesitation.

But soon, he made a decision.

In the long term, although black truffles enhance the effects of cooking, turning it into cash could speed up the ranch's development, bringing in more money.

Once the ranch develops, he could use the money to buy other top-tier ingredients with similar effects.

Thinking about this, D no longer hesitated and decisively chose to sell.

Seeing this, Zack took out a scale from the vehicle to measure it, then pointed at the marked data and said to him, "One kilogram plus one hundred grams."

He then used a calculator and notebook for a brief calculation, eventually saying, "A total of thirty-three thousand league coins."

"Okay," D nodded.

"Then I'll take this black truffle with me!" Zack said, while taking out a wad of cash from his wallet and handing it over.

D reached out to take it, counting it carefully, and then heaved a small sigh of relief in his heart.

The first sum of money as a rancher was in hand! He no longer needed to eat bland hot noodle soup!

Zack still had other goods to collect from other ranches. Before leaving, he looked around the whole ranch and suggested:

"Besides planting crops and raising Pokemon, there are many ways to make money. You haven't been to Porto Marinada Town yet, have you? Be sure to buy a fishing rod at the grocery store when you go there. During the crop growth period, you can earn money through fishing."

"Ah, but there's no rush. It's tough in the beginning, but it gets much better in the summer. Besides planting ordinary crops, you can also grow hops for brewing beer, which has a rural flavor that's very popular in the big cities!"

Hops?

Hearing this familiar name, D raised an eyebrow.

However, what he thought of wasn't brewing beer, but that the beer brewed by him might end up having some odd effects, just like his cooking...

Thinking this, D nodded, saying to Zack, "I'll keep it in mind, thank you very much."

Zack laughed heartily, "Good luck!"

With that, he bid farewell to D, driving the large van away.

D returned to the wooden house, looking at the small piece of black truffle left on the table — it was accidentally broken during the excavation.

"With truffle prices so high, I wonder if raising Oinkologne could help root out truffles hidden underground..."

D pondered a little.

He planned to keep that small piece of black truffle, waiting until he made the dish he wanted, then add the black truffle to enhance the cooking's effect.

As for now...

Looking at the thick stack of league coins in his hand, D felt cheerful.

It's time to head to the town, buy new ingredients, and cook up a meal!

D mounted Cyclizar, and after giving the direction, Cyclizar ran towards the destination.

D sat on it, looking at the sunset-painted sky, softly humming: "Riding my beloved little motorcycle~"

"Gao?"

Cyclizar looked puzzled, is the beloved little motorcycle referring to it?
