# 7: Chapter 7: What It's Like to Pick Up a Lagendary Beast at the Start

Porto Marinada Town was located in the northwest of the Paldea Region, a bustling seaside town.

From the original owner's memories, D learned that the town was home to various local festivals.

For example, there was the Spring Festival, the Water Pokemon Rally, the Summer Fireworks Show, and so on.

These unique festivals attracted many foreign visitors, and coupled with the thriving livestock industry nearby, Porto Marinada Town gradually developed.

The place known as "Porto Marinada Market" was especially renowned.

D walked along the street with Cyclizar, surrounded by all kinds of Pokemon accompanying pedestrians. The sea breeze greeted them, making for a leisurely stroll with an overall pleasant and comfortable atmosphere.

During this visit, besides purchasing food, D had another important task.

He needed to visit the town's woodworking shop, general store, seed shop, and stores selling ranch Pokemon and ordinary animals.

"It was almost six o'clock now," D worried that the shops might close soon. He thought for a moment and asked Cyclizar, "Are you hungry?"

"Gao!"

Cyclizar nodded and then shook his head. Its intelligence allowed it to see that D had things to do, expressing that it could wait a little longer.

Seeing this, D smiled reassuringly, patted its head, and said, "Good child, then let's go to the general store first."

"Gao~"

The town was not large, perhaps to ensure that the visitors from afar would not get lost. The positions of all kinds of shops were clearly marked on street corners and roadside billboards.

After locating the general store on the map, D headed straight there.

After crossing a crossroads, a red-brick building appeared in front of D.

It was dusk, and there weren't many people in the shop, only a bald uncle with glasses behind the counter playing with a Fidough.

Upon hearing the wind chime from outside the door, the uncle looked up and said, "Hey? A customer has come! Welcome!"

D politely greeted him and then asked, "Do you have seasonings here?"

Uncle: "Yes! They're on the shelf in the back!"

D went there and indeed saw various seasonings on the shelf.

Oil, salt, vinegar, soy sauce, flour, spices, red bean paste, butter, sugar, pepper powder...

The prices were clearly marked below the shelves.

For example, a bag of flour cost 200 League coins, a bottle of oil 100 League coins, and the red bean paste was 150 League coins, and so on.

Looking at these, D was somewhat confused by the prices in the Pokemon World.

A small bag of flour and a Poke Ball cost the same; seeing it this way, the price of a Poke Ball didn't seem that expensive.

After thinking for a moment, D decided to buy one of each seasoning.

When he brought the items to the counter, the shopkeeper's face showed a surprised expression. While taking out a packaging bag, he asked, "Just moved to Porto Marinada Town?"

"Yes," D nodded.

The shopkeeper smiled and said, "Well then, enjoy your life here in Porto Marinada Town! Alright, it's a total of two thousand eight hundred twenty League coins."

D didn't hurry to pay but asked, "Do you have fishing rods here?"

"Fishing rods? Yes!"

Saying this, the shopkeeper turned around, took several fishing rods from the shelf behind, and placed them on the counter to introduce:

"Each fishing rod has a different price point. This one is 2000 League coins, and with bait, you can fish Pokemon and fish wherever there's water."

"This fishing rod costs 5000 League coins; it uses more advanced materials, so its performance is better than this 2000 League one, and it doesn't easily break."

"As for this one over here, it costs 15000 League coins. It's the latest advanced fishing rod, using top-notch materials in terms of both material and performance."

D: "..."

His eyes lingered on the trio of fishing rods, eventually opting for the ordinary one priced at 2000, intending to use it for the time being.

"The total is four thousand eight hundred twenty League coins. Since you're a first-time shopper at our store, I'll gift you a free box of bait. Please visit often in the future!" The shopkeeper smiled and handed over a tin can.

"Thank you, shopkeeper."

D accepted the goods and left the store.

His second stop was the woodworking shop, located at the edge of the town, with a forest behind it.

There was a stump at the door with a saw stuck in it.

D was about to knock on the door when it automatically opened.

A strong woman with a pink ponytail and a headband walked out of the house.

After expressing his intentions, the woman led him to the workshop at the back.

"You're the new rancher?" the woman asked curiously.

D was slightly taken aback, then realized that Mayor Thomas had likely spoken to her.

He nodded and said, "My name is D."

The woman: "Just call me Keri."

"Alright, Miss Keri."

As Keri walked, she introduced the process of expanding the ranch house: "You haven't named the ranch yet, have you?"

D shook his head, "Not yet, I just arrived here yesterday."

"Don't rush, I was just asking casually. Well, Mayor Thomas told me everything about your situation; you're here to learn about repairing the Pokemon House and expanding the residence, right?"

"That's right."

"Then I'll explain one by one. To repair the Pokemon House, you need 50,000 League coins and 300 pieces of wood. Once repaired, you can raise Miltank and Wooloo there. Of course, this price is a bit high for you right now."

"So, I recommend you start by building a Chicken House to raise ordinary chickens. The eggs they lay can also be sold for profit. The Chicken House requires only 20,000 League coins and 150 pieces of wood."

"Additionally, you need to build a silo on the ranch for storing feed. We'll give you a free silo with your first construction."

Hearing this, D nodded thoughtfully and mentally noted these prices.

"What about expanding the house?"

"That's much simpler," Keri said, "Building a fully functional house costs around two million. We'll help you expand the second floor, add a separate kitchen and bathroom, and renovate the basement. Usually, ranchers use that space for winemaking."

"Besides these, our store offers custom furniture services. In addition to basic tables and chairs, you can purchase wallpaper, carpets, refrigerators, storage cabinets, various lamps, and more."

Five minutes later, D walked out of the woodworking shop.

He calculated the remaining money in his mind and suddenly felt like he had many more things to do.

"Why do I feel busier than a 9-to-9 schedule?"

D sighed and imagined the day when the ranch would return to its former glory, his face showing an expression of anticipation. By then, he could relax and truly enjoy a leisurely life as a rancher.

Since the Pokemon House couldn't be built yet, he didn't need to visit the store selling ranch animals for the time being.

Riding on Cyclizar, D headed to his last destination, the seed store.

The owner of the seed store was a middle-aged man with brown hair and a slightly plump physique, wearing a brown coat.

When D arrived, the owner was having dinner with his wife and daughter.

Seeing a customer coming, the owner immediately got up, "Welcome! Let's see what kind of seeds you're interested in buying."

D wasn't sure himself; he only had one packet of turnip seeds given to him by the mayor.

With the owner's guidance, he saw a large variety of seeds on the shelves.

Potatoes, turnips, garlic, strawberries, cabbages, cucumbers, radishes, tomatoes, grass, and there were even some berry seeds like Pecha Berries and Oran Berries.

D's eyes were dazzled, and the seed store owner explained, "These are crops and fruit trees suitable for planting in spring. They are easy to grow after artificial cultivation, and as long as you water and fertilize them on time, they will grow large and healthy."

D silently nodded.

The seeds in this world were different from those in the Harvest Moon and Stardew Valley games he had played.

Here, a packet contained many seeds and could plant a large piece of land, unlike in games where one packet of seeds could only plant one square.

After pondering for a moment, D decided to buy one packet of each seed, including the fruit trees.

The prices of these seeds varied, with the basic turnips costing only 60 League coins per packet, while strawberries and cucumbers were 120 and 150 per packet.

Altogether, all the seeds cost him 1,370 League coins.

As a result, the money from selling Black Truffles was down to 27,000.

Packing up the seeds, D led Cyclizar out of the shop.

It was already dark outside.

D looked at the stars in the sky, took a deep breath, and said, "Alright, time to have dinner!"

"Gah-oo," Cyclizar seemed a bit pitiful.

D laughed and patted its head, then led it into a restaurant.

After a long day, he planned to eat out tonight.

The waiter handed over the menu, which included food specially prepared for Pokemon, aside from human dishes.

Considering Cyclizar, D figured it would probably prefer meat.

He ordered a meat dish for Cyclizar and a berry set to reward it as a 'hardworking contributor' today.

Soon, the food was served.

Seeing Cyclizar gobbling down the food, D felt happy inside.

Pokemon are so simple and cute~

He looked down at his aromatic vegetable stew and corn chowder as their details appeared before him.

Predictably, these dishes didn't possess special abilities to enhance Pokemon.

D confirmed in his mind, "It seems only the dishes I make have that kind of power."

After dinner, D went to the grocery store to buy ingredients for the next few days.

Since he still didn't have a refrigerator at home, he only bought enough for three days.

The weather wasn't too hot this season, so food stored in the house wouldn't spoil easily.

Carrying large bags, D rode Cyclizar and headed home under the night sky, Pokemon in tow.

The air was a bit chilly at night, and the wind felt cold on the skin.

Cyclizar was afraid of the cold, but D, riding on its back, felt warm, helping it fend off the chill.

At that moment, Cyclizar suddenly craved another bowl of hot soup noodles made by D.

Cyclizar's speed was fast, returning to the ranch in less than thirty minutes.

The ranch was pitch black at night, and D had anticipated this, so he made sure to bring a flashlight before leaving.

He fumbled in the dark for the flashlight, but before he could find it, he suddenly heard a "bang."

The sound was loud and was accompanied by the noise of trees breaking.

It seemed like something had fallen from the sky, crashing into the ranch and knocking over a bunch of trees.

D was startled and finally found the flashlight, quickly taking it out and shining the light toward the ranch.

Soon, he saw a strange shadow in his family field.

D was startled, and Cyclizar beside him stood up from its riding position, looking warily over there.

It sensed an unusual presence.

The flashlight beam hit that shadow; it remained motionless on the ground as if it were unconscious.

With the light, D finally saw the true form of the shadow.

A scarlet body, a thick tail, luxurious and flamboyant dragon whiskers, and the airbag on its chest resembling a motorcycle tire.

That was...

D stood there, dumbfounded.

---

---

---

---

# 8: Chapter 8: God of the Past, Koraidon

In the small cabin lit up by lights.

D and Cyclizar looked at the Pokemon they had jointly dragged in from outside, lying on the floor.

With just one look, D recognized the identity of this Pokemon.

Paradise Guardian Dragon, Wing King, God of the Past...

These are all names for this Pokemon.

It originally had no name, but after being summoned to this era, it was discovered and named Koraidon by the professor.

That's right, Koraidon.

One of the legendary cover Pokemon of the game Pokemon Scarlet and Violet, it is said that it once cracked the earth with its fists.

Currently, everything about its ecology in this world is unclear, but some believe it is what Cyclizar looked like in the past...

Although it resembles Cyclizar in appearance, Koraidon is much more tenacious and fierce.

D compared Koraidon to Cyclizar and noticed that their skeletal structures were indeed very similar.

So, here's the question.

How did Koraidon end up in this place?

D fell into deep thought.

In his mind, he pondered the information about Koraidon from the game.

Summoned by Professor Sada through the Time Machine, it escaped Zone Zero after failing to compete for territory with another Koraidon, eventually falling exhausted on a beach in the southern part of the Paldea Region, where it was found by the game's protagonist...

"So, what exactly is happening now?"

D was a bit confused. He exchanged a glance with the innocent-looking Cyclizar opposite him, and then looked down at Koraidon.

The next second, Koraidon's tightly closed eyes suddenly snapped open, and its golden pupils filled with a fierce aura, locked onto the human in front of it.

D was taken aback.

But immediately, that fierce aura disappeared, although Koraidon still stared at him.

D: "?"

"Gah-ow?" Cyclizar tilted its head.

Upon hearing the third party's growl, Koraidon's expression slightly changed.

After seeing Cyclizar clearly, an expression of disbelief appeared in Koraidon's eyes, and then it seemed to understand something.

Suddenly, many memories surfaced in its mind...

Long, long ago, when it was still a Cyclizar, it had also encountered a Koraidon one night.

Today is just like that night.

However, back then, it was in the position of the Cyclizar opposite it.

It looked at the somewhat intimidated Cyclizar and suddenly realized how the Koraidon it had encountered when it was a Cyclizar came to be.

It was itself.

It fell into the Time Machine, waited for thousands of years in a distant time-space, and was finally summoned again, only to arrive in an even earlier time...

Koraidon's gaze was fixed on Cyclizar.

Seeing the two in a standoff, D was a little afraid that this Koraidon from who-knows-where might suddenly go berserk and attack Cyclizar.

He quickly stepped in front of Cyclizar, blocking Koraidon's sight, and forced himself to speak as kindly as possible: "Haha, um... you're okay now, right? I just saw you fall from the sky and brought you back to my home."

With such an unfamiliar and aggressive Pokemon, it's better to be cautious.

Seeing D protecting Cyclizar, Koraidon's feelings grew complicated.

Feeling both frustrated and displeased, it watched this scene, not knowing why but feeling a bit sour inside.

What complicated its feelings even more was that the Cyclizar was still itself.

Its past self...

D silently observed Koraidon's behavior.

If the opponent doesn't move, I won't move.

But just then, a rumbling sound suddenly came from Koraidon's stomach.

The air was filled with an awkward atmosphere as a result.

D: "..."

He and Cyclizar had already eaten dinner, and seeing that Koraidon seemed not going berserk, he quietly breathed a sigh of relief and said to Koraidon, "You're hungry, aren't you? I still have some food here. Do you want to eat?"

Koraidon immediately nodded obediently, completely devoid of its earlier fierceness.

The moment it heard there was food, it immediately changed its demeanor... D couldn't help but grumble inwardly.

Yet, he still stood up, bringing Cyclizar to the cupboard and taking out the fresh ingredients he bought today—a piece of pork shoulder meat.

This meat is lean but not dry, fatty but not greasy. The pan-seared pork steak cooked from it is tender and juicy, very suitable for large-sized Pokemon like Koraidon.

After cleaning the meat and removing the fat and skin, he cut it into moderately thin pieces and put them in a bowl.

D then took out the seasonings he had just bought, including dark soy sauce, light soy sauce, oyster sauce, white sugar, black pepper powder, and cornstarch for thickening.

He added the seasonings to the bowl in turn, mixing them with the prepared meat slices thoroughly before placing them in the pot to slowly cook over medium-low heat.

Soon, golden yellow grease started seeping out from the meat, accompanied by the sizzling sound of the jumping grease, and an enticing aroma began to fill the room.

Inside the pot, the meat bathed in the sauce slowly turned golden, the fresh pork brimming with vitality, gently swaying in the rich aroma and grease, bursting with juicy tenderness.

During the process, D glanced back.

He saw that at an unknown time, Koraidon had transformed into a sprinting form on all fours, watching this side while drooling.

Although Cyclizar had already filled its stomach, it still couldn't help but gulp at the smell.

D chuckled silently, estimated the timing in his heart, and when it seemed about right, he took the meat out of the pot, divided it into two portions, and placed them on white plates.

Looking down, the information about this dish appeared before him.

[Pan-Seared Pork Steak (B): Ingredients, timing, seasonings, plus unique cooking methods created this popular dish, the juicy pork steak can bring incomparable vitality to Pokemon.

Cooking Effect: Large HP recovery, large strength increase, small amount of experience and insights gained by Pokemon, duration 60 minutes.

Rating: Fresh ingredients and excellent cooking skills have produced this delicious pan-seared pork steak, perfect for Pokemon seeking strength.]

"Huh?" D was a bit surprised.

Is the effect of this pan-seared pork steak that good?

It's just that the duration is a bit short.

D thought of the black truffle he found earlier and wondered if he added this enhancing ingredient, would the duration become permanent?

"I must try it when I get the chance."

He then placed the plates in front of Cyclizar and Koraidon, watching them devour the meals. Then, after a little thought, he brought out two brand-new glass cups, two Berries, and two bottles of MooMoo Milk.

After eating meat, how could you not have a cup of fresh juice to cleanse your palate?

Besides, D wanted to know if directly juicing Berries would, like cooking, result in wonderful beneficial effects.

The appearance of Berries is similar to fruit, but since there were not enough to juice, D planned to add an appropriate amount of milk to mix.

After washing his hands, D squeezed the juice from the two Berries into a cup and finally poured in milk to mix thoroughly.

This milk was purchased at the Porto Marinada Market, 500 milliliters a bottle, costing him 120 League coins.

Soon, the juice and milk were thoroughly mixed.

D looked closely, and as expected, the information about this Berry Milk appeared in his mind.

[Berry Milk (C): A juice milk made from fresh Berries mixed with nutrient-rich MooMoo Milk, perfectly integrating the fruit scent into the milk, making it a beverage very suitable for Pokemon.

Cooking Effect: Exclusive to bovine Pokemon, consumption will slightly improve the quality of the milk produced by the consumer, making it tastier and more nutritious.

Rating: Delicious Berry Milk, you deserve it!]

---

---

---

---

# 9: Chapter 9: Koraidon Uproots the Willows

Enhancing the quality of milk?

Limited to cow Pokémon?

D raised his eyebrows, surprised.

He only knew that the cooking effect would directly enhance a Pokémon's power, speed, and other boosts like cold resistance.

But he wasn't aware that cooking could also have such auxiliary enhancement effects.

This would be a great help to the farm work!

So if other dishes are made, will there be similar effects?

There are so many kinds of food, it's probably impossible to make all the dishes in this world even in fifty years.

But no matter what, at this moment, D found himself deeply interested in those unknown dishes and effects.

"There's plenty of time, I can take my time to try them out."

D thought as he turned and placed two glasses of Berry Milk in front of Koraidon and Cyclizar.

Though neither Koraidon nor Cyclizar are milk-producing Pokémon, it's still okay for them to drink it as a regular beverage.

Before being summoned by the Time Machine, Koraidon had just finished a battle with another annoying Koraidon.

It got a bit injured in that battle, and after being summoned, realizing it had returned to this timeline, it hurried home without resting.

Now, having finished D's meal, savoring the familiar taste and slowly recovering from the injuries, Koraidon felt completely relaxed.

It couldn't help but step forward to D, gleefully licking his face to express affection and longing.

But D didn't know what Koraidon was thinking at the moment.

He was initially horrified but relaxed when he remembered that in the game, Koraidon also nuzzled against the player and Penny like this.

Feeling the slobber all over his face, D speechlessly pushed Koraidon away.

Koraidon wasn't angry, just sat nearby with a dumb grin, its big tail swaying as it watched D wash his face.

After Cyclizar finished eating, D collected the dishes, washed them clean, and stretched widely.

Having been busy all day, it was time for a good sleep!

Without a bathroom on the farm, D went outside and washed up quickly with the garden hose before lazily jumping onto the bed, yawning while browsing the "Pasture Encyclopedia" on spring crop planting.

He still had a lot to do tomorrow.

Chopping down excess trees, smashing rocks in the field, tilling the soil, and planting seeds and fruit trees.

He also needed to try fishing to see if he could catch any big fish to sell to Zack in the evening to supplement the family income.

With no mature crops and the Pokemon House not yet repaired, this was his only means of earning money.

D planned tomorrow's schedule in his head while glancing at the bedside.

Cyclizar yawned, lying drowsily on the wooden floor.

Koraidon also found a spot to close its eyes and rest.

D's presence, the familiar environment, and the lingering aroma of food in the air made it feel incredibly secure.

Although the farm was far from town, out in the wilderness, with Cyclizar around, D didn't feel scared.

Not to mention the sudden addition of Koraidon.

D smiled and then lay down.

Turn off the lights and sleep!

Early the next morning, by instinct as a corporate worker, D woke up at dawn.

He glanced at the clock on the wall to find it was only 6:15.

He intended to nap a bit more, but realizing there was still so much to do today, he instantly lost any drowsiness.

Rubbing his eyes, he got up to start making breakfast.

Hearing the rustling sounds from the bed, Koraidon opened one eye, saw D getting up, and went back to sleep.

Cyclizar, however, raised its head.

Seeing it move, D patted its head while passing by, chuckling softly, "Keep sleeping, I'll go prepare breakfast and call you up later."

"Groan~"

And so, Cyclizar closed its eyes again, lying on the floor asleep.

This morning, D prepared sandwiches for Cyclizar.

Using toast bought from Porto Marinada Market, self-fried meat patties, and freshly sliced tomatoes and lettuce all mixed together, finally spread with a thick layer of cheese sauce, a fresh sandwich was ready.

D looked down and the sandwich information immediately appeared.

[Sandwich (D): A sandwich blending various fresh ingredients, with a balanced mix of meat and vegetables, rich in nutrition, perfect for breakfast.

Cooking Effect: Energy boost (small), makes Pokémon more energetic and less prone to drowsiness.

Rating: A basic fast-food sandwich, a very nice breakfast choice.]

"Is it just an energy boost?" D was a bit disappointed but figured that with only the patty made by him, it was already pretty good.

After finishing breakfast preparations, D awakened the two Pokémon. He wanted to make himself a sandwich too, but upon seeing the remaining pack of instant noodles in the cupboard, he decided not to waste it and cooked it for himself.

However, when he served the noodles, sharp-eyed Koraidon noticed it.

It looked down at the sandwich in front of it, then at the bowl of hot noodle soup, suddenly stood upright, swapped the food, and gulped down the hot noodle soup.

D: "..."

He felt a bit surprised, looked down at the empty bowl, and asked, "You really like this?"

For Koraidon, this was the first meal D made for it, which was quite significant!

In the near future, as the farm grew wealthier, D would hardly cook hot noodle soup anymore.

So, seeing it again, Koraidon felt nostalgic.

Koraidon: "Groan growl hiss~"

D couldn't understand, but judging from Koraidon's expression, it seemed to really like eating it, right?

This was in stark contrast to Cyclizar's preference.

D didn't think much about it, and after washing the bowls, grabbed a hoe and headed outside.

A new workday was about to start!

After the practice yesterday morning, D and Cyclizar's teamwork had become very smooth.

Cyclizar used its tail to smash stones, clearing them out, while D tilled the soil with the hoe behind.

Occasionally, they would encounter some big trees, but D's strength wasn't enough, so he'd hand an axe to Cyclizar to help cut them down.

Koraidon watched from afar.

Suddenly, it let out a roar, then ran to a big tree, wrapped its arms around it, and with a burst of strength, uprooted it and tossed it aside.

Hearing the commotion, D and Cyclizar turned their heads, their minds filled with questions.

???

Koraidon didn't stop, continuing to help in the field with logging and stone moving.

D was stunned: "Such powerful strength! Is it repaying a favor?"

Indeed, having such a powerful Pokémon like Koraidon helping certainly lightened his workload.

Seeing Koraidon not stopping, D and Cyclizar continued working busily.

With Koraidon's addition, their work efficiency greatly improved.

In just one morning, they cleared out more than half of the farm area.

D, sweating profusely, carried a watering can to water the plots, softening the soil to make it more suitable for planting.

"Having a Water Type Pokémon right now would be great," D couldn't help but think.

After watering, the soil needed to cool for a while before planting.

D didn't rush to finish preparing the entire farm area.

Firstly, there weren't enough seeds, and secondly, they were short-handed and couldn't keep up.

This cleared half would keep them busy for a long time.

# 10: Chapter 10: The School of Fish

At noon, the food D prepared was cold cucumber slices.

The effect of this dish is just perfect for beating the heat, making the body feel cool after eating it.

Koraidon and Cyclizar were still eating sandwiches.

It wasn't that D didn't want to try a new recipe, but because he was so busy in the morning, he was too tired.

So this afternoon, D decided to take a good rest and try some fishing by the river.

Worried that Cyclizar might get bored after lunch, D looked at the glutinous rice flour and red bean paste in the cabinet and thought—maybe some snacks while fishing?

Once he thought of it, he started to do it.

D immediately took out the glutinous rice flour, added water to knead it into dough, rolled it into long strips and flattened them, then wrapped the prepared red bean paste and sugar into them, shaping them into balls.

Soon, the white dumplings appeared one by one on the stove.

Cyclizar was attracted by these novel little things and curiously came over to observe: "Gaaow?"

D glanced at it, smiled, and said, "This is a snack called glutinous rice dumpling, I wonder if you've ever had it before."

Cyclizar shook its head: "Gaaow..."

D: "This afternoon you can give it a try, just not sure if you'll like the taste."

Cyclizar nodded enthusiastically: "Gaaow!"

D couldn't help but laugh: "Silly, you say you like it before you even taste it, aren't you worried it might taste like hot soup noodles?"

"Gaaow…"

Cyclizar scratched its head in embarrassment with its paw.

On the other side, Koraidon couldn't even bear to look straight.

Was it this silly before?

Could that Koraidon possibly have looked at it the same way?

At this moment, D turned his head to look at Koraidon.

He called out: "Koraidon."

Koraidon immediately ran over and snuggled up to D on the other side.

"Gaaow-aaow-ahh?"

D pondered for a moment, then asked: "Did you come here from the Paldea Giant Pit?"

After thinking about it, he decided to ask about Koraidon's origins; he wanted to know if Koraidon was like in the game, escaping from Zone Zero into the Paldea region after losing a territorial fight with another Koraidon.

Upon hearing this, Koraidon nodded.

Seeing this, D said to himself in his heart, as expected.

He asked again: "Were you fighting with another Koraidon before?"

Koraidon was stunned: "Gaaow-aaow-ahh?" How did you know?

D thought, little one, is there anything I don't know?

But not wanting to hurt Koraidon's feelings, he didn't ask about the outcome of the territorial fight, only asked: "Do you have any plans for the future?"

Koraidon: "Gaou-aahh!"

Though he couldn't understand, D still saw what Koraidon meant.

It wanted to stay here.

This was exactly what D hoped for, as Koraidon's strength would certainly make it a great help here.

D felt excited but calmly said: "You can stay here, but you'll have to help me manage the ranch's affairs."

"Gaaow!"

Before D could finish, Koraidon nodded repeatedly in agreement.

Just then, after wrapping the last glutinous rice dumpling, D put them in the steamer to steam them slowly over low heat.

Estimating the steaming time in his heart, D took a small stool, a fishing rod, and bait, and headed to the river surrounding the ranch.

After putting on the bait and before casting the line, D said to Koraidon and Cyclizar: "I'm going fishing, you guys go play!"

In the springtime, the ranch was full of dandelions and a kind of small yellow wildflower.

Hearing D's words, Cyclizar went off to play by itself.

It got attracted by a butterfly and chased it through the grass.

After running for a while, Cyclizar stopped in front of a fluffy, soft white dandelion and bent its head down, curiously observing the tiny hairs on the dandelion.

Suddenly, Cyclizar felt an itch in its nose.

It couldn't help but let out a huge sneeze, and immediately, the dandelion seeds were blown into the sky by the gust of air.

Cyclizar looked up, with innocent and curious eyes, watching this scene.

By the river.

D looked at Koraidon beside him and asked: "Aren't you going to play?"

"Gaaow-aaow."

Koraidon shook its head, watching the gently flowing river and the shadows of fish under the water, it looked at the fishing rod in D's hand.

A light bulb suddenly lit up in Koraidon's head; it came up with a brilliant idea.

Before D could react, he saw Koraidon's energy suddenly surge, it jumped high, and as it transformed, it ferociously plunged into the water.

"Splash!"

A large amount of river water was thrown into the air, turning into droplets that pattered down, almost like a rain shower.

D: "???"

He stood there dumbfounded, staring at the scene.

Koraidon jumped out of the water, happily seeking praise: "Gaaow-aaow-ahh!"

D looked down and saw that as the water flow gradually calmed, shadows floated up to the surface, their eyes wide open, clearly stunned by Koraidon's attack.

For a moment, D didn't know what to say.

He looked at Koraidon and couldn't help but say: "Although I appreciate your effort to repay my kindness, it wasn't necessary to go to such lengths."

Koraidon instantly lowered its head in disappointment: "Gaaow-aaow..."

D sighed: "Alright, I didn't mean to scold you. You did great!"

Koraidon's eyes immediately lit up: "Gaaow-gaaah~"

D: "…"

He sighed again and directed Koraidon to pick up the stunned fish and put them into a small bucket filled with river water.

Koraidon obediently did as told, quickly plucking the fish out of the water.

The fish this season weren't very plump, but for D, who was just starting, it wasn't that important.

He picked out a few Magikarp mixed in with the regular river fish.

Koraidon looked at those three red fish, a bit puzzled: "Gaaow?"

"They can't be eaten," D shook his head.

He intended to release the Magikarp back into the river but suddenly thought about the lake at his ranch.

Farming Miltank takes a lot of preparation, so why not raise some fish in the meantime?

Not just Magikarp; the regular river fish could be raised as well.

After thinking for a moment, D carried the metal bucket to the lakeside and released the three Magikarp into it.

After releasing them, he didn't leave immediately but stayed to observe the state of the Magikarp.

Because he wasn't sure if they were merely stunned by Koraidon or if they had been directly killed…

Fortunately, these three Magikarp were quite fortunate and didn't die; after a few minutes of being unconscious, they slowly opened their eyes.

In this new body of water, they didn't seem frightened, continuing to float around dully in the water.

---

---

---

---

# 11: Chapter 11: Caterpie Exclusive Enhancement

The ranch has a new facility, fish pond +1!

Looking at the Magikarp swimming in the lake, D felt a sense of harvest joy in his heart.

The fishing plan was disrupted by Koraidon. D glanced up at the sky, estimated the time in his heart, and decided to return to the cabin to check if the glutinous rice balls were steamed.

He picked up the iron bucket full of fish, just turned around, and saw Cyclizar running over, clutching a small yellow wildflower in its paw.

"For me?" D asked in surprise.

"Gao~" Cyclizar nodded its head, showing an adorable and goofy smile on its face.

This little guy is just too cute, right? D reached out to take the wildflower, looked at its petals, and thought: Maybe this is why so many people like Pokemon?

They are simple, innocent, and kind. Most Pokemon will accompany you for a lifetime as long as you treat them well, even willing to protect you with their life.

Feeling deeply moved, D simultaneously reached out to touch Cyclizar's head, then turned around to call Koraidon back.

But the Koraidon, which was here watching the fish just a moment ago, had already disappeared.

Looking up, D saw Koraidon swiftly running through a vibrant clump of grass, where butterflies were startled and hurriedly fled into the distance.

About a few minutes later, Koraidon returned.

Its hand clutched a handful of colorful flowers, including Blue Roses, Tulips, and Hyacinths.

"..."

D immediately guessed Koraidon's thoughts, but honestly speaking, Koraidon should be considered the least elegant Legendary Beast, right?

During these two days of interaction, none of its speech or behavior resembles that of a Legendary Beast; instead, it acts like an innocently naive child.

Koraidon handed the flowers over: "Gao ooh hiss!"

D sighed inwardly and reluctantly reached out to take them.

He was just about to pick up the iron bucket when Koraidon nudged its head over again.

D: "..."

Indeed, just like a naive little kid.

He chuckled wryly and patted Koraidon's head.

Koraidon was satisfied.

"Let's go!" D picked up the iron bucket, and Cyclizar and Koraidon followed him side by side back to the cabin.

Placing the iron bucket in the shade ensures that the fish are not sunburned to death and waits until Zack comes to collect the goods at five in the afternoon.

Entering the house, he checked the glutinous rice balls were not ready yet, so D sat at the desk, took out the account book and pen bought earlier from the grocery store.

Recording daily expenditures and income in detail is something a qualified rancher must do.

This way, when the ranch gets bigger, you won't be completely in the dark.

Picking up the pen, D calculated and wrote on the ledger.

Spring 198, January 5.

Income: Black Truffle +33000

Expenditure: Seeds, Fishing Rod, Seasonings, Ingredients -4190

Remaining Funds: 28810

Spring 198, January 6.

Income: Fish +

Expenditure: 0

Since the fish hasn't been sold yet, D didn't know its price, so he left space to finalize the calculation at night.

Besides this, D also jotted down the funds needed to expand the house, repair the Pokemon House, and Chicken House.

This makes it convenient for him to refer while also providing motivation to strive for these goals.

After finishing the accounting, D stood up to check the steamer.

This time, those glutinous rice balls were finally done.

In the swirling steam, they looked like plump, white babies.

As D looked down, their information emerged.

[Glutinous Rice Ball (B): A spring delicacy made from glutinous rice and red bean paste, very popular among young people.

Cooking Effect: Bug-type Pokemon specific, Bug Silk Enhancement lv2, moderately increases the toughness and strength of Bug Silk. If ingredients with attributes are added, maybe the Pokemon eating them will undergo more changes...

Evaluation: Soft and chewy, sweet and delicate, fragrant and crispy, with a touch of red bean sweetness. It's a pretty good dessert.]

Hmm?

Enhancement specific to Bug-type Pokemon?

Staring at the words above, D suddenly became interested.

Each of those plump glutinous rice balls lying in the pot had the same enhancement effect, but with grade differences.

There were tier 1 enhancements, tier 2 enhancements, and the highest was tier 3 enhancements.

However, what sparked D's interest wasn't the Bug-type exclusive description, but rather the phrase [ingredients with attributes].

If he remembered correctly, Pokemon World doesn't seem to have such ingredients.

Yet in that trashy game, they do exist.

For instance, Ice Berry containing Ice-type power, Electric Lemon possessing an Electric characteristic, etc.

But, is it possible to cultivate them in this world?

D instinctively thought it impossible but suddenly recalled a cooking effect in the game.

It was a recipe called "Green Grass Cake," which, when fed to player-grown characters, allowed them to help tend farm crops.

Crops cared for by the character would undergo slight mutations.

For example, ordinary mushrooms would become Armor Mushrooms, regular lemons would turn into Electric Lemons, normal apples would become glowing Apples.

Giving the apple to the character causes it to glow like a big lamp, equivalent to a free transformation.

So the question arises, if he successfully made "Green Grass Cake" and fed it to Pokemon, could it also give Pokemon that kind of magical ability?

After pondering for a moment, D concluded it was feasible.

But the key is that he, a tech personnel, doesn't know the required ingredients and detailed recipe for the Green Grass Cake.

How do you make a cake out of grass?

This matter probably only Sheep Village residents, like Pleasant Goat, know…

When the time comes, adding Electric Lemon to glutinous rice balls might possibly create Bug Silk with added Electric-type enhancement.

Caterpie casting Electroweb is no longer a dream.

With these thoughts, D couldn't help feeling excited.

"Looks like I'll need to try making the Green Grass Cake soon."

But that's not possible at the moment; the ranch is still in the early stage, every penny cannot be wasted.

Keeping this matter in mind, D placed the glutinous rice balls onto a clean plate to cool.

Cyclizar curiously stared at the glutinous rice balls on the plate, whereas Koraidon's interest seemed less intense compared to it.

Noticing Cyclizar's gaze, D advised: "Right now, it's hot; wait until it cools before eating."

Cyclizar obediently listened; D told it not to eat, and it stood still as he commanded.

Leaving most for Koraidon and Cyclizar, D reserved a glutinous rice ball with lv3 Bug Silk Enhancement.

He remembered Caterpie on the back mountain.

He was eager to discover what kind of changes a small glutinous rice ball would bring to Caterpie, how much it could enhance Bug Silk.

---

---

# 12: Chapter 12: Bug Silk Enhancement Effects

In the afternoon, D rode on Koraidon straight towards the back mountain.

As for why he was riding Koraidon and not Cyclizar…

It was because when D mentioned going to the back mountain, Koraidon volunteered to take him there.

Cyclizar was left by D at the ranch to guard the house, preventing small Pokémon like Psyduck, Buizel, and Bibarel from stealing fish.

Riding on Koraidon felt completely different from riding on Cyclizar.

Cyclizar felt like a small scooter, while Koraidon, being much larger, was more like a large motorcycle.

However, speaking of motorcycles, D felt that Miraidon, from the future, was more akin to one.

Since Koraidon had emerged from the Paldea Giant Pit, this should be the world of Scarlet, right?

Arven might have already lost his mother, Professor Sada...

D pondered aimlessly in his heart.

Koraidon's speed was fast, and in the blink of an eye, they entered the back mountain area.

This time, their goal was not to find mountain goods to sell, but to find a Caterpie and test the effects of a recipe on it.

The afternoon forest was lush and verdant, with the distant sounds of Bird-type Pokémon echoing through the air.

D patted Koraidon's shoulder, signaling it to slow down, and then carefully observed the surroundings, trying to find signs of a Caterpie amidst the dense canopy and deep undergrowth.

It is well known that among all Pokémon, Caterpie is notoriously weak.

Although Magikarp and Wishiwashi are even weaker than it, Caterpie does not seem significantly stronger than them.

Before evolving, it can only use Tackle, Silk Thread, and Bug Bite moves.

After evolving into a Metapod, it only learns Hardening, with no other offensive means.

Only upon evolving into a Butterfree does Caterpie truly undergo a transformation and metamorphosis.

In nature, such a weak Caterpie often becomes prey for other Pokémon.

Pidgeotto is one of Caterpie's most famous predators.

Therefore, in the forest, to avoid being discovered by predators, Caterpie usually hides in thick bushy areas, using the surroundings to conceal itself.

At this moment, a rustling sound came from the nearby bushes.

D got off Koraidon and walked forward quietly.

The Pokémon hiding in the bushes, upon hearing footsteps outside, immediately stopped moving, and the rustling sound disappeared.

D parted the bushes and, sure enough, saw a tense and frightened Caterpie inside.

It's you!

Seeing the Caterpie's form, D's eyes lit up, but he quickly noticed that this Caterpie seemed to be injured?

Upon careful observation, D saw several claw marks on its body, likely caused by a Bird-type Pokémon.

Caterpie, at this moment, also noticed him and Koraidon, appearing somewhat scared.

D raised an eyebrow, wondering if this Caterpie was lucky, as he happened to have two Oran Berries for recovery on him.

Taking out the two Oran Berries from his backpack, D extended his hand towards Caterpie.

However, this Caterpie seemed agitated, and upon seeing the approaching hand, it instinctively opened its mouth and spat out a bundle of white Bug Silk.

The silk wrapped around D's hand, but its quality was not impressive; D pulled forcefully, breaking the silk easily.

"Too weak." D shook his head, opened his palm to reveal the Berries, and explained, "You understand what I'm saying, right? I have no intention of hurting you. Eat them, and you'll get better."

Hearing this, Caterpie was slightly dazed, looking at the human in front with big, dark eyes.

"Here!" D placed the Berry on the grass in front of Caterpie.

This time, Caterpie did not attack him; it lowered its head and began gnawing on the Oran Berry on the ground.

Once both Berries were eaten to the core, the injuries on Caterpie's body were mostly healed.

Witnessing this, D asked, "Want to become stronger?"

His tone was filled with temptation.

Caterpie looked puzzled.

"You are too weak right now. The silk you pride yourself on can't withstand other Pokémon's attacks. Do you often get attacked and chased as food by other Pokémon?"

Caterpie nodded blankly: "Zzz woo..."

D continued: "Then are you content with your current life? The world thinks you are weak. Have you imagined one day defeating those who see you as food with your own strength, dominating the forest, and making all Pokémon not dare to underestimate you?"

Is it really... possible?

Caterpie was completely stunned.

Is it content?

No, it isn't!

The world perceives it as the weakest Pokémon, even newly hatched Pidgey can easily prey on it.

Caterpie imagined itself stepping on Pidgeotto, intimidating all Bird-type Pokémon.

A wave of unprecedented passion surged in its heart.

Successfully convinced!

D didn't say much; he took out a pre-prepared mochi and put it in front of Caterpie, saying:

"Eat it, and you will become stronger."

Caterpie looked at the white fluffy thing, then immediately approached it, opened its mouth, and started eating.

After consuming the whole mochi, Caterpie couldn't help but burp.

On the surface, its body didn't seem to change at all.

Thinking for a moment, D asked it: "Try using Silk Thread again?"

"Zzz woo!"

Caterpie opened its mouth, and a slightly viscous white thread gushed out.

Compared to the previous Silk Thread, this time the amount was significantly more.

Even Caterpie itself was stunned.

D picked up the silk, wrapped it around his hand, and tried to break free. Although he stretched the silk into a curve, it didn't snap as it did before.

"Indeed, the ductility and viscosity have greatly improved; the strength is clearly on a different level."

D thought inwardly, but outwardly he said to Caterpie, "Congratulations, you've become stronger."

Since he tested the mochi's effects, there was no need to stay here any longer.

D mounted Koraidon and turned to leave.

Hearing those words, Caterpie seemed very excited.

But it quickly calmed down.

This level of strength isn't enough; it wants to become even stronger so that no one dares to underestimate it!

And the human who can make it stronger...

Watching the human and the powerful Pokémon's receding figures, Caterpie's heart was filled with unspoken thoughts.

# 13: Chapter 13: Farming, Still Farming

At five in the evening, Zack arrived at the ranch right on time with his truck.

D was in the process of planting the last Berry seedling into the ground with Koraidon and Cyclizar. He had just finished watering it when he heard Zack's hearty voice coming from behind.

"Did you do all this? So fast? It's been less than a day?!" Zack was somewhat stunned as he looked at the newly transformed ranch.

The fields, once overtaken by weeds and trees, were now clean and tidy. Each plot of land was square and orderly, with the soil slightly damp, as though it had just been watered.

D turned around and smiled at Zack, saying, "It's all thanks to them."

As he spoke, he stepped aside to reveal Koraidon and Cyclizar beside him.

Zack was slightly taken aback, then looked up to see, in addition to the Cyclizar he saw yesterday, another Pokemon had appeared by D's side at some point.

A Pokemon he had never seen before.

It had a tall physique, a strong build, muscular limbs and body, and a robust, powerful tail...

"Who is that big guy?!" Zack widened his eyes again.

He swore, he had never seen such a Pokemon in the Paldea Region!

As a truck driver who traveled far and wide, Zack had visited various places in his early years and had seen countless Pokemon.

He was quite knowledgeable about the Pokemon inhabiting the Paldea Region.

Although not a Trainer himself, if he encountered a Trainer on a journey from the Orange Academy, he couldn't resist having an exciting Pokemon battle with them.

But… he had never heard of such a Pokemon.

D was not surprised by Zack's reaction, as currently, besides Professor Sada, no one had seen Koraidon.

After some thought, he didn't reveal Koraidon's name, but honestly said, "I don't know where it came from either. It appeared injured at the ranch last night, and Cyclizar and I saved it. To thank us, it stayed to help with the work."

"I see…" Zack mumbled, nodding, looking at the unfamiliar Pokemon, he added, "It seems to like you a lot! If you can meet it, it means you are destined."

Having his doubts answered, Zack didn't say much more and turned to look at the delivery box, asking, "Did you find any mountain goods like black truffles today?"

"No, but I did catch some fish."

As he spoke, D went under the eaves, picked up a bucket full of fish, and placed it in front of Zack.

Zack squatted down and looked into the bucket. Seeing the scene inside, he exclaimed in surprise, "Wow? So many?!"

For a novice rancher, this kid's fishing skills were way too impressive?

"Let me see…" Zack released his own Pokemon, a Conkeldurr, and directed it to move the glass fish tank specially used for holding fish down from the truck.

"Bass, carp, catfish, herring… Wow! And two rainbow trout! These are rare and difficult to catch! Your fishing skills are amazing!"

Zack was full of admiration. He stood up and gave a thumbs up, "Awesome!"

In response, D gave a slightly embarrassed yet polite smile.

Zack took out a ledger and started calculating the accounts.

"Bass is 300 LL per fish, carp 120… rainbow trout is 1200, hmm, altogether it comes to 3680 LL."

After finishing his calculations, Zack put away the ledger, took out his wallet, and began settling the account, "Not bad at all. I hope there will be such a haul tomorrow as well!"

Indeed, in any world, fishing is a primary source of income at the beginning… D thought to himself as he took the money.

Upon completing the transaction, Zack turned to leave, as he needed to collect goods from other ranches and farms and then deliver them to Porto Marinada Market and other nearby cities overnight.

Suddenly, Zack remembered something.

He stopped, turned back, and asked, "By the way, have you thought of a name for this ranch yet?"

A name?

D paused.

Zack grinned and said, "A good name can also become a brand for the ranch, letting more people know about it! When the harvest festival is held in town, nearby ranches can compete to see whose crops are of the best quality, and the winning ranch receives a mysterious grand prize sponsored by the town!"

D understood.

To be honest, he hadn't yet decided on a name for the ranch.

However, he was always decisive when it came to such matters.

So, after thinking for just a few seconds, D came up with a name: "Let's call it D Ranch!"

Simple, memorable, and not too flashy.

"Using your name, huh? Nice! Then D Ranch it is!" Zack said with a smile, "I really look forward to the day this name is known all over Paldea!"

D: "..."

Zack left.

D watched him go, then turned around to face the newly revitalized plot fields before him.

After returning from the back hills, he saw the soil had more or less recovered, so he planted the purchased crop seeds with Koraidon and Cyclizar.

There were many kinds of seeds, and D disliked chaos, so he specifically divided the fields into different sections based on the seed types before planting.

Potatoes, turnips, garlic, spring wheat, strawberries, cabbage, cucumbers, radishes, tomatoes.

Besides that, he also bought some Berry seedlings earlier.

Like Oran Berry, Pecha Berry, Cheri Berry, and Nomel Berry.

These Berry seedlings were allocated a separate plot of land, which D intended to develop into a dedicated Berry Garden.

Ordinary Berry prices in this world are very cheap, with Oran Berries selling for only 20 LL each.

So D wasn't planning on making money from them but instead intended to use them to make fruit cakes, fruit yogurts, and fruit pies for Cyclizar and Koraidon to enjoy.

After busying himself for a few hours, D felt completely exhausted.

But looking at the orderly fields in front of him, he felt a peculiar sense of satisfaction.

"Hmm, there are quite a few crop seeds planted the first time."

Worried about making mistakes, D thought for a moment and decided to make some wooden signs to stick into the soil, labeling each section by crop type for easy identification.

He had seen a saw in the storage room behind the house that could be used for cutting wood.

But it was getting late, so D planned to do it tomorrow instead.

Stretching, D relaxed, removed his mud-stained rubber boots, washed his face at the faucet, and changed into lightweight sandals.

Just as he turned his head, he saw Koraidon and Cyclizar, covered in dirt, with muddy feet and tails.

"..."

D sighed helplessly and said to them, "Stand still, I'll hose you down!"

The two Pokemon immediately stood still, obediently.

D picked up the hose and began washing them down, ensuring all the mud was rinsed off. Both large creatures shook off the water droplets in unison afterward.

D got splashed, suddenly feeling that they resembled two large dogs.

With that odd thought, he returned to the house, grabbed towels, helped dry Koraidon and Cyclizar, then ushered them inside.

The wooden floor of the cabin, which D scrubbed meticulously clean, had not a speck of dust, and to avoid dirtying it, he would change into different shoes when leaving or entering.

By now, night had fallen deeply.

Koraidon and Cyclizar lay lazily on the floor.

Cyclizar couldn't stop yawning.

Koraidon looked at its former self with some disdain.

Under the warm-toned lights in the house, D, in his cozy slippers, stood at the stove preparing their dinner.

Hmm, what should we eat tonight?

Originally, D wanted to cook something new, but he was worn out from the day's work, so he decided to take it easy in the end.

He simply pan-fried some meat patties, and with pre-sliced bread and some lettuce and ham, made a well-balanced sandwich.

"Enjoy!"

D placed the sandwiches in front of the two Pokemon, then grabbed his share and sat at the desk, updating the rest of the entries in the day's ledger.

Spring of Year 198, January 6th.

Income: Fish +3680

Expenditure: 0

Remaining Funds: 32490

After completing the records and finishing dinner, D contentedly flopped onto the bed.

Sleep!

# 14: Chapter 14: A New Member on the Ranch +1

The next morning, D opened his eyes and heard the pattering sound of rain outside.

Looking out through the window, the world outside appeared gray and the sky was overcast.

It's raining.

The spring rain softly draped over the newly planted crops.

"At least it saves me the trouble of watering them," D thought.

On rainy days, D didn't want to get his feet muddy, so he decided not to go out for activities like fishing or gathering mountain produce.

Instead, he stayed at home rearranging his old house, moving furniture around to suit his preferences.

He moved the desk under the window, so that in the future, when reading, he would have a broader view and wouldn't feel as constrained as before.

The bed's position remained unchanged, as the corner wall gave him a sense of security.

As for the cabinet...

D circled it once, finally deciding to move it to the other side, using it as a divider between the resting area and the kitchen.

However, as D directed Koraidon to move the cabinet, he was surprised to discover a dusty fireplace behind it.

The inside of the fireplace was pitch black, with some scorch marks still remaining.

D leaned in to take a closer look and found that the fireplace seemed to be in working condition.

Since he had nothing else to do, he began cleaning out the old soot and ashes from the fireplace.

Soon, the fireplace was cleaned thoroughly, and looking at it, D couldn't help but think, "This is quite a pleasant surprise!"

This way, when winter comes, he could light up the fireplace to heat up the house, without worrying about shivering in the cold.

As someone living in the modern world as a salaried worker, it was his first time encountering something like a fireplace.

Speaking of fireplaces, D remembered the fairy tales he read as a child in storybooks.

Like the warm scene of Mama Bear knitting sweaters for Baby Bear in front of a fireplace, with Baby Bear playing with a ball of yarn nearby.

Honestly, such a life is quite enviable.

Of course, it would be even better with a few more Pokémon.

Like the adorable Eevee, the warm Vulpix, the cute Pikachu, and the lovely Skitty.

"This is true living!"

D couldn't help but remark.

If he hadn't crossed over, he might be rushing to catch the subway to work in the heavy rain by now.

However, what's past is past, and moreover, there's nothing to miss there.

Seizing the present is most important.

D shook his head. It was a bit stuffy inside, so he opened the door to let the breeze in.

Sitting at the desk with the cool wind blowing, D began to flip through the "Pasture Encyclopedia," checking information about spring crops.

Including how to fertilize, growth cycles, and potential harvest times, etc.

Cyclizar and Koraidon didn't disturb his reading, quietly lying by the door listening to the rain.

And at that moment, Koraidon seemed to sense something unusual.

It suddenly straightened up, taking a couple of steps forward.

"Kaa-seeh?"

Hearing the sound, D turned his head and saw Koraidon peeking its head out the door, looking to the side.

"What's the matter?"

"Kaa-seeh."

Koraidon tilted its head, looking at that familiar Caterpie.

Seeing its actions, D also got up to take a look.

Looking at the Caterpie curled up under the eaves, drenched by the rain, D showed a surprised expression.

"Caterpie?"

As if hearing his voice, the Caterpie weakly opened its eyes, looking over.

"Si-ooo..."

Witnessing this, a bold idea immediately emerged in D's mind.

Could this Caterpie be the one he used to test the effects of the rice dumplings yesterday?

Why did it follow him here?

Surprised, D observed the state of this Caterpie and noticed it seemed very weak, so he brought it inside, helping it dry off and wrapping it in an old blanket.

"Si-ooo..."

Seeing the human's actions, the Caterpie's eyes were full of gratitude.

It had followed since yesterday.

But due to its slow speed and encountering some Pokémon attacks on the way, it didn't arrive until after dark.

The strength of those Pokémon was formidable, and previously, Caterpie could only flee from them. But after devouring that magical fruit, it had gained the ability to fight.

Even if it couldn't defeat them, its resilient Bug Silk could entrap them.

Realizing its newfound power made the Caterpie even more grateful to the human who helped it.

It wanted to become stronger, so other Pokémon wouldn't dare look down on it, and that's why it came.

However, it worried that it might disturb the other party, so it stayed outside the door.

D brought a plate of warming hot juice and placed it in front of the Caterpie.

Watching it sip the juice, D pondered whether the Caterpie got overexcited and wanted to follow him like Ash's Heracross in the anime.

After thinking for a while, D tentatively asked, "Do you want to choose me to be your Trainer?"

The Caterpie stopped drinking, feeling its body warming up, and nodded repeatedly: "Si-ooo!"

"But I'm not a Trainer!" D shrugged, "As you can see, I'm just an ordinary rancher and don't know how to train Pokémon."

Regarding Pokémon training, he only had plenty of theoretical knowledge from watching anime, and no practical experience.

"Si-ooo..."

The Caterpie looked a bit disappointed.

D scratched his head, realizing that in the Pokémon World, even a bug is so smart.

To become stronger, this Caterpie proactively followed him and learned from the legendary Yang Shi Cheng Men Lixue...

If he didn't show some gratitude, it would be unreasonable.

D pondered deeply.

Caterpie was a rather weak Pokémon, but its evolved form, Butterfree, was quite good, capable of spreading pollen across the crops on the ranch, aiding their growth.

Thinking of this, D added, "I'm not a Trainer, so I can't train you like a Trainer would, but I might be able to find other ways to help you become stronger. Even so, do you still want to follow me?"

With so many kinds of cuisine, who knows, one day he might create a dish that enhances the abilities of Butterfree or Caterpie.

Though the probability wasn't one hundred percent, so D used the term "might."

If the Caterpie was willing, he would let it stay on the ranch to help with the work.

The Caterpie understood the meaning of these words, looked down at the blanket wrapped around it, and then at the juice in front of it, which warmed its body.

This human is so kind!

Not only did he rescue it in the wild, but he also gave it the fruit that made it stronger, and now provided it a blanket and food.

At that moment, Caterpie no longer chose this human solely to become stronger.

——It liked this human.

Thinking of this, the Caterpie nodded.

"Si-ooo~"

ps: Begging for monthly votes, recommendation votes, and continued reading support!

# 15: Chapter 15: Fish: Suffered Real Hard

This spring rain continued until the evening before it finally stopped.

D glanced at the old-fashioned wall clock; it was still over twenty minutes until five o'clock.

After some thought, he decided to take Koraidon out for a bit to see if he could catch some more fish to sell to Zack for household expenses.

The air after the rain was filled with moisture, giving an incredibly damp feeling.

Transparent droplets slowly slid down the veins of grass blades, dropping into the moist and soft soil.

D stood under the eaves, put on rain boots, left Caterpie and Cyclizar to guard the house, and took Koraidon to the riverbank.

The reason for this was because Pokemon couldn't wear shoes, and going out would undoubtedly cover their bodies in muddy dirt. If he took them, he would have to bathe them after returning.

D found it troublesome, so he only took Koraidon along.

The left-behind Cyclizar stood under the eaves, looking this way with longing eyes.

Koraidon glanced back, feeling extremely happy that D only took it out.

Even though that Cyclizar was actually itself.

D had no idea what was on Koraidon's mind, and when they reached the river, he smiled at Koraidon, "It's up to you, Koraidon!"

On rainy days, the air pressure is low, and the oxygen levels in the river decrease, causing fish to come to the shallow waters to breathe. It's often the best time for fishing.

Hearing D's words, Koraidon instantly understood. It confidently patted its chest with its claws, saying, "Ghaa!"

Leave it to me!

With that, Koraidon's expression suddenly became serious. Its aura surged as its strong, crimson body leapt up, with the whiskers on its head turning into wing-like structures, morphing as it savagely struck down.

With a loud "boom," the water surface was blasted open.

The river water was jolted upwards, turning into droplets that splattered down.

D: "..."

Was that move just now Koraidon's exclusive move, Collision Course?

D observed Koraidon's actions and, seeing it revert to battle form and return to the shore, confirmed his guess.

Indeed, it was Collision Course.

In the game, Koraidon's exclusive move was a super powerful move with a power of 100 and accuracy of 100, capable of further increasing its power when hitting a weak spot.

Using Collision Course to fish seemed quite extravagant.

D looked up at the river surface, where fish of various sizes were floating.

These poor fish...

However, Koraidon seemed to have deliberately controlled its strength. Although it looked exaggerated, it didn't actually harm the fish, only stunned them with the residual shockwave.

"Ghaa-ow!"

Having completed D's task, Koraidon looked extremely pleased.

It watched the fish shadows upstream, eager to try again: "Ghaa-ow?"

Although he couldn't understand, D miraculously understood its intention and hurriedly stopped it, "These are enough!"

As a competent rancher, one should consider the long-term and not exhaust resources, ensuring sustainable development.

We must give fish the time to reproduce, right?

Releasing ordinary Fish-type Pokemon like Magikarp and only keeping the regular river fish, they spent over ten minutes catching them all.

The metal bucket was filled to the brim, and looking at it, D felt very pleased.

A bountiful harvest!

Carrying the fish bucket back to the wooden house, Cyclizar was still under the eaves, peering outside.

Seeing D return, its eyes lit up, and it eagerly approached.

That scene reminded D of a big dog waiting for its owner to come home.

"Alright, alright, I'll cook you some grilled fish tonight!" D patted Cyclizar's head and picked a few plump, fresh trout from the bucket.

Just as he was busy, he heard the sound of a truck starting.

Looking up, he saw Zack wearing rain boots, pulling open the car door and jumping down.

"Today's weather is just awful! It rained the whole day, and I just went to several ranches, but there wasn't much to buy, just gathered a few baskets of eggs and a few cans of milk." Zack couldn't help but complain.

With this weather, most ranchers wouldn't venture out. They'd rather stay home, watching TV or chatting with their wife and kids.

"Do you have anything to sell?" Zack asked.

D carried out the bucket of fish he had just caught, "It's easier to catch fish on rainy days."

Zack widened his eyes, "So many?"

D exchanged a knowing smile with Koraidon beside him.

"That's quite unexpected!" Zack categorized the fish, weighed them, and then said, "Altogether, it's 5210 League Coins. You really work hard, don't you! Here!"

He said as he handed over the money.

"Thanks for your work!"

D took the money.

Just then, a light drizzle started falling from the sky again.

Zack glanced up, picked up the metal bucket, and said, "I won't linger, I'm off! Before the rain gets heavier again!"

"Alright."

The truck came and went in a hurry, driving towards Porto Marinada Town with fresh produce.

D took a deep breath and then began washing the mud off Koraidon.

Once clean, Koraidon instinctively shook the water droplets off its body, then happily shifted to speed form, pouncing on D and licking his face.

D was covered in slobber and almost knocked over, feeling speechless, "Why the sudden excitement…"

"Ghaa~"

He draped a blanket over Koraidon, helping it dry off before turning back inside to tuck the day's earnings away in a drawer.

Spring, 198, January 7th.

Income: Fish +5210

Expenses: 0

Remaining funds: 37700

"Just about 10,000 more, and I'll have enough to repair the Pokemon House. Once repaired, I can start breeding Wooloo and Miltank on the ranch… but I don't know the prices of Wooloo and Miltank, so this might not be enough."

"Maybe I should consider building a Chicken House first?"

D fell into thought.

Unlike the Pokemon House, building a Chicken House only costs 20,000 League Coins, and hens are much cheaper compared to Miltank and Wooloo.

Hens lay eggs quickly, with one healthy hen laying one egg per day. The laid eggs can be sold, used to hatch chicks, or kept for making dishes like omelets, steamed eggs, or omelets over rice.

This way, he wouldn't need to buy eggs from outside anymore and could be self-sufficient.

Although eggs are far from Chansey's quality, for him right now, raising Chansey is simply not feasible.

In comparison, raising chickens offers much better cost-effectiveness.

With this thought, D made a decision.

Once the weather clears, he'll commission Keri from the carpentry shop to build a Chicken House and formally start farming.
