# 25: Chapter 25: The Gradually Lively Ranch

In the Chicken House, D tossed feed into the feeding trough.

Smelling the aroma, three hens and a rooster swaggered to the trough and began to eat.

"It seems they have already adapted here!" The girl behind him watched the scene with a smile on her face. "In that case, Mr. D, I'll take my leave now. If you need anything, you can call the store. Our phone number is 9573868."

D: "...I haven't had the chance to buy a phone yet."

The girl was taken aback, seemingly not expecting this: "Huh?"

D asked with a smile, "Is it okay if I inquire directly at the store?"

The girl nodded quickly: "Of course!"

After explaining the necessary precautions, she put on a straw hat and left the ranch on her tricycle.

D stood in the Chicken House, watching the chickens.

Although hens can lay eggs without a rooster, those are unfertilized eggs and cannot hatch.

Only eggs laid after mating with a rooster can hatch chicks.

This is why D specifically bought a rooster.

The three hens are healthy, adult hens, already capable of laying eggs.

This means that starting tomorrow, he can expect at least three eggs a day.

"Well, a single egg doesn't sell for much, so the best method is to use all three for hatching." D thought to himself.

This way, by the time the crops outside mature, the ranch will welcome three new little lives.

The newly built Chicken House appeared a bit cluttered. D picked up the hayfork to move the hay stacked at the door to the corner.

After completing the task, D couldn't help but glance at his current attire.

Wearing brown overalls and carrying a hayfork on his shoulder, he looked just like a rustic farmer.

Putting down the hayfork, D walked out of the Chicken House, making sure to close the door behind him to prevent the hens from running wild.

On the way back to the cabin, D recalled the girl's words and thought to himself, "Besides Pokemon and crops, the basic facilities on the ranch need to be supplemented too."

Things like a phone, TV, phonograph, and computer.

A phone would allow him to contact people in town.

A TV could keep him informed of news and information beyond the ranch.

The phonograph could be used for edification, to play a serenade on rainy days when work outside wasn't possible, serving as a pleasant form of entertainment.

As for the computer...

It would help D quickly look up necessary information and also allow him to communicate and exchange ideas with netizens as far away as Kanto, Johto, Hoenn, and Alola.

But from the original owner's memory, it was clear these things weren't cheap. Before building entertainment facilities, he urgently needed to resolve food issues for himself and his Pokemon.

In any case, onward to a better tomorrow!

D was filled with determination.

In front of the cabin, Koraidon and Cyclizar had already returned.

Across from them, Alcremie and Smoliv were shivering with fear.

Seeing D, the two little ones immediately hid behind him, clinging to his left and right legs, peeking out at Koraidon's actions.

D: "..."

Koraidon had a puzzled look: "Gaii?"

So they were afraid of him?

But what was so frightening about him?

Koraidon looked down at his own body, then glanced at the well-behaved Cyclizar by his side, thinking: It's just a slight change in appearance...

D crouched down and patted the heads of Alcremie and Smoliv, soothingly saying, "Relax, it won't hurt you."

"Gaii!" Koraidon nodded vigorously, agreeing with this statement.

Hearing this, Alcremie and Smoliv cautiously looked over at Koraidon.

Koraidon stood there, letting them observe.

After a while, Alcremie and Smoliv exchanged glances.

"Mama..."

"Moo..."

It seems it really won't eat them, huh?

Seeing this, Koraidon human-like patted his chest with his large paw.

"Gaii Gaii!"

See, see!

Alcremie and Smoliv's fear faded, replaced by pure, innocent smiles.

"Mama~"

"Moo~"

D didn't understand what the Pokemon said.

But seeing Alcremie and Smoliv's expression, they seemed less afraid of Koraidon, so he picked them up one by one and carried them into the cabin.

"From now on, this will be our home!" D looked around and asked, "Where do you want to live?"

He gave them the option to choose.

Understanding D's intention, Alcremie and Smoliv began looking around, observing their surroundings while selecting a suitable place to live.

Soon, Smoliv chose a spot first—the windowsill above D's desk.

There, it was bathed in sunlight, with a gentle breeze occasionally blowing in from outside—an ideal place for a Grass Type Pokemon that relies on Synthesis to maintain energy.

D nodded, placing Smoliv on the windowsill, then turned to Alcremie.

"So, where do you want to be?"

Alcremie's gaze turned toward the kitchen's cupboard, eyes gleaming: "Mmm!"

"The cupboard?" D raised an eyebrow and led Alcremie forward, opening the cupboard to reveal the Berries and various spices stored inside.

Alcremie jumped in willingly, tucking itself into a small corner of the cupboard.

"Mmm!" This is it!

"Uh... alright." Initially, D thought it was too small for Alcremie to live there.

But on further thought, considering Alcremie's ecology, he realized there might not be any place more suitable.

Perhaps out of concern that it might be eaten by sweet-tooth Pokemon, Alcremie naturally liked tight spaces where it felt secure.

"That's not too bad."

D nodded. Smelling the faint sweet scent in the air, he thought the cream produced by this Alcremie might have a caramel flavor.

D hadn't had sweets for a long time. Now smelling this scent, he had a sudden, terrifying urge to lick Alcremie.

He quickly dispelled that thought and, after settling Alcremie, turned to look at Smoliv.

Staring at the somewhat empty windowsill, D fell into thought.

It seemed something was missing there...

Besides sunlight, Grass Type Pokemon also need soil and water.

Oh right, a flowerpot!

D remembered there was an empty flowerpot in the storage room behind the cabin!

He immediately headed to the storage room, retrieved the dusty flowerpot, rinsed it clean under the tap, and filled it with some loose soil from the field.

D placed the flowerpot in front of Smoliv.

Smoliv looked delighted.

It jumped into the flowerpot, sinking its little feet into the soil, then lazily basked in the sun.

"You really know how to enjoy life."

D chuckled softly, placing the flowerpot on the windowsill, then opened the window to let in the gentle breeze from outside.

# 26: Chapter 26: Caramel-Flavored Cream Fruit Cake

Some Grass-type Pokémon are quite special.

They do not need food and can live only on sunlight, soil, and clean water.

Smoliv is one of them.

When it bathes in the sun, it converts the nutrients produced by photosynthesis into oil stored in the fruit on its head, which allows it to go a week without eating or drinking.

Watching Smoliv's lazy demeanor, D also sat at the desk.

Turning around, Koraidon stood there energetically looking at him.

Perhaps tired from chasing butterflies outside, Cyclizar lay on the wooden floor yawning.

In the cupboard, Alcremie looked around this new home contentedly.

On the windowsill, Smoliv lazily basked in the sun and enjoyed the breeze.

Under the eaves outside, the Metapod that had evolved from Caterpie was diligently growing, waiting for that day to come.

In the newly-built Chicken House, hens clucked as they moved about.

Under the soft soil, crops that bring hope to people were thriving.

In the cave at the back of the house, mushroom mycelium was sprouting, and it was believed that within a few days, delicious plump mushrooms would grow.

The entire ranch had undergone a significant change compared to when D first came here.

It's really...getting more and more lively!

A feeling of unprecedented satisfaction emerged in D's heart.

He took out the ledger and meticulously recorded today's income and expenses.

Spring 198, January 14th.

Income:

Expenses: Construction of Chicken House, wooden fence, purchase of hens ×3, rooster ×1, Alcremie ×1, Smoliv ×1, -30600

Remaining funds: 5900

Looking at the names of Alcremie and Smoliv in the ledger, D suddenly realized something.

Actually, he didn't need to buy some Pokémon from the ranch in town.

A Rancher isn't a Trainer and doesn't have much combat ability.

Wild Pokémon like Garganacl, Noibat, and Miltank are pretty much impossible for ordinary Ranchers to capture, and even if they manage to capture them, it takes time to train them to work in the ranch.

Compared to wild Pokémon, those bred artificially are generally very tame, do not attack people, and actively cooperate with Ranchers in their work.

So, it is most convenient for them to directly purchase from a ranch where they are specifically bred.

But for D, it's different.

He turned his head and exchanged a glance with Koraidon.

Koraidon, being a legendary Pokémon, known as the king of wings, should have far superior combat abilities compared to normal Pokémon.

With Koraidon around, D could do what ordinary Ranchers couldn't, capturing Pokémon from the wild to bring back to his ranch.

It's decided, tomorrow I'll head to Casseroya Lake to investigate the Pokémon ecology there!

See if there's anything suitable to keep on the ranch.

————

Outside, the sky was already late, and the entire ranch was shrouded in the twilight.

D, along with Koraidon and Cyclizar, checked the condition of the crops in the farmland.

In the past half month, the turnips, which grow quickly, have successfully sprouted leaves, neatly arranged in the grid-like farmland, looking very pleasing to the eye.

"Luckily, only a few weeds grew, no pests."

After pulling out the weeds, D looked up at the sky, seeing that it was getting late, so he returned to the house with Koraidon and Cyclizar to start preparing dinner.

Upon seeing his actions, Alcremie immediately jumped out of the cupboard, moved to the stove, showing no fear of fire.

D raised an eyebrow, seeing Alcremie's action, he guessed: "You want to help me prepare dinner?"

"Maa maa!"

Alcremie nodded happily.

D looked down at the ingredients placed before him, including eggs, berries, smoked sausages, slices of toast, raw meat patties, and noodles.

They could be used to make fried pork cutlets, steamed eggs, sandwiches, and clear soup noodles.

To celebrate the arrival of Alcremie and Smoliv, D intended to make several dishes tonight.

Upon hearing Alcremie's words, he began to ponder what dishes could be made with cream.

As everyone knows, Alcremie can produce whipped cream from its hands, which it decorates berries with and then gives to Trainers it trusts.

The happier Alcremie is, the richer the taste of the whipped cream it produces.

With desserts adorned with such whipped cream being exceptionally delicious, having Alcremie as a partner is every Pastry Chef's dream.

Cream...

Looking at the packaged toast slices and berries, D suddenly thought of a good idea.

Cream fruit cake!

Although he did not have an oven or whisk yet, he could use toast slices as the cake base. By spreading a layer of whipped cream and placing a few berries on top, he could create a simple fruit cake.

"In that case, let's prepare together!"

D stacked several pieces of toast together, shaped them into a circle with a knife, and placed them in front of Alcremie.

"Use your cream to coat this cake base!" D said.

The clever Alcremie immediately understood his intention.

It extended its hand, and the whipped cream poured out continuously, coating the cake base made of toast.

When it was almost done, D called out for it to stop, smoothing out the cream with a knife, then cutting the berries into small pieces and placing them on top.

Thus, a caramel-flavored cream fruit cake was completed.

As D looked down, the cake's information appeared before him.

[Cream Fruit Cake (Caramel Flavor) (D-): A dessert of average quality, made with Alcremie's hand-whipped cream, if a proper cake base had been used, its grade might have been higher.

Cooking Effect: Sweet Dream lv1, after Pokémon eat it, they can temporarily forget worries and sadness, having a sweet dream where they meet the people and things they wish to see.

Evaluation: A dessert that can bring happiness to humans and Pokémon alike.]

Huh?

A dream-inducing effect for Pokémon?

D was a bit surprised; he originally thought the effect might be to recover stamina.

But come to think of it, having sweet dreams seems to be the ability of the Dream God Cresselia?

D mused a little.

Successful in helping D make the cake, Alcremie seemed extremely happy.

D couldn't help but chuckle: "Just wait a bit, I'll make a few more dishes."

"Maa maa?"

Upon hearing this, Alcremie immediately raised its hand, asking if cream was still needed?

"No need for now." D shook his head: "Let's make desserts together again next time!"

"Maa maa~"

Alcremie happily agreed.

D then began preparing the remaining dishes.

Fried pork cutlets, clear soup noodles, steamed eggs, sandwiches, and finally, a few glasses of Berry Juice.

When making the first three dishes, D specially added Purifying Salt given to him by the elder at Happy Ranch.

As expected, after completing these dishes, an extra effect [Stamina Recovery] appeared.

D had made fried pork cutlets and clear soup noodles before, but this was his first time making steamed eggs.

The steamed egg had a soft yellow color, dotted with small clusters of green scallions, finally drizzled with a drop of sesame oil, glistening under the light. When served, the steamed egg in the bowl gently wobbled like pudding with any movement.

[Steamed Egg (B+): A dish of average quality, the addition of Purifying Salt grants it an extra magical effect.

Cooking Effect: Slippery Lv3, Hairless Pokémon only, after consumption, the Pokémon's body becomes smooth and shiny, making it easier to escape from contact moves during battles, duration: 3 hours.

Additional Effect: Stamina Recovery

Evaluation: Enjoy it with your Pokémon!]

# 27: Chapter 27: Chicken Bro's Exclusive Enhancement (Double-Length Chapter)

Slippery…

What are these bizarre dishes!

After serving a few dishes on the table, D picked up Alcremie and placed it on the table.

He secretly sniffed his hand, and sure enough, it had a sweet caramel cream scent left on it.

Koraidon and Cyclizar also gathered around the table, and D went to bring Smoliv over, placing it next to Alcremie.

The two cute little guys sat side by side, happily eating the cake D had cut, their little faces covered in cream.

"How does it taste?" D asked curiously.

"Ma~"

"Fuu~"

Alcremie and Smoliv showed happy smiles on their faces; from the looks of it, they seemed to find the cake quite delicious.

"That's good."

The fresh cream produced by Alcremie is of excellent quality, and cakes made from it are bound to taste good. Koraidon and Cyclizar should also like it.

When D turned to look at Koraidon and Cyclizar, he saw them both with cream all over their paws and faces, just like two big flower cats.

D: "…"

No, to be precise, they were like two big dumb lizards.

"I wonder how the effect of the Sweet Dream will show."

After a brief thought, D decided to stay up a bit later to observe the sleep condition of the Pokémon.

As for that slippery steamed egg…

Cyclizar, having finished the cake, simply picked up the bowl, and the incredibly smooth steamed egg slid right into its mouth with a slurp.

"Gulp!"

Cyclizar swallowed the steamed egg with little effort; it seemed to have eaten too quickly to savor the taste.

Cyclizar had a puzzled expression on its face, feeling as if it had eaten nothing at all.

Cyclizar dropped its head, looked at the empty bowl, then at the other bowls with steamed eggs.

Alcremie and Smoliv were enjoying their cake, and their portion was still untouched.

Koraidon's bowl was clean as well, but it ate slowly, unlike Cyclizar's rough manner, apparently knowing how to savor the taste of the steamed egg.

After looking around, Cyclizar returned its gaze to its own bowl.

Why does it feel so silly?

D recalled what the old village chief had said, that this Cyclizar hasn't fully matured yet, so its mind might not be fully developed.

So it would foolishly chase after butterflies, playing around in the bushes.

Thinking of this, D couldn't help but push his portion over: "Have mine!"

"Gao~!"

Cyclizar's eyes lit up, just about to happily grab it when it suddenly felt an unignorable gaze from the side.

Koraidon: Stare!

"Gao…"

Cyclizar seemed a bit scared of Koraidon.

D raised an eyebrow slightly.

"Grahh!"

Koraidon gave Cyclizar a warning call and then extended a large paw to push D's portion back.

D couldn't help but say: "Koraidon, don't scare Cyclizar, it's still young…"

"Grahh!"

Unexpectedly, after hearing those words, the dragon whiskers on top of Koraidon's head drooped down immediately, looking very aggrieved.

D: "… I didn't mean it like that." Why does he sound like an old father who favors his little son?

He was momentarily speechless, not knowing what to say.

At this point, Cyclizar realized something was off with the atmosphere; although it wasn't particularly smart, it gradually became aware of what had happened.

Plus, considering what Koraidon had said earlier—D only made five servings of steamed egg, one for each Pokémon, and if it took that portion, D wouldn't have any left.

Cyclizar looked down at its bowl, then shook its head towards D: "Gao!"

D also found his own solution; refusing to play favorites, he stood up to make four more bowls of steamed egg.

"There, now everyone can eat!"

D looked at Koraidon and handed it a glass of Berry Juice, "I was wrong, I shouldn't have blamed you earlier, don't be mad."

Koraidon looked at him, then extended a paw to take the Berry Juice, showing a happy smile again.

D breathed a sigh of relief, suddenly feeling that raising Pokémon is akin to raising children.

Koraidon is the eldest, Cyclizar is the second, with Alcremie and Smoliv as the babies in the stroller.

After the somewhat harmonious dinner ended, the sky outside had completely darkened.

D washed up the dishes and stood in front of the door, looking towards the Chicken House, suddenly unable to remember if he had closed its door properly.

Feeling uneasy, he took a flashlight to check, ensuring the door was closed properly before returning to the cabin with ease.

D prepared a basin of water, using a towel to wipe off the cream on the Pokémon, letting them Sleep first, then went outside to take a quick cold shower.

Although ranch life was tiring, it was much better compared to his past life's endless 996 overtimes.

Especially when he saw the crops he planted grow, the inner sense of fulfillment was indescribable.

Not to mention having the help of a bunch of cute Pokémon.

Recalling the events of these days, D couldn't help but relax, feeling no longer bewildered or at a loss as when he first arrived.

People always need to find something to do to lead a fulfilling life.

After drying himself off with a towel, D put on large shorts and entered the cabin.

The Pokémon were already asleep; D wanted to observe their sleep state, but when he looked, he saw something like bubbles above their heads.

---

Inside, there were a series of different scenes playing out, as if these were the dreams they were having.

Seeing this scene, D was a little surprised.

"Is this the effect of Sweet Dreams?"

At this moment, D suddenly remembered that there seemed to be a similar feature in a game.

That dish also made his little characters dream, and as a player and creator, he was also able to see the characters' dreams through the screen.

This function could deepen players' understanding of the little characters they cultivated, encouraging them to invest more emotions in these two-dimensional characters, spending money to buy various tools for their use, thereby creating more profit for the company.

So, as the creator of the recipe, he could also see the dreams of the Pokémon.

D was a bit curious, and while drying his hair, he began to watch the dreams of several Pokémon.

Koraidon's dream was very simple.

The background of the dream was a sky, with the long whiskers on Koraidon's head spreading out and transforming into wings, carrying him soaring in the sky.

There were no other Pokémon there, just the two of them in the boundless sky.

In the dream, Koraidon looked very happy, carrying D and flying repeatedly, as if this was the happiest thing for him.

D watched this scene amusedly, thinking that even though they'd only known each other for less than a month, Koraidon almost saw him as the entire world.

Gently reaching out to stroke the head of the sleeping Koraidon, D didn't wake him up and turned his head to look at Cyclizar next to him.

The background of Cyclizar's dream hadn't developed into the sky; it remained in this small wooden house.

However, it seemed that it was winter.

It was snowing outside, and a thick layer of snow covered the roof and windowsill.

Inside the house, a roaring fire burned in the fireplace, with a thick carpet spread on the wooden floor, and a few books laid on the carpet.

D sat on the carpet reading, with Cyclizar lying behind him serving as a cushion, dozing with his eyes closed.

He looked peaceful, without any worries or sorrow.

Looking at this scene, D thought, "Is this what Cyclizar wants to see the most?"

That's quite simple.

When winter comes, he could help Cyclizar fulfill this wish.

D then went to the windowsill.

Smoliv was curled up sleeping in a flowerpot, her dream background was out in the ranch.

In the dream, she had already evolved into Arboliva, gently providing D with the rich and delicious oil she made.

Because D was good to her, not only making her good food, but also reserving the best spot in the room for her, and even specifically finding a flowerpot for her to live in.

Smoliv liked this rancher a lot, so she wanted to evolve into Arboliva, working hard to produce more oil for him.

Seeing this scene, it's impossible not to feel touched.

Pokémon are very simple; if you treat them well, they will treat you well in return.

D was filled with emotion, reaching out to touch Smoliv before coming to the cupboard, opening the top door to see Alcremie's dream.

The background of Alcremie's dream was a kitchen.

In Alcremie's dream, he seemed to have become a Sweet Dessert Master.

In the dream, Alcremie was preparing desserts with him, providing fresh cream while he made the desserts.

In just a few minutes, they produced all kinds of cream cakes, cream pies, cream puddings, and fresh fruit milk.

These desserts filled every corner of the room, enveloping them within.

Alcremie happily swam in the ocean of desserts.

The background kept changing, and they fell from the kitchen into a land made of various desserts and chocolates.

Alcremie leisurely lounged on a giant fruitcake, bathing in the sunlight.

And in the dream, he was like a big fool, voraciously eating the gigantic desserts around him.

D: "..."

Could it be that Alcremie wanted to subconsciously share his produced fresh cream for him to taste?

But the scene in the dream was really terrifying.

If he really indulged like that, he'd probably have diabetes in less than a month.

"..."

Having witnessed the effect of the fruit cream cake, D gained an initial understanding of it.

And this was only the lowest level, lv1; what would happen if it reached lv3?

D couldn't imagine.

————

Lying in bed, D's mind replayed what had just happened.

Clearly, for these Pokémon, he had suddenly entered this world and had already become an indispensable part of their hearts.

D took a deep breath.

At this moment, the confusion he had when he first arrived in this world was gone, replaced by a new motivation.

Not just managing the ranch with a carefree gaming attitude, but truly for his Pokémon companions to have a better life, and for his own newfound life.

He wanted to work hard to run the ranch and build a home for them!

D's heart was full of energy.

Early the next morning, he got up early to go feed the chickens in the Chicken House.

Overnight, three various-sized eggs appeared on the ground of the Chicken House.

D picked them up and stored them before starting to add water to the basin and sprinkle feed into the feeder.

The so-called chicken feed was actually a mix of various grains, shaped a bit like cat food.

Looking at the feed in his hand, D had a thought, suddenly remembering the Steamed Green Grass and Spicy Iron Block he had tossed out before.

The question arose: could the chicken feed be processed the same way?

Though a human, D thought his thinking shouldn't be limited to ingredients edible by humans; he should open his mind and try making dishes with other things.

With this thought, D grabbed a handful of chicken feed and took it back to the wooden house.

He stared at the feed in the bowl, lost in thought.

What method should he use to prepare it?

Should it be steamed, boiled, fried, or stir-fried...?

Chickens are omnivores; whether it's vegetables, grains, or meat, they accept it all.

D thought these processes were no different from melting store-bought chocolate and pouring it into his mold to cool and shape.

"Anyway, let's try it first!"

With this in mind, D set a pot to boil water, preparing to try steaming the chicken feed.

Composed of grains, steaming the feed too long would make it fall apart, so D steamed it for only three minutes before taking it off the pot.

The steamed chicken feed was a bit soft and mushy, and as the steam rose, D smelled a grain aroma, but from the appearance, it looked completely like a dark dish.

Looking down, the relevant information emerged.

[Steamed Chicken Feed (F): An unappraisable dish, not suitable for human consumption, perhaps some other creatures might like it.

Cooking Effect: Egg explosion. When given to roosters, it accelerates the fertilization of eggs. When given to hens, it can produce a large number of eggs in a short time.

But after laying eggs, hens enter a 15-day cooldown period during which they can no longer lay eggs or consume this dish, requiring a large amount of food to replenish energy.

Evaluation: A work of wild imagination!]

Poof!

Whoa!

It actually works?!

D was extremely shocked.

Eager to see the effect, he took it to the Chicken House.

Koraidon and Cyclizar also followed along like little tails.

D casually selected a hen and placed the soft and mushy steamed chicken feed in front of it.

The hen tilted its head to look down before eating it.

One second, two seconds, three seconds, on the fifth second, the hen suddenly flapped its wings and took flight on the spot.

With wide-open eyes, it swiftly ran to a patch of dry grass, exerting all muscles from top to bottom at once.

"Cluck cluck!"

Soon after, with a loud crow, a round egg rolled out from behind the hen.

Then another, one after another like a seed machine gun crazily falling onto the dry grass.

D, Cyclizar, and Koraidon all stared at this scene, dumbfounded.

This crazy egg-laying lasted about a minute, and with the fall of the last egg, the hen showed a relieved expression, like a constipated human who finally found relief.

Exhausted, it flapped its wings and ran to the side.

Meanwhile, the pile of dry grass was now filled with a mountain of eggs.

---

---

---

---

# 28: Chapter 28: Unexpected Visitors

"One, two, three... seventeen, eighteen, twenty... Whoa, that many?!"

D was a bit shocked.

He turned his head to look at the hen that, after laying so many eggs at once, seemed to be in a state of exhaustion. It was wobbling as it walked, looking quite spiritless.

After wobbling for a while, the hen walked over to the feeding trough and began devouring the chicken feed inside like crazy.

Seeing this, D felt a bit worried.

Could it be that he broke the hen?

D watched for a moment, then recalled in his mind the effects of that special chicken feed:

When fed to a rooster, it speeds up the fertilization of the eggs. When fed to a hen, it causes it to lay a large number of eggs in a short time.

However, after laying eggs, the hen would enter a 15-day cooldown period during which it cannot lay eggs or consume this feed and requires a lot of food to replenish its energy.

"So, it's now replenishing its lost energy." D felt relieved in his heart.

Thankfully, the effect of this feed doesn't completely drain the hen. Based on the description, it should be something like borrowing from the future, laying the eggs that would have been laid over the next 15 days all at once.

Once the eggs are laid, the hen enters a state of exhaustion and needs to consume a large amount of feed.

D stood by and observed the hen's actions.

He saw that after the hen finished eating its portion of the feed, it started to eat the feed of the other two hens as well.

It didn't stop until it finished all the feed in the trough, then it returned to its nest, lay on the pile of eggs, yawned, and quickly fell asleep.

D stood up and began calculating in his mind.

From the original owner's memory, he knew that eggs in this world were very cheap. A box of 10 eggs sold for just 250 League coins in the market.

And Zack bought eggs from the ranch at wholesale price, so the price he sold them for was slightly lower than the market price.

This meant that the entire pile of eggs in front of him was worth less than 500.

After deducting the cost of the feed the hen consumed, there was hardly any profit.

Thinking about it this way, the effect of this feed didn't seem that outrageous.

D took half of the eggs to keep for his own use, and left the other half for the hen to hatch.

At the same time, he opened the door of the Chicken House and let the other three hens out to bask in the sun and get some fresh air.

After finishing all this, D went to water the crops in the field.

He only had a little over four thousand left, and it would be over half a month before this batch of crops matured.

Relying on selling eggs couldn't earn much, so today D planned to explore Casseroya Lake to the north of the ranch.

To prevent any wild Pokemon from causing trouble on the ranch while he was away, D planned to leave Cyclizar to guard the house.

Back at the cabin, he took out six Poke Balls he had bought from a store in Porto Marinada Town. Ever since he bought these, they had been left in the drawer.

Because they weren't needed on the ranch, his original intention was to take Pokemon with him during his future outings.

Thinking of this, D glanced at Koraidon by his side.

Koraidon was also looking at him.

"Have you seen this thing before?" D asked.

"A-ga-si!" Koraidon nodded its big head.

Seeing this, D thought: It must have seen this when it was in Zone Zero with Professor Sada.

In the game, Koraidon was always Professor Sada's Pokemon.

After Professor Sada passed away, it lost a territory battle with another Koraidon and met the game protagonist by the sea after fleeing the Paldea Giant Pit.

Then the game protagonist got Koraidon's Poke Ball from Professor Sada's son, Arven.

So, Koraidon's Poke Ball should be in Arven's hands now?

D fell into thought.

Arven, nicknamed Batman, because his parents, Professor Sada and Professor Turo, also passed away like Batman's parents.

As the son of Professor Sada and Professor Turo, Arven once lived with his parents and Koraidon for a long time.

But later, due to Koraidon's deep-seated wildness and fighting spirit causing it to attack other Pokemon,

Seeing that Koraidon's existence was about to be known to the world, and to prevent more Pokemon from being attacked by Koraidon, Professor Sada had to take it back to the Paldea Giant Pit and has lived there ever since.

This made young Arven feel that Koraidon took his mother away from him, and coupled with Professor Sada being busy with experiments and neglecting Arven, led Arven to hate Koraidon and his parents, disliking it when others mentioned them.

"Forget it, I'll deal with this later!"

After all, Koraidon's Poke Ball is with Arven, and once a Pokemon is captured, it can't be captured again with another Poke Ball.

Because when the Poke Ball first contains the Pokemon, it marks it with something like a tag.

In such cases, trying to capture it with another Poke Ball won't have any reaction.

If D didn't remember wrongly, regular Poke Balls seemed to work like this.

To test it, he placed the ball against Koraidon's body.

But after a few seconds passed, Koraidon did not get drawn into the Poke Ball.

Seeing this, Koraidon looked down at the Poke Ball, appearing somewhat puzzled.

"So, it really won't work."

D put away the Poke Ball, abandoning the idea of capturing Koraidon with it.

He packed drinking water, berries, Poke Balls, and other items into his travel backpack, then slung it over his back, ready to set off with Koraidon to explore Casseroya Lake and check out the information about the fish there.

Just then, the sound of Cyclizar's growl came from outside the door.

D, with his travel backpack on, walked out the door to check and saw Cyclizar looking toward the ranch entrance, barking loudly, "Ga-ow!"

"What's up?"

D followed its gaze, and then saw two familiar yet unfamiliar figures.

In the front was a valiant woman, wearing a white lab coat with a primal-styled fur outfit underneath, accessorized with a fang necklace, exuding a wild charm from head to toe.

Behind her was a small boy, about eight years old.

He had the same light brown short hair as the woman in front, and from their appearance, they seemed like a mother and son duo.

They stopped at a distance, their gazes falling on D and Koraidon standing under the eaves.

The woman's eyes were full of confidence, her gaze lingering on Koraidon before she politely inquired of D:

"Hello, did you save this Pokemon?"

---

---

---

---

# 29: Chapter 29: Determination

What the hell!

Looking at the two people opposite him, two names instantly flashed in D's mind—Professor Sada and her son Arven.

What's going on? Isn't Professor Sada already dead? D brainstormed quickly, looking at the little boy beside Professor Sada.

The boy wasn't very old, but from his striking resemblance to Professor Sada, there was undeniably a blood relationship between them.

He is Arven!

So, exactly what time is it now?

Could it be that he hasn't arrived at the beginning of the "Pokemon Scarlet and Violet" game storyline, but rather ten years earlier?

But why would Koraidon flee the Paldea Giant Pit injured, just like in the game's story?

No, now doesn't seem to be the time to discuss this.

D looked toward Professor Sada and Arven waiting for an answer not far away. He nodded, pretending not to know: "And you are?"

Professor Sada stared into his eyes for a while, then shifted her gaze to Koraidon beside him: "I am Professor Sada, and the Pokemon next to you is Koraidon, from a distant ancient past."

"Not long ago, we used certain technology to summon it from that era to here, but after it arrived, it attacked us and fled the research facility."

"Koraidon..." D pondered this information that wasn't in the game.

Professor Sada continued, "However, the primordial and elemental power from the distant past is extremely ferocious, and this power will one day inevitably drive it to attack other Pokemon and humans."

After this Koraidon left, Professor Sada and her husband, Professor Turo, respectively used the Time Machine to summon another Koraidon and a Miraidon from the future.

They lived with these two Pokemon alongside their son Arven for some time.

Initially, everything was fine, but suddenly, one day, Koraidon and Miraidon became violent and attacked other Pokemon.

To prevent more Pokemon from being attacked, Professor Sada and Professor Turo took them back to Zone Zero at the bottom of the Paldea Giant Pit.

At the same time, they began tracking the Koraidon that had escaped Zone Zero, eventually discovering its trace in Porto Marinada Town.

D frowned as he listened.

Koraidon beside him was tense: "Roaaar..."

When D looked at it, Koraidon's tense expression immediately softened.

Seeing this scene, Professor Sada felt a bit surprised and unexpected, but remembering the terrifying images of Koraidon and Miraidon attacking Pokemon and ravaging the land, she continued:

"It seems it has developed feelings for you, but I'm very sorry. The Pokemon summoned from ancient times are fierce and unlike modern Pokemon, we cannot ensure it won't harm you, nor can we ensure it won't harm other Pokemon and humans beside you."

"So, I hope you can return Koraidon to us, and we will take it away from the Paldea Region to where it belongs."

Hearing this, D's heart tightened.

He knew Professor Sada was right; even though Koraidon appeared clumsy and cute in the game, it also had a fiery temper that forced Professor Sada to take it back to Zone Zero.

Logically and emotionally, Professor Sada could reclaim this Koraidon.

But...

Professor Sada took out a specially crafted Poke Ball from her lab coat pocket, preparing to capture this Koraidon and take it back to Zone Zero.

Professor Sada threw the Poke Ball towards Koraidon.

Koraidon, watching the Poke Ball flying towards it, suddenly went berserk.

A terrifying power surged wildly within it.

Koraidon took a step forward, letting out a roar as its massive crimson body charged forth, its Dragon Claw, gleaming with cold light, striking down.

Before anyone could react, the Poke Ball was swatted away.

The red and white Poke Ball was shredded by Koraidon's Dragon Claw, fell to the ground with a crackling sound, and emitted black smoke as it broke.

Then, Koraidon exhibited an aggressive stance towards Professor Sada.

D was startled by Koraidon's sudden movement and hurriedly shouted: "Koraidon!"

At the same time, Little Arven, who seemed unhappy before, stepped in front of Professor Sada: "You're not allowed to hurt her!"

A white light emerged from his pocket and fell to the ground, revealing Mabosstiff's figure.

Koraidon ignored Mabosstiff, stopping immediately upon hearing D's call, and turned back with a grievance to D.

"Roaaar..."

"Koraidon?"

D called tentatively.

And then he saw Koraidon walk over to him, sadly using its large paw to embrace him, as if it were acting cute.

D was slightly startled.

Professor Sada watched this scene with a shocked and bewildered, incredulous expression.

That Koraidon...

Although D didn't understand what happened, he miraculously comprehended Koraidon's intentions at this moment, feeling its emotions.

—It wanted to stay here, it didn't want to leave him.

Memories of the past surged in D's mind, he looked into Koraidon's eyes, and asked: "Will you harm me and other Pokemon?"

Koraidon shook its big head: "Roaaar..."

With Koraidon's promise, D looked at Professor Sada and Arven, saying to them: "Sorry, Professor Sada, I cannot give Koraidon back to you."

Professor Sada was puzzled: "Why? Aren't you afraid it might one day suddenly attack you?"

D shook his head, his heart pure and clear as a mirror: "I trust it, rather than worrying about things that haven't happened, I prefer to believe in my eyes. The Koraidon I now see and understand wouldn't do such things."

Upon hearing these words, Professor Sada paused.

At this moment, she gazed at this "special" Koraidon, suddenly wanting to know what had happened to it after leaving Zone Zero.

Sada had a dream in her heart.

She wanted to see a world where ancient Pokemon and modern Pokemon lived harmoniously together.

So, after reading the Book of Scarlet and Violet, she and her husband ventured into the dangerous Paldea Giant Pit, building an observation station there, and began researching the Time Machine using the Crystal at the deepest point to summon Pokemon from ancient and future times onto this timeline.

However, the Koraidon and Miraidon they summoned displayed a violent side, which left Professor Sada and her husband feeling defeated.

And now... she was witnessing another side of the Pokemon dubbed as "The Winged King" in the Book of Scarlet and Violet.

"If possible, could you tell me about your experiences with this Koraidon?" Professor Sada asked.

---

---

---

---

# 30: Chapter 30: Book of Scarlet and Violet

In the cabin, Professor Sada and young Arven sat on chairs meant for entertaining guests, observing the surroundings.

The room was furnished with various pieces of furniture. Although it was somewhat cramped, everything appeared to be in perfect order.

On the windowsill sat a flower pot, with a Smoliv lazily basking in the sun. Judging by its demeanor, it was well cared for.

It seemed the owner here was someone who loved cleanliness and had a deep affection for Pokémon.

"Thanks for waiting!"

D brought drinks to Professor Sada, young Arven, and Mabosstiff.

There wasn't any tea on the ranch, so he used MooMoo Milk from Porto Marinada Market, added some Alcremie's cream and Berry chunks, creating a simple caramel-flavored Berry Milk.

The effect of this milk was to remind Pokémon of sweet, joyful events while restoring their energy.

The still-sullen young Arven took a sip, and his face immediately showed a surprised expression.

Beside him, Mabosstiff licked a few times with its tongue, eyes widening, then couldn't help but speed up its licking.

Professor Sada didn't drink but instead began to speak:

"My husband Turo and I have been researching [past] and [future] Pokémon in the Sky Pit of the Paldea Region, until recently, when the Time Machine was successfully developed. Those Pokémon recorded in the Book of Scarlet and Violet were summoned to our world."

D looked at Professor Sada, keenly noticing the information in her words.

"Book of Scarlet and Violet?"

Professor Sada was surprised, "You don't know?"

"I've heard of it."

He was well aware. D thought to himself, but he was concerned that Professor Sada mentioned the Book of Scarlet and Violet, not separately the Book of Scarlet and the Book of Violet.

As everyone knows, GF divides games into two versions for sale. Pokémon Scarlet tells the story of Professor Sada and past Pokémon; that was the Book of Scarlet.

The author of the Book of Scarlet was a man named Heath, who documented his exploration with the Observation Team to an unknown area in the Paldea Region, where they saw Paradox Species Pokémon not native to the Paldea Region.

Meanwhile, Pokémon Violet narrated the story of Professor Sada's husband, Turo, and future Pokémon.

This version had no Book of Scarlet, instead, it had the Book of Violet, featuring future paradox Pokémon.

According to Professor Sada's words, the world's Book of Scarlet and Violet wasn't separate but was one the same.

Likewise, Professor Turo, responsible for researching future-form Paradox Pokémon, existed in this world.

Realizing this information, D's first thought wasn't about those past and future-form Paradox Pokémon.

Instead, it was that Arven might lose both his father and mother sometime in the future, becoming a true Batman...

With that thought, D looked over to Arven drinking milk with Mabosstiff.

Professor Sada, noticing his gaze, turned to her son beside her, her eyes revealing a hint of an imperceptible gentle expression, and she introduced, "This is my son, Arven."

At this time, Arven was not yet the senior exploring for Secret Spices, but just a child not yet ten years old.

D thought of Arven just now bravely facing Koraidon to protect Professor Sada and praised, "He's a very courageous boy!"

Due to his parents being busy with work, young Arven mistakenly believed Koraidon and Miraidon took his parents away, thus disliking others mentioning them.

But in fact, he deeply loved his father and mother, wishing them to spend more time with him.

Unfortunately...

Poor child. A sense of pity welled up in D's heart.

Hearing his words, Arven looked up at him.

Beside him, Professor Sada also showed a smile.

"Arven has always been very sensible. My husband and I, because of work, have no time to care for him. He has lived alone since a very young age."

Arven lowered his head again, silently sipping juice without speaking.

"Woof woof!" Mabosstiff beside him barked twice.

Professor Sada added, "Oh, and Mabosstiff accompanies Arven in his life."

D nodded but said no more, listening as Professor Sada recounted his encounter with Koraidon:

"That night, it suddenly appeared in the ranch; because it was injured, I, along with Cyclizar, brought it back to the house, treated it with Berries and medicine, and since then, it stayed, living with us."

"During this time, has it shown any aggressiveness or anger?"

D thought back carefully and shook his head: "No, it has always been well-behaved."

Recalling how Koraidon blasted fish with its powerful moves, he couldn't help but smile: "It also helps me work in its own way, though sometimes it's a bit heavy-handed, using its best moves to help me catch fish."

Professor Sada: "..."

Imagining the scene, she suddenly felt incredible.

Professor Sada looked thoughtful.

Moments later, she turned to see Koraidon resting not far away, pretending to nap while actually keenly aware of the surroundings.

Its strong body, crimson skin, flamboyant, imposing whiskers, and large, heavy paws...

Everything appeared identical to the Koraidon they later summoned.

Is it because it knows this rancher saved it, forming a bond with him?

Beyond this, Professor Sada couldn't think of any possibilities.

After pondering for a while, Professor Sada pulled out a somewhat outdated phone from her lab coat pocket.

She told D, "Please wait a moment, I want to communicate with my husband, Professor Turo."

D nodded slightly, "Okay."

Professor Sada went outside, standing on the ranch grass as she dialed the phone.

Inside the house, only D and Arven remained.

Looking at the unfortunate boy with such a tragic life, D couldn't help but say, "Your name is Arven, right?"

Arven withdrew his gaze from his mother's back and replied sullenly, "Yes."

Following his gaze, D said, "Your mother is an amazing person to have developed something like a Time Machine. Have you ever fantasized about traveling to the distant past and future?"

Arven looked down at Mabosstiff beside him, sounding a bit depressed: "That's their wish."

At this moment, Professor Sada ended the call and returned inside.

After brief communication with her husband, they decided, after careful consideration, to let Koraidon stay here.

Professor Sada glanced at Koraidon, then said to D, "D, I'll leave Koraidon in your care."

It seemed they had already discussed and agreed on this.

D nodded, "I will."

Professor Sada continued, "Do you have contact information? Let's exchange! If anything happens to Koraidon in the future, or if you need any help, feel free to contact me. I'd also like to learn more about Koraidon from you."

---

---

---

---

# 31: Chapter 31: Video Phone

D felt a bit awkward: "I don't have a phone yet."

Professor Sada was slightly stunned.

She suddenly thought about the situation she had just seen outside.

This ranch seemed to have just started, and from the young man's attire and living environment, his life seemed somewhat financially strapped.

Thinking of this, Professor Sada didn't say much.

She simply said she would come back in the afternoon and left with little Arven.

D looked at her back, bewildered, and turned around only when she was out of sight, looking at Koraidon hiding behind the door, secretly watching.

D breathed a sigh of relief in his heart and smiled at it: "They've left."

Koraidon: "Grrr..."

D stepped forward and petted Koraidon like a dog. Koraidon was so comfortable that it almost purred with contentment.

Seeing it enjoy itself, D teased, "Do you like me that much?"

All in all, they had only lived together for half a month.

Upon hearing this, Koraidon opened its eyes, looking extremely happy, and began licking D's face wildly with its big tongue.

Nowadays, D no longer felt afraid; he knew Koraidon meant no harm to him.

Wiping the slobber off his face with his hand, D stood up, looked at the crops growing not far away in the field, and took a deep breath.

Professor Sada said she would come back in the afternoon, so it seems his adventure to Casseroya Lake will have to be postponed by a day.

Cyclizar was off chasing a butterfly again, it would still take some time before Metapod evolves into a Butterfree, Smoliv was sunbathing on the windowsill, and Alcremie was resting in its little nook.

Taking advantage of the free time, D took Koraidon to the cave behind the wooden house.

As soon as they entered, they felt a refreshing coolness.

In the corner of the cave were rows of mushroom cultivation dishes, and after just one night, mushrooms had already started growing on them.

D stepped forward to observe and used his golden finger to check the information on these mushrooms.

[Small Mushroom: A rare small mushroom that grows in forests and mountains, highly popular among enthusiasts and can sell for a great price to them. Used in cooking and as an exchange item in games. Highly valuable.]

Wow? Am I that lucky?

D felt a little surprised.

Because if he remembered correctly, this small mushroom was a currency exchange item in the game, selling for 500 League coins each, much more valuable than eggs.

Apart from small mushrooms, other currency exchange items include big mushrooms and fragrant mushrooms.

A big mushroom can sell for 5000 League coins, and a fragrant mushroom can sell for a high price of 15000.

It's said to be a very rare mushroom, making the entire surrounding area fragrant.

More often used in making expensive perfumes to sell to high society ladies than in cooking.

Unfortunately, there were no traces of them in the mushroom cultivation dishes, except for two small mushrooms and the rest were ordinary mushrooms.

Nonetheless, D was quite happy. Mushrooms grow very fast, which means in a few days, he could sell these mushrooms, exchange them for 1000 League coins cash, and store them in his little treasure chest.

After checking the growth progress of the mushrooms, D saw that it was still early and decided to take Koraidon on a trip to the back mountain.

Compared to last time, nothing much had changed in the back mountain.

The area was mostly inhabited by small and gentle Bug Type Pokemon and Grass Type Pokemon, which, upon seeing Koraidon, fled deeper into the forest.

D didn't give chase and instead took Koraidon along the river passing through the ranch.

He wanted to see if he could encounter some Water Type Pokemon and hire them to help water the fields.

Unfortunately, the river was home to mostly Magikarp and tiny Wishiwashi.

This left D speechless, "Looks like I'll have to find Water Type Pokemon at Casseroya Lake."

Unknowingly, it was already noon.

D and Koraidon had found nothing, so they glanced at the sun and decided to return to the ranch.

Before leaving, Koraidon crouched down and called back to him: "Grrr!"

D thought it wanted to give him a ride back, so he flipped himself on.

But once he was settled, Koraidon beneath him suddenly transformed.

Its long whiskers changed into wings, its hind legs powered up fiercely, and with a leap, it soared into the air, flying steadily towards the ranch.

"Holy crap!"

D was startled by Koraidon's actions. As the ground below gradually shrank, D, afraid of falling, quickly hugged Koraidon's neck tightly and exclaimed, "You can fly?!"

Koraidon seemed very happy and shouted loudly: "Grrr!!"

D then realized that Koraidon had not yet experienced any territory battles with the Koraidon in the game and was uninjured—it could fly from the very start!

Once the initial shock passed, D's emotions slowly calmed down. It was his first time experiencing riding a Pokemon in flight, and he couldn't help but look around in wonder.

Koraidon's flight altitude wasn't exaggerated, not up to the clouds, but from its back, one could clearly see the scenery below.

The vast, endless forest, the tall back mountain surrounded by green trees, the distant ranch, Porto Marinada Town, and even the endless Paldea Sea.

Feeling the rhythm of the wind, D's heart was filled with joy.

This feeling was simply amazing!

————

At three o'clock in the afternoon, Professor Sada and Arven finally returned here.

She instructed a man wearing a blue duckbill cap and work clothes: "This is the place."

D was slightly puzzled and couldn't help but ask: "What's this?"

Professor Sada smiled gently and said: "This is the latest model of the video phone, funded by the lab for your installation. With it, you can contact me anytime. Consider it a gift for taking care of Koraidon for us!"

D: "..."

He quickly realized Professor Sada's intention—she was probably hoping to learn more about the lifestyle habits of the Koraidon beside him through him.

Sada earnestly expressed her gratitude to D: "Thank you so much for agreeing to help us research Koraidon's ecological habits. You must really like Pokemon, right?"

D nodded, his gaze falling on Cyclizar and Koraidon not far away, and smiled: "They are smart, sensible, innocent, and pure. I think nobody would dislike them, right?"

Hearing this, Professor Sada also smiled slightly, said no more, and turned to leave with Arven. There was still work waiting for her in Zone Zero,

As D watched those two figures, one large and one small, Professor Sada's fate flashed in his mind.

She and her husband, Professor Turo, would one day die in the future due to an experimental accident that caused an explosion.

---

---

---

---

# 32: Chapter 32: Familiarity (Double Chapter)

Watching Professor Sada and little Arven leave, D returned indoors. Seeing the familiar visual phone, he was reminded of the scene in the anime where Ash calls Professor Oak from the Pokémon Center.

The arrival of Professor Sada also made him aware of the timeline he was currently in.

Ten years before the Scarlet and Violet storyline, at this time, Ash might not have started his journey, or perhaps he had just set out.

If that's the case, then things could get interesting.

Because it means that Mewtwo has not yet been created, Lugia has not yet exploded, Groudon and Kyogre have not awakened, and Team Galactic has not controlled the Space-Time Gods...

Of course, all of this presupposes the existence of Ash in this world.

But whether he's here or not doesn't seem to concern him.

After all, he's just an ordinary rancher.

Life must go on.

Today he hasn't made any income, and things can't continue like this.

D sighed and lifted an iron bucket, riding Koraidon upstream towards the river.

When he returned, the iron bucket was now brimming with fish.

At five in the evening, Zack arrived punctually with his truck to collect the goods.

As the temperature gradually warmed up, Zack had shed his thick jacket, working in just a t-shirt.

Looking at the renewed ranch, Zack remarked, "Wow, it's really changing every day!"

He glanced at the seedlings in the field and said to D, "In half a month, the crops should be ready."

D: "Yeah!"

"Before you plant your next batch of crops, you might want to buy some fertilizer in town. The higher the quality of your crops, the better price they'll fetch!" Zack casually suggested, then asked, "Any harvest today?"

D handed him the bucket of fish.

Zack counted them, then took out his ledger to calculate, finally pulling out a wad of cash from his bag and handing it over with a smile, "A total of 2,620 League currency, please take care!"

D reached out to take it.

Zack loaded the goods onto the truck, then got into the driver's seat. As he reversed, he waved to D, "See you tomorrow!"

D also replied, "See you tomorrow!"

After seeing Zack off, the sky gradually darkened.

It was getting late, so D returned to the house to prepare dinner.

Alcremie jumped down from the cupboard, scampering to the desk to play with Smoliv.

D looked at them and thought about how these two little fellows had been staying indoors and hardly went outside. He said to them, "Don't you feel cooped up staying inside all the time? You should go out occasionally for a walk!"

"Maa maa..."

"Smol..."

Upon hearing this, Alcremie and Smoliv looked outside, seemingly a bit afraid of the unfamiliar world.

D understood.

Before coming here, Alcremie and Smoliv lived out in the fields of Happy Ranch. They grew up there and naturally weren't afraid.

But now, they've come to a completely unfamiliar ranch, and for them, only this small wooden house and D could provide them a sense of security.

So, for the past two days, they've been playing indoors and haven't ventured outside.

"This won't do!"

D thought for a moment, deciding to take them for a walk around the ranch after dinner so they could get used to the environment.

For today's dinner, D prepared a meal consisting of rich creamy mushroom soup, Chinese-style meat buns, peppered grilled meat patties, plain boiled eggs, and concluded with five cups of milk infused with different fruit juices.

A sumptuous dinner was completed.

All the Pokémon ate happily.

Smoliv and Alcremie ate politely, chewing slowly and savoring each bite.

In contrast, Koraidon and Cyclizar ate with wild abandon, their paws and mouths covered in oil.

With a serious expression, D reminded them, "Remember to wash your face and paws at the outdoor faucet after dinner!"

Last time, when he went to tidy up the dishes, he returned to find the sheets and floor covered in oil stains they had left behind.

"Gah-sii..."

"Gah-aww..."

Koraidon and Cyclizar obediently nodded in agreement.

They got it!

Quickly, dinner time was over.

D prepared to do the dishes, while Koraidon and Cyclizar went to the outdoor faucet to clean their mouths and paws.

Seeing that they could take care of themselves, D nodded with satisfaction and confidently turned to the kitchen to clean up.

Smoliv and Alcremie, seeing the two big guys, exchanged a glance and checked each other's mouths and little hands.

"Maa maa!" We should wash up too, get ourselves clean!

"Smol..." Smoliv looked fearfully at Koraidon and Cyclizar.

Compared to her, Alcremie's courage was slightly bigger.

In these two days together, she noticed that the two big Pokémon showed no hostility towards them.

This made Alcremie, who was initially a bit scared, less fearful.

Holding Smoliv's hand, she jumped down from the chair, sneaking to the door, peeking at Koraidon and Cyclizar.

Koraidon had switched from its four-legged sprinting form to its upright battle form.

In this form, it exuded an unparalleled wild aura, combined with its towering height of nearly two meters and muscular physique, it presented a formidable sense of intimidation.

---

It stood tall, looking fierce, spraying water with its big claws from a faucet onto the shorter Cyclizar next to it.

"Ma... Ma..." So scary...

Alcremie hesitated a little.

Seeing this, Smoliv innocently asked, "Fu?" Didn't you just say you weren't afraid of that big guy?

"Ma!" I did not!

To not lose face in front of its companions, Alcremie took a deep breath and stepped forward bravely.

At this moment, Koraidon noticed them.

It turned its head, its golden pupils narrowing slightly, staring intently at the small Alcremie and Smoliv.

Alcremie felt as if it was being targeted by the most terrifying predator in the world, and its small body instantly petrified on the spot.

Fortunately, Koraidon soon looked away, calling out to them: "Agaa-sii!"

"Fu?" Smoliv trembled as it asked, is it calling us?

"Ma... Ma..." It seems so... what do we do?

Alcremie also felt a bit panicked.

Seeing them like that, Koraidon felt a bit speechless.

It toned down its voice: "Agaa-sii..."

After driving Cyclizar away, Koraidon approached and picked up Alcremie and Smoliv, then helped them water with the faucet.

After a while, Smoliv and Alcremie's bodies, stiff with fear, finally relaxed.

They belatedly realized that besides looking intimidating, this big guy might not actually harm them?

By the time D finished his bath and returned, there was no sign of Alcremie and Smoliv inside the house.

Puzzled, he heard the sound of water outside, went out the door, and saw Koraidon playing with them in the water.

Meanwhile, Cyclizar stood foolishly on the side, just watching, not coming forward.

It seemed not to like getting too close to Alcremie.

"Is it because of type matchups?" D pondered.

After all, the Fairy-type Alcremie has a double advantage over the Dragon-type Cyclizar.

However, the Dragon-type Koraidon didn't seem to reject Alcremie at all.

Maybe it's because Koraidon is strong enough to ignore the type influence from Alcremie? D thought.

Seeing him come over, the Pokemon also stopped.

D returned to the house and took a towel to dry Alcremie and Smoliv.

He curiously asked, "How does it feel?"

Alcremie's voice sounded very happy: "Ma!"

Smoliv also seemed very pleased: "Fu~"

It seemed they really liked Koraidon!

Once they were dry, D walked around the pasture with them on either side, introducing them to the pasture.

Koraidon and Cyclizar followed behind.

The sky had not completely darkened yet, a sliver of sunset lingering on the horizon, enveloping the whole land in a gentle twilight.

The hens, after a day of grazing, returned to the Chicken House, settling into hay bales to close their eyes and rest.

The crops in the fields were growing well, with the fastest-growing turnips already sprouting several large leaves, and the buried fruits likely taking shape.

D walked with the Pokemon on the grass, pointing to the fence surrounding the pasture, and said to Alcremie and Smoliv:

"The places enclosed by those fences are our pasture, it's very safe, no bad guys will hurt you."

Smoliv and Alcremie curiously looked around.

D smiled and said, "The grasslands here have lots of wildflowers, and during the day many butterflies come fluttering about."

The grass in this world grew vigorously, and with a little trimming, it turned into a tidy lawn, looking very aesthetic.

Because of this, D only chopped down excess trees, not cutting down the grass with a scythe.

He planned to take a couple of days to sort out the stone materials in the utility room and lay a stone path connecting the Chicken House and the wooden house on the pasture.

Seeing Alcremie and Smoliv's curious expressions, D said to them:

"You can come tomorrow during the day to see, there are lots of butterflies and flowers."

"Fu~"

"Ma!"

The two Pokemon began to look forward to it.

After D's introduction, they had a preliminary impression of the pasture — the place surrounded by fences is safe.

They were not as afraid of the world outside anymore.

After walking around the pasture, the last glow of the setting sun disappeared, and only until the sun was no longer visible did D end the walk.

He brought the Pokemon back into the house.

Alcremie went to the cabinet, and Smoliv returned to the flower pot, sitting at the edge, dangling its little feet and yawning, looking sleepy.

"Go to sleep!" D said with a smile.

"Fu~"

Smoliv responded, lying back into the pot, resting on the soft soil with peace of mind, and soon began to snore.

D turned around, looking at Koraidon and Cyclizar by the bed, then sat on the bed to tidy things up.

He planned to set out to Casseroya Lake the next morning because he wasn't sure if he could rush back by noon, so D intended to make a few nutritional sandwiches and Berry Juice for Cyclizar and the others.

This time over there, besides finding new fishing spots, D also planned to hire Water-type Pokemon skilled in Water Gun and Rain Dance to work there.

At the same time, he needed to bring some food because capturing Pokemon is quite a skillful task.

---

Based on years of watching anime, I know that to capture a wild Pokemon, besides battling, you can also use delicious food or form emotional bonds.

After all, Orges never submit unless there's a promise of food and housing.

Pokemon are even simpler than Orges; aside from those that can only be caught through battle, most will follow you as long as you give them some food.

So, D prepared several delicious dishes, catering to Pokemon who enjoy Berries, those who like meat, and those with sweet, spicy, or sour preferences.

These dishes also needed to be easy to carry and store.

After working overtime to finish them, D packed them into his backpack. As he was unzipping it, he suddenly saw a few Poke Balls at the bottom.

Looking at them, D suddenly remembered something.

Professor Sada didn't give him Koraidon's Poke Ball!

No, to be precise, Koraidon had already destroyed its Poke Ball!

Images of Koraidon using Dragon Claw to smack away the Poke Ball flashed through D's mind.

So here's the question: does the mark left by the Poke Ball on a Pokemon still exist after it's destroyed?

After contemplating for a moment, D picked up a Poke Ball and tried using it on Koraidon.

But several seconds passed with no reaction from the Poke Ball.

"Looks like the mark is still there," D thought.

He casually tossed the Poke Ball back into the backpack, but as he zipped it up, he suddenly thought of something else.

He reopened the backpack, took out a red-and-white ball, and approached Cyclizar.

"Ga-ow?"

Cyclizar quickly recognized the Poke Ball and realized that its trainer was finally ready to capture it.

Cyclizar's face lit up with joy as it eagerly leaned its head forward. The moment it touched the button in the center of the Poke Ball, its body turned into a red light and was absorbed into the ball.

The ball then gently wobbled twice before coming to a stop.

This signified a successful capture.

"Is this what it feels like to capture a Pokemon?"

D weighted the Poke Ball in his hand, holding it softly, and then made a pitching motion.

He saw trainers in anime throw Poke Balls like this.

However, fearing the Poke Ball might hit the wall and break, D didn't really throw it but pressed the button while holding it instead.

Immediately, the Poke Ball opened automatically, and a beam of light shot out, revealing Cyclizar's figure on the wooden floor.

"How do you feel?" D asked.

Cyclizar looked very excited: "Ga-ow!"

Judging by its reaction, going inside the Poke Ball didn't seem so bad after all.

However, D believed that there were differences among Poke Balls.

The regular red-and-white ball is the most basic Poke Ball. Above it are the Ultra Ball, Great Ball, Luxury Ball, and so on.

The further up you go, the better the Poke Ball's performance and living environment.

The internal space of the Luxury Ball, which can make Pokemon feel luxurious and comfortable, was personally confirmed by Meowth from Team Rocket.

Well, for now, we'll just make do. Once I make a fortune, I'll get Cyclizar a Luxury Ball to live in.

As for now, it's late, and it's time to get to bed!

D stretched, took off his clothes, and dove onto the bed.

He didn't notice Koraidon's gaze toward the Poke Ball at all.

"Ga-sii?"

---

---

---

# 33: Chapter 33: The Pokemon of Casseroya Lake

Early the next morning, after feeding the chickens and watering the plants, D slung on his backpack, standing in the pasture to bid farewell to Cyclizar, Alcremie, and Smoliv.

"I've placed the food in the cabinet. The upper layer has the pressed Berry Juice and MooMoo Milk, sandwiches and smoked sausages are below. I might not make it back by noon, so if you're hungry, help yourselves," D said.

Apart from its ride form, Cyclizar also has a bipedal combat form.

In this form, Cyclizar has hands and feet and can accomplish tasks just as well as humans, making it a loyal and reliable partner for people in the Paldea Region.

Leaving the farm to it, D felt very assured.

"Grrr…"

Cyclizar, hearing this, first nodded its head, then looked towards Koraidon.

Although Cyclizar wasn't very bright, at this moment, it slowly realized that whatever it could do, Koraidon could do too, and what it couldn't do, Koraidon could do even better.

This made Cyclizar, whose life goal was to carry its owner, feel a little less needed.

It couldn't help but lower its head in a dejected manner.

"Grrr…"

Seeing Cyclizar like this, D patted its shoulder comfortingly and said, "Alright, don't be sad. There are many dangerous Pokemon inhabiting near Casseroya Lake, and Koraidon's flying capability helps me avoid them."

D paused, then seriously said, "Besides this, I have something even more important to entrust to you."

"Grrr?" Something more important?

Cyclizar quickly raised its head to look over.

D: "That is to take good care of Alcremie and Smoliv, protect our home, and don't let wild Pokemon come in and eat our crops and chicks. Can you do that?"

Cyclizar suddenly felt a strong sense of responsibility come over it. It nodded earnestly: "Grrr!"

D chuckled, "Very good."

He then turned to look at Smoliv and Alcremie, and said to them, "See you later!"

Alcremie and Smoliv stood beside Cyclizar, one on the left and one on the right, waving their small hands and swaying their bodies as they said goodbye.

"Smol~"

"Alcre~"

D mounted onto Koraidon's back.

Koraidon leaped with its hind legs, taking him into the high sky.

The farm below gradually grew smaller, and Cyclizar's silhouette turned into a small black dot, eventually disappearing.

Gazing at the vast Paldea Land below, D surveyed his surroundings, and upon confirming his direction, he said to Koraidon, "Let's go!"

"Krrr!"

Koraidon responded, diving toward their destination.

In the pasture, the remaining one large, two small figures stood on the grassland, wide-eyed.

Today, Cyclizar unusually refrained from chasing butterflies.

It felt that being entrusted with such an important task by D meant it had to earnestly fulfill its duties.

So, Cyclizar went to the wooden cabin's eaves, lying on the floor, silently watching the frolicsome Smoliv and Alcremie on the nearby lawn, guarding this place.

Above its head was Metapod, diligently accumulating nutrition, waiting to evolve.

The gentle spring breeze lightly caressed Cyclizar, causing it to grow slightly with the wind.

————

Koraidon's flying speed was remarkably fast, reaching the Casseroya Lake area in less than an hour.

This area, located in the northwest of the Paldea Region, is vast, resembling a cauldron, with mist shrouding the lake surface where islands of various sizes float, resembling boiling dumplings.

From the air, Casseroya Lake truly resembles a cauldron, and the islands appear like ingredients floating on the water surface.

Looking eastward, one can see the great waterfall cascading down from Glaseado Mountain.

The waterfall's flow follows the river into the lake, converging into Casseroya Lake, then flowing into the Paldea Sea to the west.

Wisps of white fog drift over the lake, giving the appearance of a celestial realm, veiling one's sight, making it impossible to see its end at a glance.

Therefore, the Paldea authorities have built lookout towers in three different directions around Casseroya Lake, enabling travelers and Trainers to observe the lake's ecological environment.

Such abundant water resources have nurtured a complete Pokemon ecosystem.

Countless wild Pokemon inhabit this region.

Deerling drinking water in the lake's woodland, Tropius feeding under the weighty fruit trees, Slowpoke basking in the sun idly floating on the water, the ferocious and menacing Gyarados prowling the waters.

And Tatsugiri camouflaged like sushi, pretending to be weak to lure prey closer.

Beneath the Tatsugiri, a corpulent, catfish-type Pokemon, Dondozo, lay waiting with its mouth wide open.

D could guarantee that once prey took the bait and dove down to catch Tatsugiri, Dondozo would leap from the water, swallowing both the prey and Tatsugiri whole.

Of course, Dondozo wouldn't eat Tatsugiri.

Because, to Dondozo, Tatsugiri was a great hunting partner.

Though Dondozo was a glutton, it wasn't very adept at hunting and wasn't very smart.

Thus, it would team up with Tatsugiri to catch prey, regarding it as a leader, where Tatsugiri would reside in Dondozo's mouth, protecting itself from external threats.

"Tsk, what a marvelous Pokemon ecosystem."

D withdrew his gaze from Tatsugiri, looking around to find the Pokemon he desired.

Yes, he had come to catch a wild Dragonite, or the unevolved Dratini and Dragonair.

First, because this type of Pokemon has a cute appearance; second, because they can use Rain Dance, essentially achieving a dual purpose.

Rain Dance could water the plants or help deliver mail.

In this era, the internet was not yet highly developed, and life was quite slow-paced, thus more people still used somewhat outdated methods of communication—sending letters.

Unfortunately, despite riding Koraidon over the lake for half a day, D had not spotted even a trace of Dragonite.

"Strange, is the distribution of Pokemon in reality different from the game?"

D muttered in thought.

If he remembered correctly, Dragonite could be found around Casseroya Lake in the game.

Seeing that he couldn't find Dragonite, D shifted his attention to other Pokemon.

Tropius, Skiddo, and Gogoat…

The neck of Tropius could produce fruit, yielding twice a year, sweet and delicious.

It's said that in the southern lands, children would snack on the fruits growing from Tropius's neck, highly favored by kids.

Because of this, many farms raising Tropius are found in warmer areas.

Skiddo and Gogoat can produce goat milk, though not as famous as Miltank's MooMoo Milk, they are an excellent source of milk.

To D's great surprise, on a nearby hillside by the lake, he discovered the presence of Nacli and Naclstack.

In town, a Nacli is sold for ten thousand. If he could capture a few here, wouldn't that mean he could save tens of thousands to spend on other matters?

---

---

---

---

# 34: Chapter 34: The Mysterious Pokemon

Last time D went to town, he bought six Poke Balls in total.

After using one on Cyclizar, four remained.

This meant he could bring back five Pokemon to the ranch today.

"One Tropius, two Skiddo and one Gogoat, plus two Nacli, just enough! Oh, no, I'm still missing a Pokemon that knows Rain Dance and Water Gun."

D silently calculated in his mind.

Of course, if these Pokemon didn't want to be captured, he wouldn't force them.

The key was mutual consent because even if he captured them forcefully, they might not cooperate with his work back at the ranch.

So, the willingness of the Pokemon was also a very important factor.

D circled around the area, observing the wildlife and the types and ecology of the wild Pokemon from above.

He wasn't in a rush to capture them right away; a qualified rancher must have enough patience.

After all, haste makes waste.

Just then, D suddenly heard someone calling out.

He looked in the direction of the sound and saw a young man with a backpack and binoculars energetically calling out to him from a nearby watchtower.

"Hey there, fella! Hey! Over here!"

D was slightly startled.

Was he being called?

He pondered briefly and said to Koraidon below him, "Let's go over there and take a look!"

"Gha-szz—"

Koraidon responded and turned to fly towards the watchtower standing by the lakeside.

When they got closer, the man could finally see Koraidon's appearance clearly.

His attention was instantly captured, and his surprised gaze carried a hint of curiosity: "What kind of Pokemon is this?"

Koraidon glanced at him sideways: "Ah-sszz?"

D introduced from the side, "It's called Koraidon."

"Koraidon?" Upon hearing the name, the man hurriedly picked up a book from the ground, quickly flipped through it, and searched for something on the pages.

Soon, his eyes widened involuntarily.

A Pokemon not in the illustrated book!

Doesn't that mean it's an undiscovered Pokemon!

"It's astonishing!" The man circled around Koraidon, inspecting closely.

Koraidon looked at him disdainfully.

Seeing the man in a daze, D speechlessly said, "Did you call me for something?"
