The man snapped back to reality and quickly put away the illustrated book, introducing himself:

"I'm Yousuke, from the faraway Galar region, and I'm currently traveling around Paldea to research Pokemon ecology!"

His face showed a slightly awkward expression: "I just saw you riding it around the sky, so... I'm really sorry!"

"But speaking of which, the Pokemon ecology here in Paldea is truly fascinating! I've been staying at this watchtower for half a month now, and I've discovered up to forty-eight different species of Pokemon!"

Saying this, the man squatted down and took out a sketchbook from his casually placed satchel: "Look, these are the Pokemon I've illustrated! It records their ecological habits and areas of appearance in detail!"

Seeing this scene, D felt a stir in his heart.

The range of Casseroya Lake was vast, and he wasn't familiar with the Pokemon appearance times there. Could he get more information on rare and seldom-seen Pokemon from this person?

With this thought, D reached out and took the sketchbook from him, casually flipping through a page where he saw a hand-drawn depiction of a Pokemon from the Galar region.

It was an Urshifu.

It had a tall stature, with a benevolent smile on its face.

Beside it stood a slightly immature, short-haired boy.

Urshifu... it's a Legendary Beast!

D was somewhat astonished.

Yousuke noticed his expression and came over, smiling as he looked at the scene in the drawing, asking, "Can you guess what their relationship is?"

Hmm? D raised an eyebrow slightly, with Koraidon also leaning over to take a look.

"Trainer and Pokemon?" D guessed.

"No!" Yousuke shook his head with excitement in his voice: "Father and son!"

What? D was dumbfounded immediately.

His first reaction was how could that be...

But Yousuke seemed to have guessed what he wanted to say: "Quite surprising, right? But it's true. I encountered them in the Galar region, and they are exactly what made me realize that there are other possibilities between humans and Pokemon."

"At that time, I was really taken aback! It was only later that I found out, the child had been abandoned as a baby and floated down the river to where this Urshifu lived, being adopted by it."

"Humans and Pokemon, father and son, I never thought such a thing existed in this world!"

Yousuke spoke with great enthusiasm.

D thought, what's so special about that? He's even heard of humans marrying Pokemon!

Like Gardevoir and female Machamp, they're regulars.

He didn't take this matter too seriously, instead focusing his attention on the sketchbook in front of him.

Then Yousuke leaned over again and asked, "Can I do a drawing of this Pokemon? As a reward, I can give you my specialty sandwiches that even Pokemon find delicious! Please, I'm begging you!"

D: "..."

He waved his hand casually and sat down cross-legged: "Go ahead, draw!"

The man was overjoyed: "Arigato!"

He took out another half-used sketchbook and a pencil from his backpack, sitting on the ground and focusing intently on sketching Koraidon's figure.

Only then did D notice that the sketchbook in his hands was already filled to the brim.

"Gha-szz..."

Hearing Koraidon's call, D looked up to see it with a stiff body, pitifully gazing at him.

Just as D was about to speak, Yousuke said, "No need to be too tense, just sit in your most relaxed, natural pose!"

So, D gestured for Koraidon to come over, and Koraidon lay down beside him, resting its big head on D's lap.

Seeing this, Yousuke's eyes lit up instantly: "Good, very good, just like that! Perfect posture! This Pokemon really trusts you!"

D scratched Koraidon's chin with a smile and then flipped through the sketchbook in his hand, checking the Pokemon ecology around Casseroya Lake.

Indeed, the Pokemon in this area were more than he imagined.

Besides what he just discovered outside, there were also gentle Azumarill, Swablu with wings as white and soft as clouds, Sliggoo that lived in damp caves, and the pink loli Tinkaton with a hammer...

The book unfolded from Yousuke's perspective, and aside from the Pokemon he knew, there were some shrouded in mist, revealing only a vague outline.

Next to the shadows was a question mark, suggesting that even Yousuke hadn't seen these rare Pokemon.

Some of the shadows D couldn't identify either, but when he flipped to a certain page, he suddenly saw a familiar silhouette.

It seemed to be a cliff, shrouded at the top in mist, with the shadow of a Pokemon within it.

The Pokemon had a plump body, looking very round.

A question mark was also drawn beside it.

Below the question mark was a description.

[Unknown Pokemon, appears promptly at seven every evening on the cliff, sits there still until five the next morning, and then flies back to the sea. Unfortunately, the cliff is too high for me to reach, and I can only see a blurry shadow due to the mist.]

---

---

---

---

# 35: Chapter 35: Choosing a Target

Is that... a Dragonite?

As he gazed at the silhouette, D's mind immediately conjured up the image of Dragonite's chubby and round body.

He wanted to ask Yousuke about this particular Pokemon, but as he looked up, he saw Yousuke completely absorbed and focused on his drawing.

D thought it over and decided not to disturb him for now.

He continued flipping through the picture book in his hand.

However, since his thoughts were entirely captivated by the Dragonite, D showed little interest in the other Pokemon like Sudowoodo, Slaking, and Amoonguss that came afterward.

After about an hour, the man finally put down his pen.

His face lit up with surprise as he looked at his artwork, "Perfectly done! Take a look!"

Saying this, he handed the paper with the drawing of Koraidon to D.

On the paper, Koraidon lay peacefully beside D, resting its big head on his leg.

Meanwhile, D was engrossed in the book in his hand, creating a scene that seemed natural and warm.

"Amazing!" D gave him a thumbs up and asked, "Have you been learning to draw for a long time?"

Yousuke scratched his head, "Not really; my parents signed me up for art classes, but after three months, I ran off to travel. I'm still honing my skills along the way."

D didn't know much about this area.

Yousuke, satisfied, put away his pen, closed the picture book, and, with keen interest, asked,

"Speaking of which, are you also a Trainer traveling around?"

"No." D shook his head, "I'm a Rancher, currently managing a ranch. If you need eggs, you can buy them at D Ranch, east of Porto Marinada Town."

As he answered, D didn't forget to promote the goods from his ranch.

Yousuke's eyes widened upon hearing this, "A Rancher?"

"Yep!"

"Alright." Yousuke opened his mouth to speak, "I originally thought you were a Trainer here to catch Pokemon in Casseroya Lake. There are many Pokemon species living around here, and I've often seen Trainers coming to catch Pokemon these past few days."

Speaking of wild Pokemon, D flipped open the page with the drawing of what seemed to be a Dragonite in front of Yousuke, "Is this Pokemon still around here?"

"You mean that one? It's still here!" Yousuke said excitedly, "Are you interested in it too?"

D didn't deny it, "If it's convenient, could you tell me about it?"

"Of course!" Yousuke began recalling as he narrated, "I discovered it about a week ago."

"At the time, I was on the Lake Island on the west side of Casseroya Lake, investigating a Pokemon not found in the Galar region that mimicked the appearance of sushi."

"Out of curiosity, I wanted to observe it closely, but for some reason, I was suddenly attacked by a Dondozo."

"Panicked, I fell into the lake and was chased by a frenzy of Veluza. I was carried by the current to the foot of a cliff by the lake, where I looked up and saw this Pokemon sitting on the cliff watching the moon."

"However, in the evening, Casseroya Lake gets foggy, and I couldn't clearly see what it looked like, only a blurry shadow."

"That kind of Pokemon doesn't exist in the Galar region; I was drawn to it, so I started observing it and found that it arrives around 7 PM every night."

"It stays there until 5 AM the next day, then it flies back to the sea, very punctually, regardless of the weather!"

"Unfortunately, I don't have a flying Pokemon, so I can't get to such a high place."

Yousuke showed a face full of regret, but suddenly recalled that there was a flying Pokemon right in front of him!

His eyes lit up at once, "Can you take me to see it? I just want to have a look at it!"

"I can, but..." D hesitated.

The mysterious Pokemon would only appear at 7 PM, but he couldn't stay here that long since he had to return to attend to ranch work and prepare dinner for a few Pokemon.

After a moment's thought, D asked, "It stays here all night and won't leave in between, right?"

Yousuke nodded, "That's right."

D: "How about this, I'll come back after 7 PM."

He explained what work awaited him back at the ranch.

Yousuke immediately nodded, "No problem, I'll wait for you guys here!"

After making the arrangements, D rode Koraidon away from the watchtower.

He looked at the five Poke Balls in his hand, contemplating which Pokemon to take back to the ranch.

Firstly, the ranch currently needed Nacli the most.

Because both its salt production and value are very high.

According to the Pasture Encyclopedia given by Mayor Thomas, Nacli, as long as it moves, will rub against the ground to produce salt grains.

Therefore, people build special Salt Houses for them, laying a specially designed film on the floor to collect the salt.

An average Nacli can produce a small bag of about 500 grams of rock salt every five days.

Such a small bag of rock salt sells for 3000 league coins on the market.

The high price is due to the rock salt being rich in minerals, which are highly beneficial when consumed regularly by humans and Pokemon.

Moreover, this rock salt can be directly used on Pokemon to very quickly heal their wounds.

After deducting the feeding costs, one can earn over 2500 league coins every five days from a single Nacli, amounting to fifteen thousand a month, and owning ten would yield one hundred and fifty thousand a month.

And when they evolve into Naclstack and Garganacl, the salt production will increase even more.

If the Rancher doesn't mistreat Nacli and is willing to spend time playing with them and ensuring their health every day besides feeding, ensuring they don't get sick,

then this Rancher is bound to make a fortune.

With this in mind, D directed Koraidon towards the location where he previously found Nacli.

It was situated on a rocky area on the east side of Casseroya Lake, near Glaseado Mountain.

Behind was a cave that seemed to be the residence of Naclis.

D gave Koraidon's neck a gentle pat, and it let out a cry before understandingly landing in front of the cave.

The few Naclis playing and sunbathing on the rocks sensed foreign aura, causing them to panic and scatter, fleeing back into the cave.

D: "..." Was he that scary?

Although the initial attempt failed, D wasn't discouraged.

He took out his prized delicacies—salted small fish and salted plum.

Worried that suddenly entering the cave might frighten the group of Naclis, D opted not to go in but stood outside, starting to toss small fish and plums inside.

---

---

---

---

# 36: Chapter 36: Fishing for Nacli

If he remembered correctly, Nacli, this kind of Pokemon, prefers a salty taste.

After evolving into Naclstack, they even actively spray salt at their prey, turning them into salted products.

So, for them, specially salted plums and dried fish have great attraction.

At first, the Nacli that fled into the cave had no reaction.

But as more dried fish and plums were thrown in, they couldn't resist, cautiously stepping forward to feast on the plums on the ground.

"Salt salt~" So delicious!

The faces of the group of Nacli revealed expressions of happiness.

But the thrown-in snacks were limited, and there wasn't just one Nacli in the cave, so soon those plums and dried fish were all eaten up.

Just as the group of Nacli began to feel sad, another piece of dried fish suddenly flew in from outside.

The dried fish was tossed just right, landing not far from their position.

A few Nacli blinked, ultimately unable to resist the temptation, moved forward, picked up, and ate that piece of dried fish.

Then, another piece of dried fish was tossed a little farther up ahead.

The previous dried fish wasn't dangerous, so this one must be safe too!

After the previous attempt, this group of Nacli reached this conclusion.

So they happily walked and ate, slowly lured out of the cave.

By the time they realized, they were unknowingly in front of the two big guys from earlier.

"Salt salt!"

The Nacli were panicked and shivered.

If only they hadn't been so greedy, boo-hoo!

D: "..."

He glanced over simply, there were exactly five Nacli in front of him.

Five, just right, and he had five Poke Balls on hand. If he could take these five away...

D cleared his throat softly, then opened his backpack, took out a plum, and handed it in front of the foremost Nacli.

"Here!"

The Nacli froze in place immediately.

Then he distributed food to the other four Nacli.

Except for one that was the smallest and looked somewhat malnourished, the remaining four were not as afraid of him as before.

"Eat, eat up!"

D tried to release his goodwill, while Koraidon beside him transformed into a gentle speeding form, restraining its aura.

Several Nacli hesitated a bit, then cautiously started eating.

D glanced at the smallest-looking Nacli; its reaction was slightly sluggish, and it took a while before it began to eat.

Patiently waiting for them to finish eating, D revealed his true intention with a hint of temptation in his words: "Is it delicious?"

"Salt salt!"

"Do you want to eat these foods every day?" he asked.

The Nacli blinked happily: "Salt salt!"

"Then, come with me!" D took out an empty Poke Ball: "Come to my ranch, it's large with plenty of food."

Just when D thought he had them, reluctance was evident in the Nacli's eyes.

"Salt salt..."

"Eh?" Is that not okay?

D fell into thought, and it was then that a voice from behind suddenly emerged.

"D? What are you doing here?"

D turned around to see Yousuke running over, carrying a backpack with a surprised face.

D stood up and didn't hide his intention: "I want to catch these Nacli to take them back to the ranch to live together."

"Nacli?" Following his gaze, Yousuke also saw those Nacli.

He looked around as if searching for something: "That's odd... I remember there was a Garganacl with this group of Nacli? Did you see it?"

"Garganacl?" D shook his head: "No, when I came, I only saw these Nacli. They looked very hungry."

Seeing his confusion, Yousuke explained: "I've been observing the wild Pokemon ecosystem around here these days. I encountered this group of Nacli a few days ago; they hung around a Garganacl."

"That Garganacl seemed to be the leader of their group, normally leading them to find food and wander outside, look!"

As he spoke, Yousuke took out a sketchbook, turning to a page that clearly recorded the scene of the Nacli surrounding a Garganacl in this area.

Garganacl's big hand held a few berries, feeding the Nacli.

"I didn't see it." D shook his head.

Yousuke stroked his chin, pondering for a moment, "That's strange! Garganacl wouldn't leave Nacli alone, and there aren't any Pokemon around here capable of defeating it, so there seems to be only one possibility left."

D also thought of this at the moment, and the two of them spoke in unison: "Captured by a Trainer?"

If so, that Trainer was too irresponsible, wasn't he?

They captured the leader and left behind a group of helpless little ones.

If he were in their shoes, he would certainly take everything with him, leaving nothing behind.

"This won't do!" Yousuke frowned: "Without Garganacl, another Pokemon will soon come to compete for this territory."

And the weak Nacli almost have no combat ability...

Hearing this, D's desire to take them back to the ranch grew stronger.

But looking at the group of Nacli, they seemed unwilling to leave, waiting for Garganacl to return.

"D, take them to your ranch!" Yousuke urged.

D: "But they seem unwilling to leave here?"

Yousuke: "Then capture them in the way of a Trainer! Prove your strength through battle!"

Hearing this, D turned to look at Koraidon.

Using Koraidon to battle Nacli felt too brutal...

After considering, he decided to use his own method.

D squatted down, no longer using food to entice, but instead looked at the group of Nacli in front of him and asked: "Are you waiting for Garganacl?"

"Salt salt..."

Nacli lacked hands, feet, and a neck, resembling a salt mushroom in appearance, somewhat reminiscent of Minecraft's art style.

D couldn't determine their answer through actions and language, so he turned to Koraidon.

Koraidon quickly understood his intention and nodded: "Ga-sis!"

This indicated that they were indeed waiting for Garganacl.

Although the possibility that Garganacl was captured was high, D couldn't be entirely sure without seeing it in person.

Therefore, he decided to wait with this group of Nacli for a few more days.

If Garganacl doesn't return after a few days, it means it won't come back.

Hearing his plan, Yousuke seemed a bit helpless: "I really can't help you, but I understand. I'll help you watch over them, so feel free to go back to work at the ranch! But don't forget to take me to see that mysterious Pokemon at seven tonight!"

"Don't worry!"

He had prepared food in advance, so there was no concern for Cyclizar and the others going hungry. D stayed at Casseroya Lake until the afternoon before heading back.

Upon returning to the ranch, even before D landed, he saw an elderly woman with gray hair looking inside from outside the ranch.

D approached and politely asked: "Hello, is there something I can help you with?"

The old lady seemed equally polite, asking: "Are you the Rancher of this place? I'm Boji·Dantes from the town, and I wanted to ask if you have goat milk for sale?"

"My husband is ill and needs fresh goat milk to make dishes for healing. If you have any, I'm willing to purchase at a high price."

This was a treatment method she got from a mysterious culinary master, a healing method called dietary therapy, said to have been passed down from some ancient country, and regular use slowly improves the body.

She had already made numerous trips to the Porto Marinada Market and nearby ranches but hadn't found any selling goat milk.

Just when she was about to give up, she suddenly heard that this ranch had resumed operations.

So, she came over specifically.

Compared to the common MooMoo Milk, goat milk was indeed quite rare.

D looked at Mrs. Dantes and shook his head: "I'm sorry, we currently don't have any at the ranch, but maybe in some time, we will."

This undoubtedly gave the old lady hope, and her eyes immediately lit up.

D thought about buying more Poke Balls from the town later, then capturing Gogoat and Skiddo at Casseroya Lake to obtain goat milk.

He told the elderly lady: "If it's convenient, could you leave me your phone number, and I'll contact you when we have goat milk."

Mrs. Dantes quickly provided her phone number, thanking him repeatedly: "Thank you so much!"

---

---

---

---

# 37: Chapter 37: Gigantic (Double Chapter)

Seven o'clock in the evening.

After preparing dinner for Cyclizar, Alcremie, and Smoliv, D told them he was going out for a while and rode Koraidon towards Casseroya Lake.

The moon was bright tonight, with a faint mist rising over Casseroya Lake, and the soft moonlight spilled onto the lake's surface.

Whenever a Pokemon leaped out of the water, the silvery full moon would shimmer and break with the ripples.

"You finally made it!"

Yousuke, not returning to the watchtower, had simply set up a makeshift campsite by the lake, with a campfire burning brightly in front of the tent, clearly visible in the night.

Beside him stood a Pokemon bearing a shield in its left hand and a sword in its right, with a thick black eyebrow and a calm, composed demeanor.

The group of Nacli were dozing off beside the fire.

D placed the food he had prepared in front of them, and the young Pokemon immediately opened their eyes, full of surprise and delight.

"It's true, they like your dried fish more than my sandwich!" Yousuke scratched his head. "They haven't eaten anything tonight."

His Secret Sandwich held no appeal for the Nacli.

Watching the Nacli begin to eat, D smiled and said, "Considering they're born from layers of rock salt underground, it's no wonder their taste in food differs from humans."

Yousuke agreed, "True."

He glanced at the time and said, "It's already half-past seven. When should we set off?"

D replied, "We can go now."

"Great!" Yousuke seemed very excited. He stood up, took a picture book and a pen from his backpack, and said to the knight-like Pokemon, "Sirfetch'd, I'll leave them in your care!"

"Quack!"

Sirfetch'd saluted, indicating it would surely protect the group of Nacli.

D curiously observed Sirfetch'd and asked, "It evolved from Farfetch'd, didn't it?"

"That's right. Only the Galarian form of Farfetch'd can evolve into Sirfetch'd," Yousuke explained with a smile. "It's a very brave and diligent Pokemon, and its stance in battle is quite like the valiant warriors in movies!"

D nodded, his attention caught by Sirfetch'd's hands holding a leek-like sword and a shield made of leek leaves.

The large leek is a very important item to them, used as a weapon, and is more valuable to Sirfetch'd than anything else.

Yet at the same time, the leek is an excellent culinary ingredient.

Feeling an unfriendly gaze on itself, Sirfetch'd quickly turned its head with a sharp look.

Following D's gaze, Yousuke chuckled and said, "The leek Sirfetch'd carries only grows in the Galar region. Want some? I have a few more in my backpack; I can give you one."

D was also curious about the Galarian leek, knowing it was a delicious seasoning that could enhance the flavor of dishes.

"Then, thank you in advance," D said, turning towards the distant Casseroya Lake, and spoke to Yousuke: "We should set off. Do you still remember where the Pokemon is?"

"Of course!"

"Then hop on!"

D patted Koraidon, which obligingly bent down.

D got on first, and when it was Yousuke's turn, he seemed a bit overwhelmed: "Can I really ride this Pokemon too? It won't get mad, right?"

Hearing this, D looked at Koraidon.

In consideration of D, Koraidon graciously shook its head, "Kreashh!"

Yousuke immediately became excited, "Thank you so much!"

He carefully climbed on.

Once he was settled, Koraidon flew up into the air, above the layer of faint mist.

Yousuke looked around, determined the direction, and then pointed westward, saying, "It's on the west side of Casseroya Lake, near the Paldea Sea. We need to cross Casseroya Lake first."

"Okay," D replied concisely.

Now nighttime, some Pokemon that are hard to find during the day began appearing in this area.

D glanced down, spotting a few Murkrow perched on the branches and some sneaky Misdreavus up to unknown activities.

Suddenly, Yousuke exclaimed, "Oh my god! Slaking is standing up!"

D, following his gaze, saw a Slaking pacing back and forth on a grass patch below.

It seemed to have sensed an unfamiliar presence, as the Slaking sharply raised its head and looked over.

"It's noticed us!" Yousuke was somewhat shocked.

Everyone knows that Slaking is very lazy, lying on the ground motionless, living their life lying down, only eating grass that grows within reach.

Only when the surrounding grass is eaten up will it reluctantly move to another place—even Trainers causing trouble in front of it won't get a reaction.

But now, the Slaking wasn't lying down; instead, it was energetically pacing around, with a keen expression, constantly observing its surroundings.

That look didn't match a Slaking at all.

D recalled a line he had heard while playing games—when you encounter a wandering Slaking, it's definitely not a Slaking, but a Ditto or a Zoroark transformed.

---

So... is it a Ditto or a Zoroark?

Judging from its sharp, alert movements, D thought there was an 80% chance it was a Zoroark.

Zoroark, the fuzzy Demon Fox Pokemon, the Phantom Ruler.

D's heart stirred.

Koraidon didn't stop; the three of them quickly left the area, and that Slaking also disappeared from their sight.

D looked back begrudgingly at the scene behind him.

For a moment, he even considered abandoning the search for that mysterious Pokemon, going down to verify whether it was a Slaking or a Zoroark.

Let it go, you can't pick up sesame seeds and lose the watermelon.

There will be bread, and there will be milk.

Casseroya Lake is right here; he can come back to explore any time.

D cleared his thoughts; at this point, Koraidon crossed the vast Casseroya Lake to the other side.

Behind him, Yousuke excitedly looked at the nearby cliff and exclaimed, "It's over there!"

D followed the direction of his gaze and saw a familiar Pokemon at the top of a steep cliff.

The Pokemon had a rounded body, with a delicate dragon horn and whiskers on its head, and small dragon wings on its back.

It was sitting dazed on the grass at the cliff top, looking melancholically at the moon in the sky, occasionally letting out a troubled sigh.

It really was a Dragonite!

D's heart surged with excitement.

Dragonite, a pseudo-legendary Pokemon from the Kanto Region, powerful and extremely rare, the signature Pokemon of the renowned Dragon Elite, Lance.

It did not disappoint him!

"That Pokemon is?!"

Upon seeing the Dragonite clearly, Yousuke couldn't help but show a shocked expression.

D slowly uttered its name, "Dragonite."

Koraidon hovered in mid-air and didn't immediately land.

The Dragonite either didn't notice them or noticed them but didn't care.

After observing from above for a moment, D had Koraidon land behind the Dragonite.

Yousuke immediately took out a pen and sketchbook and captured the scene of the "Dragonite Gazing at the Moon."

Hearing sounds from behind, Dragonite turned around curiously. Upon seeing the suddenly appearing people and Pokemon, it was startled, revealing a surprised expression.

"Awoo?!" When…did you get here?!

So cute! D's gaze was fixed on this Dragonite.

The Dragonite in reality was very plump, with a chubby body, and its big belly looked extremely soft; he couldn't help but wonder what it felt like to touch it.

Those chubby thick dragon claws also looked very adorable.

No wonder it's so chubby!

D really wanted to take this Dragonite back to his ranch to raise it.

Thus, he tried to show his good intentions as much as possible.

"Good evening, Dragonite!"

Dragonite blinked its big round eyes without showing hostility, nor did it make any attack moves. It just glanced around, let out a small sigh, then stood up, soared into the sky, and left, flying towards the ocean.

D and Yousuke were left looking at each other.

After a while, D said, "Did we... disturb it?"

Yousuke, looking at Dragonite's back, murmured, "It seemed to be quite troubled."

Does Dragonite, a Pokemon at the top of the ecological system, also have unsolvable troubles?

Yousuke suddenly seemed to recall something: "Shouldn't you have immediately sent out Koraidon to battle and capture it in a Poke Ball?"

"...Wouldn't that be a bit too much?" D politely retorted, "Besides, I'm not a Trainer and don't plan to be one. Even if I captured it, I wouldn't use it to battle. I just want to live alongside it. You understand that feeling, right?"

Yousuke gave an understanding nod, "A collecting hobby? I've seen people like that before, who only capture specific Pokemon for their collection."

This made D think of that crazy collector in Lugia's explosive birth.

He couldn't help but ask, "Not like I imagine, making specimens for collection, right?"

"How is that possible?" Yousuke widened his eyes, "Those guys just have peculiar tastes, not maniacs. I've seen people who only capture fluffy, cute, and small Pokemon, and reportedly that guy sleeps surrounded by them every day."

"Also, there are Trainers who only like muscular Pokemon, like Machamp, Incineroar, Rillaboom, Grimmsnarl, Obstagoon — I think they surely lacked a sense of security when they were young."

D: "..."

He wore an awkward yet polite smile.

What a coincidence, he was one of them too.

Yousuke asked again, "But seriously, aren't you going to chase it?"

D considered briefly and shook his head, "Better not; forcibly approaching might annoy it. If I'm not mistaken, it will come back here. It'll be better to get to know it slowly then."

Moreover, he couldn't stay out all night; otherwise, Cyclizar and the others would worry.

Yousuke agreed, "That's true."

The two returned to their campsite on the east side of Casseroya Lake, where the Nacli were cuddled up asleep by the warm campfire after filling their bellies.

Yousuke lowered his voice and whispered, "Are you heading back?"

D also lowered his voice, "Yeah, come back tomorrow."

---

Besides feeding Nacli, he had something even more important to do.

That was to fish in Casseroya Lake and bring the catch back to the ranch for sale.

If this group of Nacli were to leave with him, the ranch would have five more mouths to feed.

Not only that, he would also need to ask Keri to build a Salt House specifically for the Nacli to reside in, making it easier to collect Rock Salt.

With the little money he had, it wouldn't be enough.

Looking at the marks left by the Nacli moving around on the ground, D felt a twinge of heartache.

There were expenses everywhere, and he had to work hard.

"When you have time in the future, feel free to stop by my ranch, I'll make sure to give you a proper reception then."

D informed the Traveler from the Galar region of the exact location of the ranch.

Yousuke's face lit up with a smile, "Alright, once I'm done with the Pokémon ecological survey here at Casseroya Lake, I'll head over."

He was very curious about what kind of ranch this young man had built.

D smiled and nodded, turned around to say goodbye, and rode Koraidon back to the ranch.

Before setting off, he left the salty provisions in his backpack behind, knowing he needed to make a trip to town in the morning and might not return by noon.

By the time D returned to the ranch, it was already late at night.

Even though it was very late, Cyclizar had not yet gone to sleep; it was lying under the eaves, watching the outside.

Seeing D and Koraidon return, Cyclizar's eyes lit up, and it happily stood up.

D stepped forward to pat its head, knowing that Cyclizar had been diligently watching over the ranch, and said with a smile, "Alright, I'm back, go get some sleep!"

"Gaao~"

Cyclizar went to rest, while D went to the Chicken House to check the feed and water in the feeding troughs and the condition of the chickens.

When he saw that the water trough was nearly empty, he added more water, and only after busying himself did he go to sleep.

Early the next morning, after finishing his work at the ranch, D went to town, found the fishing tackle shop using the roadside sign, and bought a few giant fishing nets from there.

Unfortunately, Metapod had not yet evolved into Butterfree; otherwise, he could make the fishing nets himself.

In the following days, D became busy.

He and Koraidon shuttled between Casseroya Lake, the West Paldea Sea, and the ranch to catch fish, sometimes working until nearly dawn.

It wasn't just him; even Koraidon, this legendary Pokémon, was exhausted.

Though the work was tough, Koraidon didn't utter a word of complaint; instead, it felt happy and fulfilled.

Seeing him running back and forth between Casseroya Lake and the ranch, Yousuke could also sense the hardships of operating the ranch.

Once, when D went to visit Nacli and deliver food, Yousuke couldn't help but say, "You're really pushing yourself too hard! Sometimes you need to relax a bit!"

Covered in sweat, D handed Koraidon a bottle of juice to restore its energy and stamina, and said,

"There's a mountain of tasks waiting for me at the ranch, and a lot of mouths depending on me for food, I have to earn more money!"

"Alright then."

Realizing he couldn't change D's mind, Yousuke turned his attention to the five Nacli in front of him as soon as D and Koraidon left.

"Garganacl was captured by a Human Trainer and won't be coming back."

"You see? All the food these days has been given to you by that person. He really hopes you can follow him back to the ranch to live with him."

"Salt…"

The Nacli visualized D working at Casseroya Lake, taking the time to visit them, and bringing them delicious food.

Even their leader might not treat them so well.

What's more, their leader had been taken away by a human and wouldn't return.

The Nacli showed expressions of gratitude.

The five of them exchanged glances and then decided to follow the human who protected them and brought them food, and leave here to live at his ranch.

Although the Nacli's understanding was still immature, at this moment, they vaguely felt that besides D, no one in the world would treat them so well.

---

---

---

---

# 38: Chapter 38: Effort and Reward

At eleven o'clock deep into the night, D and Koraidon returned to the ranch after a day of work.

Even deep into the night, the ranch remained brightly lit.

Since the day he returned from Casseroya Lake, D began researching the stone path in the ranch.

But without professional machinery, he couldn't grind the stones into plates, so he resorted to using wooden boards to lay a path in the ranch.

These days, they were busy until late at night. For lighting, D made torches using wooden sticks and cloth soaked in oil, placing them on both sides of the wooden path.

Once it got dark, lighting the torches could illuminate quite a large area.

Under the firelight, the entire ranch seemed livelier.

It was so late that when D returned home, the Cyclizar had already fallen asleep under the eaves.

D did not wake it but instead worked with Koraidon to pour the fish from the fishing nets into the small lake in the ranch.

Without a vehicle, their method of fishing was quite cumbersome.

D would spread bait on the lake surface in advance, and after a while, the nearby fish would gather there.

Then it was Koraidon's turn to act; it fished in this waterway while D angled in another, filling the fishing nets before airlifting the full net of fish back to the ranch.

Finally, they dumped the fish into the lake, waiting for Zack to come and collect them the next evening.

As long as Koraidon moved at full speed, it could get the fish to the lake before they suffocated.

Of course, some fish did not survive, and D would often use them to concoct meals as a reward for Koraidon.

For convenience, they had prelaid an oversized fishing net at the lake's bottom.

This way, when Zack came to collect them, he could gather the four corners of the net, catching all the fish in one scoop.

Notably, to make room for the fish, D had placed the few Magikarp he had carelessly tossed there back into the river.

"Good job!"

D used the hem of his shirt to wipe the sweat off his face, smiling brightly at Koraidon.

Koraidon wiped the sweat from its face with its big paw: "Roooo~"

D looked around, noticing it was getting late, and said, "Let's call it a day!"

They returned to the wooden cabin, D waking the Cyclizar to go indoors and sleep, while he headed to the kitchen area to prepare some supper.

The noise woke up Smoliv and Alcremie, who opened their eyes and looked over.

So D had to make two more portions.

The pleasant supper time ended, and Smoliv and Alcremie went back to sleep.

Koraidon was too exhausted today to feel competitive with Cyclizar, returning to its place, yawning and resting its head on its paws to sleep.

While Cyclizar stood by D's side.

"Roar..."

D took off his dirty blue overalls, leaving only a thin white t-shirt.

Seeing Cyclizar's appearance, he reached out to pat its head and said, "Things will get better after this period. Nothing major happened at the ranch today, right?"

Cyclizar, who had been diligently guarding the place recently, shook its head in response, "Roar!"

"That's good." D smiled and said, "You've done a great job; I should thank you properly."

"Roar..."

D patted its shoulder, "Alright, it's late, go sleep. I'll get to bed after I finish recording the accounts!"

Cyclizar obediently went to sleep.

D took out the ledger to record the day's income.

The main source of the ranch's recent significant income came from the fish they caught from Casseroya Lake and the western Paldea Sea.

As for the eggs laid by the hens, D did not sell them, instead making them into steamed, boiled, fried, and scrambled eggs to reward the Pokémon.

After deducting the cost for bait and fishing nets, they earned a total of 43,320 League coins over five days.

Once they sold the fish from the lake tomorrow and added the remaining money, his savings would exceed 50,000 League coins.

Although tiring, looking at the drawer full of money filled D with energy.

The labor day and night had made his body stronger, with more defined muscles and a noticeably tanner skin, making him more and more like a real rancher.

"Just right, I can finally take a good rest for a while!"

D stretched comfortably, leaning back in his chair, contemplating the tasks for the next day.

Now that he had money, he planned to go to the town to find Keri and sort out the Salt House, although he wasn't sure if those Nacli would come to the ranch, but being prepared wouldn't hurt.

Besides, he needed to buy more Poke Balls, so while Keri was constructing the Salt House, he could check out Casseroya Lake to see if he could catch a Skiddo, Gogoat, or Tropius.

Once these things were done, the first batch of crops they planted should be ready for harvest.

After harvesting, they had to plow the fields again to plant the next batch of seeds.

With these thoughts in mind, D glanced at the old clock on the wall, realizing it was nearly one o'clock in the morning.

D: "..."

Time to sleep, time to sleep!

Early the next morning, D went to the friendly store in town to purchase ten Poke Balls.

Although it cost him 2,000 League coins, D believed that the Pokémon he caught with them would surely bring far more value than that.

After leaving the store, he headed to Keri's woodworking shop, located at the edge of town, in a cabin in the woods.

Keri, wearing a light blue work outfit, was chopping wood with an axe.

Seeing D arrive, she raised an eyebrow slightly: "I've heard from Zack about what you've been doing these days. He said you and Koraidon caught a lot of fish and made quite a bit of money. Impressive!"

D smiled, "After all, the ranch has so many Pokémon waiting to be raised, there's no choice but to work hard!"

Keri looked up and down at the young man in front of her.

Compared to their first meeting, his body had become much more muscular, and his skin was tanned, making him look healthier.

Keri admired, "Not bad, you're becoming more like a real rancher! You haven't met the other ranchers in the area, have you?"

D shook his head, "I haven't had the chance to visit them yet. Is there something about them?"

Keri laughed, "Each of them is quite large and muscular, with years of farmwork making them very strong, and I feel you'll become like them one day too."

D shrugged nonchalantly.

He's never been very concerned about his appearance.

After some brief small talk, Keri got down to business, "What do you want me to build this time?"

"Salt House," D replied.

All things considered, raising Nacli to collect and sell rock salt was one of the most profitable ventures.

So, he prioritized the construction of the Salt House.

---

---

---

---

# 39: Chapter 39: S-Tier Milk Porridge

"No problem, the construction cost is a total of 20,000 League coins, but by the way, is there still wood in the ranch?" Keri asked.

She remembered that the last time she went over, she saw that almost all the trees in the ranch had been cut down.

Keri pitched: "If not, you can buy it in the store, one piece of wood sells for 100 League coins, the price of stone is cheaper, only 50 League coins."

The 20,000 is the labor construction cost. If the rancher doesn't have materials, they'll need to buy from them.

D thought for a moment, these days he's been busy fishing and hasn't cut any trees.

The trees in the ranch are limited. Even though there are many trees in the back mountain, large-scale logging would destroy the environment and also make some Pokemon homeless.

After a slight thought, he said directly, "Buy from you then!"

Keri smiled: "Okay, the construction team will go to the ranch this afternoon to start building, you'll be there, right?"

The Salt House is a standard format, and Keri knew very well how much material was needed to build one, and how much land it required.

D nodded, then paid, spending a total of 25,000 League coins.

After leaving the carpenter shop, he went to Porto Marinada Market to buy some fresh ingredients.

He hadn't developed a new dish for a long time.

Due to limited ingredients in the ranch and being busy with work, he could only make the same few dishes back and forth.

Now with eggs and milk available, it was time to start experimenting with new recipes.

By the time he returned to the ranch, it was almost noon.

D didn't have time to rest and started processing the ingredients.

Since there were many eaters, and with Koraidon's huge appetite, if there wasn't enough food, it might end up with an empty stomach.

Because of this, D had to prepare a large amount of food every day, which also made the cooking time longer.

Initially, he lacked experience and worked late, but later he simply started cooking in advance.

For example, right now, it was only 10:46, but D started bustling around.

He scraped the fish scales, removed the guts, added ginger, Galar leek, a little garlic, and then used a prepared sauce for extra freshness and flavor enhancement, and finally placed it in the pot for steaming.

While steaming the fish, D began multitasking.

He cleaned the ribs he bought, blanched them, and then put the ribs with diced white radish into the rice cooker to stew.

Cooking every day improved D's culinary skills, even his knife skills became much more proficient.

As time ticked away, the rich aroma of the pork rib soup spread.

The boiling broth kept bubbling; the steam from the rice cooker made the lid bob up and down, making a bubbling sound.

Koraidon and Cyclizar were attracted to it, gazing longingly at the steaming rice cooker.

D, wearing an apron, shook his finger at them: "No, it's not done yet, have to wait a bit more before eating. You guys go play outside for a while."

"Gaoo..."

"Grrr..."

Cyclizar and Koraidon didn't want to play outside, so they sat on the wooden floor and watched from there.

Seeing them like this, D shook his head helplessly and started simmering the rice porridge.

The ordinary rice porridge D had made once before was E-level cuisine, with the effect of slightly restoring Pokemon's energy.

But this time, looking at the rice porridge in the pot, D suddenly fell into deep thought.

What should be added to it?

A list of names appeared in his mind.

Century egg and lean meat porridge, carrot and millet porridge, mushroom chicken porridge, oatmeal porridge, eight-treasure porridge, corn porridge, purple sweet potato porridge...

But unfortunately, he didn't have most of these ingredients at home.

D turned to look back.

The window sill was empty, Smoliv and Alcremie were probably playing in the sunshine now.

Compared to the meat-loving Koraidon and Cyclizar, Alcremie and Smoliv have a preference for sweet tastes.

And with the steamed sea bass and white radish ribs soup which are meat dishes, one more dish that Alcremie and Smoliv like was still missing.

Thinking of this, D turned to open the cupboard, looking at the ingredients inside, he finally took out a bottle of MooMoo Milk and white sugar.

He poured the MooMoo Milk into the pot, added an appropriate amount of white sugar, and soon, the name of the dish in the pot changed.

[Milk Porridge (B): Milk porridge with added white sugar and MooMoo Milk.

Cooking Effect: Better Milk Lv1, for milk-producing Pokemon only. After eating, it will slightly enhance the quality of the milk produced by the eater, making it taste better and more nutritious, also having the effect of restoring energy.

Evaluation: A nutritious milk porridge, suitable for all ages.]

Oh? A new product?!

D raised his eyebrows.

Better milk.

As the name implies, eating it can improve the quality of the milk produced by Pokemon.

The suitable Pokemon for it...

The image of Miltank and Skiddo's family appeared in D's mind.

"Interesting, just when visiting Nacli this afternoon, I should see if there's a chance to capture a Skiddo."

But sadly, the effect level of this dish is only Lv1.

D guessed it might be because the rice he used was of average quality.

If replaced with higher quality rice and white sugar, the enhancing effect of the dish should increase further.

Seeing the porridge was almost ready, D wanted to serve it and call Smoliv and the others back to eat.

Then he suddenly remembered something.

There was still a small piece of black truffle he had left in the cupboard!

Thinking of this, D immediately turned and took out that piece of black truffle.

Information about black truffle popped into his mind.

[Black Truffle: A top-quality premium ingredient, a rare and delicious delicacy, can be made into black truffle steamed egg with eggs, or creamy thick soup with butter, though it doesn't possess its own benefits, it can significantly enhance the effects of a dish.]

Sure enough, he didn't remember it wrong! It's an enhancing cuisine effect!

D's heart skipped a beat. If he put it into the milk porridge...

Thinking of this, he immediately cut off a quarter of that small piece of black truffle and added it to the pot.

Soon, the name of the dish in the pot changed again.

From ordinary [Milk Porridge], it became [Black Truffle Milk Porridge].

[Black Truffle Milk Porridge (S): Milk porridge with added black truffle, white sugar, and MooMoo Milk. The additional black truffle greatly enhanced the quality of the dish.

Cooking Effect: Better Milk Lv3, for milk-producing Pokemon only. After eating, it will significantly enhance the quality of the milk produced by the eater, making it taste better and more nutritious, also having the effect of quickly restoring energy.

Evaluation: An exquisite milk porridge, rich in nutrients suitable for the growth of all Pokemon, and a rare delicacy.]

Whoa!

An S popped out!

D looked shocked.

He immediately realized the importance of black truffle.

If... if...

At this moment, D even thought about adding black truffle to the pork rib soup and steamed sea bass to see the effect.

But rationality stopped him, because the effects of pork rib soup and steamed sea bass are very ordinary dishes.

One enhances stamina and strength temporarily, while the other restores energy.

"Forget it, better save this for later," D muttered.

He carefully stored the remaining black truffle, simultaneously planning for the future in his heart.

Raise pigs! Must raise pigs!

Raise a large herd of Oinkologne and send them to the back mountain to help find black truffles!

---

---

---

---

# 40: Chapter 40: Nacli Joins!

Lunch time.

D propped his chin on his hand and watched the few Pokémon in front of him enjoy their lunch.

Alcremie and Smoliv were eating as usual—slowly and in small bites.

Koraidon and Cyclizar, as always, were more rugged.

After finishing a few large bones, Koraidon picked up the bowl of Black Truffle Milk Porridge and gulped it down, kind of like a pelican swallowing fish.

"It's a shame Koraidon and Cyclizar can't produce milk."

Watching this scene, D couldn't help but feel a little regretful.

Alcremie can spray cream, but cream isn't milk, kind of like the difference between water and ice—a conceptual difference exists.

And what Smoliv produces is olive oil, which has nothing to do with milk.

In the afternoon, Keri arrived on time with the construction team.

D showed her the pre-selected location, and then rode on Koraidon towards the direction of Casseroya Lake.

Today, he wasn't going fishing but rather checking in on Yousuke and those five Nacli.

Seeing that he didn't have a fishing net or rod, Yousuke seemed a bit taken aback.

"Not fishing today?" he asked.

D shook his head, "Sometimes you have to relax a bit."

Yousuke said, "That's not what you told me a few days ago."

D replied, "...Alright, I've saved enough for building the Pokémon House, so I'm planning to take a break until the construction is finished."

"I see!" Yousuke said with a wink, "I have some good news for you too."

D was a bit surprised, "Oh?"

Yousuke turned and clapped his hands, "Everyone, come out!"

As his voice fell, the five Nacli emerged from the cave, huddling together.

"Nacli~ Nacli~!"

Yousuke laughed, "They've made a decision; they've decided to leave here and live with you on your ranch!"

D was overjoyed to hear this, "Really?"

Yousuke didn't respond; the group of Nacli answered in unison, "Nacli~"

D was extremely happy, "That's great!"

Yousuke said, "What are you standing there for? Quickly take out your Poké Balls and catch them! They've been looking forward to this for a long time!"

"Oh!" D hurriedly took out five Poké Balls from the pocket of his blue overalls.

He squatted down and placed the Poké Balls on the ground. The five Nacli lined up and walked forward one by one.

With a gentle touch of their heads, their small bodies turned into a ray of red light and were absorbed into the Poké Balls.

The Poké Balls wobbled gently twice before settling completely.

Then came the second, the third.

Until the last one was captured, D held the five Poké Balls in his hands, filled with joy.

"Congratulations!" Yousuke said with a smile.

D put away the five Poké Balls, then thanked Yousuke, "I should be thanking you. It's been a hassle for you these past few days."

Yousuke waved it off casually, "Just lending a helping hand. I also needed to study the Pokémon ecology around here. By the way, have you checked on the Dragonite lately?"

D nodded, "Yes, I have."

While fishing with Koraidon, he'd sometimes take a detour to see the Dragonite.

"But... it still ignores us like the first time, sighing quietly before leaving, as if it doesn't like being disturbed."

Yousuke rested his chin in thought, "Is that so?"

He shared his findings from the past few days.

"Based on my research, there's no sign of Dragonite around Casseroya Lake. Given it flies in from the Paldea Sea and returns to the deep sea each time, I suspect its nest isn't nearby but somewhere in the distant ocean."

Hearing this, D's heart was stirred.

It reminded him of the giant Dragonite that appeared near Bill's Lighthouse in the Kanto Region.

He asked, "By the way, Yousuke, have you heard the legend of the 'Giant Shadow on the Sea Surface'?"

"No," Yousuke said curiously, "What's that?"

D said, "It's said that in the depths of the ocean, there's a super-size Dragonite, dozens of meters tall, like a monster, roaming the seas while roaring."

Yousuke was a bit shocked, "A giant Dragonite dozens of meters tall? How could such a Dragonite exist? Only in Pokémon Hollywood monster films, right!"

D said, "...Forget I said anything."

Yousuke didn't pay much mind and continued thinking, "However, I have heard another rumor about Dragonite, want to hear it?"

"Hmm?"

Yousuke didn't hold back the suspense, continuing, "It's said that somewhere in the vast ocean, there's an isolated island inhabited solely by Dragonite."

"A traveling fleet was sailing on the sea and was caught in a storm. A shipwrecked man clung to the wooden planks, drifting in the ocean. A Dragonite rescued him and brought him back to that island, the Dragonite's paradise."

"Things like that could happen," D said.

After all, most Dragonite are kind-hearted Pokémon.

Everywhere, you can hear stories of them saving people or Pokémon from drowning in the sea.

If a Dragonite finds a ship in distress during a storm, it will guide it to the shore. For this reason, many ships place Dragonite statues at the bow, hoping for a safe voyage.

So, the question is, did that Dragonite come from Dragonite Island?

At this moment, both D and Yousuke had this question in their hearts.

They exchanged a glance, and Yousuke's eyes lit up with interest. He excitedly said, "Could such an island really exist in the sea?"

A unique, mysterious island where numerous Dragonite reside.

"Perhaps it does?"

D recalled that in the anime, Ash once visited such an island and even captured a Dragonair there.

That Dragonair later evolved into a hug-loving Dragonite that helped Ash win the Eight Great Masters Championship.

Hearing this, Yousuke grabbed D's shoulder.

D was startled.

"D, let's go on an adventure together!"

Yousuke's eyes gleamed with the light of longing.

An island where only Dragonite exist! It's so exciting! If I could witness such a sight with my own eyes...

D speechlessly pushed his face away, "You don't even know the exact location of the island."

Yousuke immediately looked defeated, "True."

Then he quickly perked up again, filled with energy, "But I won't give up! As long as I keep traveling, one day I'll find Dragonite Island!"

"Go for it!" D encouraged him a bit and then waved his hand, "I have to go."

Yousuke quickly asked, "Where are you going?"

D put away the Poké Balls holding the Nacli and looked at the other empty Poké Balls inside, saying, "Going to see if I can capture any other Pokémon to bring back to the ranch."

Like Skiddo, Tropius...

---

# 41: Chapter 41: Skiddo: Prove Your Strength to Me!

As the saying goes, "All is ready except for the east wind."

The Black Truffle Milk Porridge, which can enhance the quality of milk, has been made, and now all that's left is a milk-producing Pokemon.

Miltank is out of the question for now.

Firstly, because after building the Salt House, he was left with very little money and still needed to save up for the next batch of crop seeds.

Secondly, because the Pokemon House hasn't been repaired yet, leaving no place for a Miltank to stay.

These domesticated, milk-producing Pokemon have certain requirements for their living environment; ideally, they should be kept indoors in warmth, as a good environment aids in milk production.

By contrast, Skiddo and Gogoat don't have such concerns; they live a rougher life.

They use the leaves on their backs to generate energy with just water and sunlight, so they can survive even on barren rocky mountains.

Compared to indoors, they prefer windy grassy fields where they can munch on the freshest grass at any time.

Thus, D plans to fence off a patch of tall grass on the west side of the pasture and build an open pen for the Skiddo family to live in.

"I'll come with you!" Upon hearing D's plan, Yousuke quickly got up to pack up the campsite with Sirfetch'd.

They extinguished the campfire and folded the tent, storing it back in the bag.

The large tent easily fit into Yousuke's small backpack.

D's attention was instantly captured, and he looked surprised, "A Space Backpack?"

Yousuke nodded, "Amazing, right? Despite being small, this backpack holds so much. It's a must-have for every traveling trainer."

Yousuke unzipped the backpack, and D peeked inside, seeing a vast space.

There was not only the tent but also tables, chairs, and cooking pots.

It's black technology! Definitely black technology!

If you could use this to store fish, wouldn't it be much better than a net?

D made up his mind to ask about the Space Backpack at the department store in town later.

For now, he proceeded with Yousuke to the area where the Skiddo were active.

Upon hearing which Pokemon D wanted to capture, Yousuke eagerly offered to lead the way since he was familiar with the area and knew where to find Skiddo.

The two journeyed around Casseroya Lake, crossing a shallow area to reach a zone filled with dense grass.

There, they indeed spotted several Skiddo leisurely grazing.

"1, 2, 3, there are three. Which one do you plan to catch?" Yousuke whispered.

"Of course, I want them all!" D's eyes sparkled with excitement.

"..." Yousuke was momentarily speechless, but then he recalled how he had criticized a trainer for capturing Garganacl and leaving behind a group of Nacli. If it were him, he'd definitely take them all home, and the thought made him feel better.

"Well, then you should jump out now to challenge them."

Knowing D wasn't a trainer, Yousuke began explaining the process for trainers to capture Pokemon.

However, D's method for capturing Pokemon was different from that of regular trainers.

He shook his head, declined Yousuke's suggestion, and instead took out three green dumplings from his bag.

Yousuke was puzzled, "What's that? Is it a Sticky Glob used by ancient trainers to capture Pokemon?"

He had heard that centuries ago, trainers used something called a Sticky Glob to engage in close combat and capture Pokemon.

D was speechless, "...No, I specially prepared this food for them."

[Grass Ball (C): A special dish made with glutinous rice flour as the crust, filled with plump spring grass and a touch of white sugar.

Cooking Effect: Slightly increases the chance of Grass Type Pokemon learning Grass Type Moves.

Additional Effect: Slightly boosts the affection of Grass Type Pokemon.

Review: An imaginative dish!]

The real-life Skiddo looked incredibly cute.

They resembled small goats covered in fluffy, dark brown fur, with white faces, and a patch of brown fur connecting to their backs, giving them a charming cat-like allure.

They had round black noses, and their shiny, round eyes looked intelligent and bright.

Most captivating was the leafy green mane around their necks, which looked like they were wearing a cute little bandana.

So adorable! D started imagining himself strolling with a Skiddo.

At this moment, Yousuke began to introduce them, "Skiddo are known for their gentle nature. Records show they were among the first Pokemon to live alongside humans. Even today, mountain residents still ride them to navigate mountain roads, so capturing one shouldn't be an issue for you."

D nodded slightly, then left Koraidon behind and approached with a few Grass Balls.

The sound of his approach immediately attracted the attention of the three Skiddo.

They turned their heads towards him, not afraid but curious, their black, round eyes sparkling with interest.

D gently tossed the Grass Ball, which enhances affection, towards them.

The Grass Ball landed neatly in front of one of the Skiddo.

It lowered its head, glanced at it, and then gobbled it up.

In the next second, a happy expression appeared on its face.

"Baa!" Delicious!

D then gave the other two Skiddo each a Grass Ball as well.

After they ate, they showed the same pleased expression.

Once they finished all three Grass Balls, the Skiddo looked up at the human with eager anticipation.

D beamed with a warm, friendly smile.

He took out two Poke Balls from his pocket and said, "Come with me! You'll be able to eat this food every day!"

As soon as he said that, two of the Skiddo rushed over to him, raising their heads and bleating gently.

Skiddo: ヾ(✿ﾟ▽ﾟ)ノ

D: "..." Was it really this easy?

He now understood why Skiddo were among the first Pokemon to live with humans.

Perhaps ancient humans accidentally fed them extra grass, making Skiddo realize that following humans meant having food every day, so they started living with humans.

It wasn't just him; even the other Skiddo seemed very surprised.

It looked at its companions, who left with the human trainer just for some food, and seemed dumbfounded.

With an expression that seemed to ask how could they do such a thing?

At this moment, D turned his attention to the remaining Skiddo.

Noticing his gaze, the Skiddo immediately became full of fighting spirit.

It tapped the ground lightly with its hooves.

"Baa!"

Human trainer, if you want to capture me, prove your strength!

Seeing its demeanor, D was instantly taken aback.

He tentatively asked, "Do you want to have a Pokemon battle with me?"

"Baa!" Skiddo nodded seriously.

D: "..."

But he's not a trainer!

Just then, Skiddo glanced at something behind D.

Following its gaze, D saw that at some point, Koraidon had come to his side.

Yousuke followed closely behind and noticed the determined look of the Skiddo. He couldn't resist telling D, "It wants to battle you!"

At times like this, trainers must battle with the Pokemon. Only by winning will it agree to enter the Poke Ball and allow you to become its trainer.

D obviously understood this, but…

He turned around, lowered his voice, and whispered sneakily to Koraidon, "Can you fight?"

Koraidon instantly nodded with enthusiasm, "Gah sis!" Of course! An insignificant Skiddo is no match!

Oh, no! Seeing Koraidon's confident expression, D felt that a single Collision Course from Koraidon, and the Skiddo might just end up knocked out.

He quickly said, "Don't go all out, take it easy!"

"Gah oo?" Koraidon blinked, pondered for a moment, and seemed to understand D's intent.

Confidently, it patted its chest with its big paw, "Ah gah sis!" Leave it to me!

With that, it transformed into its fierce and mighty battle form, standing opposite the Skiddo.

---

---

---

---

# 42: Chapter 42: New Members for the Ranch +1+1+1

With Koraidon's arrival, the battle for recognition officially began.

Skiddo bleated with determination, its eyes sharp, body slightly lowered, as two vine-like tendrils shot out from its body.

"Gaaa?"

Recalling D's words, Koraidon symbolically extended its Dragon Claw.

One vine coiled around its wrist like a serpent.

Then, Skiddo abruptly exerted its strength.

"Baa!"

The vine entwined around Koraidon tightened swiftly.

This was Skiddo's most skilled move, using Vine Whip to entangle the opponent, then lifting them into the air for a slam attack.

Every part of Skiddo's body strained simultaneously, trying to lift Koraidon into the air with its Vine Whip.

However, the expected scene did not unfold.

Koraidon remained motionless, slightly puzzled as it looked at the vine on its wrist.

"Baa?"

Skiddo was taken aback.

In the next instant, Koraidon grabbed the vine and gave it a gentle tug, pulling Skiddo along.

Skiddo: =͟͟͞͞(꒪ᗜ꒪‧̣̥̇)!

"Koraidon's strength is incredible!" Yousuke widened his eyes and murmured.

D: "..."

He didn't say a word, continuing to watch the battle unfold.

Due to Koraidon's immense strength, the vine snapped halfway through the pulling.

Skiddo barely managed to stop itself, standing up somewhat awkwardly.

This Skiddo had a very good physique and decisiveness; as soon as it stabilized, it launched a Razor Leaf at Koraidon.

At the same time, Koraidon moved, leaping forward, raising its right claw high, with a surge of energy transforming into a massive Dragon Claw, charging towards Skiddo amidst a flurry of Razor Leaves.

The Razor Leaves hit Koraidon but seemed so light that they barely caused any damage.

Just as Koraidon was about to strike Skiddo, it glanced at its Dragon Claw, suddenly feeling that this move might be too powerful.

So, the surging energy was withdrawn, the massive white Dragon Claw instantly shrank, and then lightly tapped on Skiddo's body.

"Baa ooh!"

Yet, Koraidon still misjudged its own strength.

With one swipe, Skiddo was sent flying and tumbled onto the grass.

Koraidon: !!!

It quickly turned to look at D, with an apologetic expression, as if to say it wasn't intentional.

D: "..."

"Alright, let's stop here!" D spoke up at the right moment.

He and Yousuke ran over to Skiddo, and after a careful examination, found it was only knocked out, and he breathed a sigh of relief.

"Gaaa?"

Koraidon approached, inquiring about Skiddo's condition.

Seeing its worried face, D reassured, "It's okay, just fainted."

"Don't worry!" Yousuke chimed in, "Every Pokemon is prepared for injury during battles, D, you should capture it now!"

D nodded, took an empty Poke Ball from his pocket, and gently pressed it against Skiddo's forehead.

In the next second, Skiddo's body transformed into a red light and was absorbed.

After a successful capture, D opened the Poke Ball and let the injured Skiddo out.

With the stimulation of being captured, Skiddo had already woken up.

Its gaze towards D changed, becoming much more docile.

"Baa~"

This indicated that it had recognized this human's strength, deeming him qualified to capture it.

"Please take care of me in the future!"

D smiled, patting Skiddo's head, then took a Berry from his backpack and fed it.

Seeing the food, two other Skiddo wandered over foolishly.

D, helpless, took out two more Berries from his backpack to feed them.

After they finished eating, he took out two other Poke Balls and captured them.

These two Skiddo didn't struggle or resist at all, and the Poke Balls only shook slightly before settling.

Watching this scene, D always felt the Poke Balls shook because the Pokemon inside were turning over.

"You come back too!"

D captured the last Skiddo into a Poke Ball, then stood up and looked at the shimmering Casseroya Lake in front of him.

It was already evening, and the setting sun spread its glow across the entire lake, making it look very beautiful.

The breeze blew, and Yousuke felt immensely pleased, taking a deep breath and then asked, "Is there any other Pokemon you want to capture?"

"Tropius." D thought for a moment, but soon realized he seemed to be capturing too many too quickly.

Five Nacli and three Skiddo, totaling eight.

The facilities at the ranch weren't perfect yet, capturing too many at once overcrowded the ranch, making it difficult as a Rancher.

After a brief thought, D decided to settle the Skiddo and Nacli before considering anything else.

Thinking of this, D shook his head and said, "Forget it."

Yousuke: "Why?"

D explained, "My ranch is just starting, and I can't take care of too many Pokemon yet."

Yousuke realized and laughed, saying, "I see, then next time you come, no one will guide you!"

The past few days had been very busy, and D hardly rested. He twisted his neck and flexed his wrists before asking, "Where are you planning to go next?"

"Me? Maybe to the Kanto Region!" Yousuke said, "I've taken an interest in Dragonite. I've heard there's a culture surrounding Dragonite in Kanto and Johto, so I plan to visit there."

"Kanto..." D said, "That's also quite a nice place!"

"Mhm!" Yousuke nodded and asked, "It's getting late, shouldn't you head back to the ranch now?"

D invited, "Want to come to my ranch?"

Yousuke hesitated a bit, "Not right now, I haven't explored one area of Casseroya Lake yet. I want to document the Pokemon there before going."

"Alright." D smiled and nodded, "You're always welcome."

He mounted Koraidon, waved goodbye to Yousuke, and headed back towards the ranch.

"Milk-producing Skiddo are in place, and salt-producing Nacli are in place. Next, I should solve their accommodation and food."

D planned the next phase in his mind.

And just then, he suddenly thought of something.

This trip to Casseroya Lake, he hadn't captured a Pokemon that could use Rain Dance or Water Gun!

D thought again about the Dragonite moon-gazing at the cliff top.

He planned to visit one last time tonight.

If it still flew away at the sight of him, it meant it really didn't want to be disturbed, and he wouldn't bother it any further.
