# 43: Chapter 43: Milking

At five in the evening, D returned to the ranch with Nacli and Skiddo.

Keri's construction team, which was building the Salt House, hadn't left yet. Zack's large truck was parked in front of the ranch, and he stood in front of the Salt House watching Keri's construction.

Keri was the first to notice D's return. She pointed and said to Zack, "He's back."

Zack turned to look and then laughed heartily, "Finally back! Do you have anything to sell today?"

"Yes, I do!"

D and Koraidon walked to the lake, and together they pulled up the fishing net.

Suddenly, the fish from the entire lake were scooped into the air.

"Wow! There are still so many!" Zack was somewhat surprised. He went forward to start weighing the fish and calculating the account.

As he jotted down notes in his ledger, he said to D, who was waiting beside him:

"By the way, D, the news of the many fish you've caught has spread throughout the Porto Marinada Market. A seafood restaurant in town heard about it and wants to partner with you, to specially purchase fish from you. Are you interested in collaborating?"

"Seafood restaurant?" D was a bit surprised.

Zack nodded, revealing a smile on his dark face, his white teeth standing out: "A very famous seafood restaurant, quite a few out-of-towners come specifically to taste the delicacies!"

D pondered for a moment, ultimately refusing the partnership: "Let's pass on that."

The ranch is too far from Casseroya Lake and the western Paldea Sea, plus his and Koraidon's method of fishing is quite primitive.

This method is only suitable for the initial phase of transition; if he were to fish day and night like that all the time, he might as well work the dreaded 996 schedule!

Besides, raising Nacli is much more profitable than fishing.

"I understand." Zack nodded: "I'll let them know. Okay, it comes to a total of three thousand three hundred and sixty League coins!"

Saying this, he pulled out sufficient money from his bag and handed it over.

D reached out to accept it.

Zack turned back to his truck: "See you tomorrow!"

D gave a slight nod: "See you tomorrow!"

Zack drove away in his truck.

Keri and her construction team also stopped work: "It's getting late today. We'll come back tomorrow to continue working."

"Alright."

Seeing Keri off, D took out eight Poke Balls from his backpack, releasing Nacli and Skiddo one by one.

The eight Pokemon curiously surveyed their surroundings.

D said to them, "This will be our home from now on!"

"Nacli~"

"Bleat!"

The Naclis showed happy expressions while the Skiddos were drawn to a lush patch of grass.

They wandered leisurely on the expansive safe pasture, occasionally bowing their heads to take a bite of the tender, lush grass.

Since the Salt House wasn't finished, D brought the Naclis inside, choosing to let them stay there overnight.

The Skiddos stayed outside, enjoying the grass.

After briefly introducing the Pokemon to each other, D hurried to the kitchen to prepare dinner.

The new arrivals didn't elicit much reaction from Cyclizar and the potted Smoliv.

Cyclizar went outside to watch the Skiddos graze while Smoliv lay drowsily in its pot, and Koraidon stuck to D going to the kitchen.

Meanwhile, Alcremie gave the group of Naclis a curious look.

"Ma?"

The Naclis timidly glanced at this Pokemon exuding a sweet aroma.

Compared to them, Alcremie was slightly larger, and since they were new, the five Naclis huddled together a bit fearfully.

"Mama…" Alcremie was somewhat perplexed; was it really that scary?

To soothe their emotions, Alcremie decided to share its delicious fresh cream with them!

So, as D brought dinner to the table, he saw the Naclis covered in cream.

The Naclis nibbled at the cream, looking very happy.

Alcremie stood before them, happily sharing its tasty cream with these rock-like Pokemon.

"Mama!" Eat up, eat up! Don't be afraid of me! Once you've had the cream, we'll be good friends!

D: "…"

Although he didn't know what had happened, it seemed like they had already become good friends!

D placed the dishes on the table. As he looked at the Naclis, something unexpected happened.

Alcremie's cream and Nacli's rock salt merged, resulting in a magical chemical reaction.

D's mind was filled with related information.

[Caramel Rock Salt Cream: A new cream blending milky, caramel, sweet, and salty rock salt flavors, perfectly combined, a wonderful choice for making desserts, greatly appealing to Pokemon that love sweetness and saltiness.]

"Mama!"

Seeing D come out, Alcremie happily called back to him.

Noticing how well they were getting along, D casually said, "Remember to take good care of these Naclis for me!"

"Mama!" Alcremie promised that it would take good care of these uniquely flavored Pokemon!

D smiled, thinking it was not a bad arrangement, and imagined Alcremie sitting on the shoulders of their future evolved forms, Garganacl.

The combination of caramel cream and rock salt sounds pretty good!

With dinner pleasantly concluded, darkness fell outside.

D settled the Naclis on the room's floor and then grabbed a bundle of grass to head outside.

Upon smelling the familiar fragrance, the three Skiddos immediately gathered to eat the grass bundle D prepared for them.

D circled around them, then crouched down, carefully observing the Skiddos' bodies, and discovered the presence of milk-producing organs in their wool.

"As I thought, Skiddos can produce milk too," D murmured to himself.

Like Miltank, they can use the Milk Drink move.

This move is originally Miltank's exclusive move, drinking its own milk during battle to restore a significant amount of stamina.

Later, Skiddos can also comprehend this move.

But why is it called Milk Drink instead of Goat Milk Drink?

These wild Skiddos haven't undergone systematic training, so D wasn't sure about their attitude towards milk production.

After pondering for a moment, D pointed at a Skiddo's milk organ and asked, "Can I touch it? Can I milk goat milk from here?"

After saying this, D felt the question was a bit abrupt.

However, the Skiddo didn't seem uncomfortable, it looked down, its expression appearing slightly puzzled and contemplative.

In its understanding, only baby Skiddos would drink milk.

The Trainer wanting its milk, but the Trainer doesn't seem like a baby anymore, right?

---

---

---

---

# 44: Chapter 44: The Clumsy Dragon Takes Flight First

"Baa?"

The Skiddo let out a puzzled cry.

The other two Skiddo also looked over in confusion.

D suddenly felt a bit embarrassed.

Fortunately, at this moment, a light bulb appeared above the head of one of the Skiddo, as if having a sudden realization.

"Baa!" Maybe the Trainer wants to give the milk to other Pokemon on this ranch!

They can produce a lot of milk, and if there are young Skiddo babies in need, they will feed them.

If there are no Skiddo babies, they will temporarily store it.

So, all three Skiddo were indifferent about this matter.

"Baa baa~"

You want milk? Sure, we'll give it to you! After all, you're our Trainer and you feed us such delicious food!

Seeing the Skiddo's look, D felt a bit perplexed. He tentatively asked, "Are you willing to share the milk with me?"

"Baa!" The Skiddo nodded in unison.

D felt a surge of joy, but he couldn't shake the feeling that the Skiddo misunderstood something. Thinking it over, he explained his plan to sell the milk for profit.

After a pause, D continued, "I'll use the money I earn to buy more ingredients to make more grass dumplings for you."

"Baa?"

The Skiddo looked puzzled, seemingly asking how much more that would be.

D thought for a moment and replied, "More than enough, you won't finish it in your lifetime."

"Baa~!"

Upon hearing that, the Skiddo's eyes shone with excitement, eager to offer all the milk in their bodies in exchange for the grass dumplings.

D: "..." There's no need to go that far.

He breathed a sigh of relief, smiling at the Skiddo, "Then let's settle on that."

The Skiddo would provide milk, while he was responsible for taking care of them for life, a mutual agreement, companionship forever.

Night had fallen.

The sprawling ranch was enveloped in darkness. The Skiddo yawned and lay down on a patch of grass and soon fell asleep after trampling down a large area.

In the cabin, well-fed and close together, the Nacli also drifted into slumber.

D stood at the door, looking at the few Pokemon inside the room, with a gentle smile.

The ranch was becoming more and more lively.

"You should rest too!" D said to the Cyclizar standing nearby, "I'll be going out again tonight, don't wait for me, go to sleep first!"

"Grooa..."

The Cyclizar obediently nodded.

D patted its head, then gently closed the door and flew towards Casseroya Lake on Koraidon.

Guided by memories, D once again reached the clifftop.

Before getting close, he had already seen the familiar figure sitting there.

Dragonite was still sitting there in a daze, looking at the moon with a forlorn expression.

Koraidon landed slowly, and upon hearing the sound, Dragonite turned to look.

D thought this Dragonite would fly away, as it had done for the past few days, but to his surprise, it remained still.

Upon closer inspection, compared to a few days ago, this Dragonite seemed to have some noticeable new scars and appeared quite battered.

Oh? It's injured?

D instinctively took out some berries with restorative effects from his backpack and offered them.

Dragonite blinked, looking at the berry in the human's hand, then looked up at the human.

"Go ahead and eat!" D said, "Eating it will help heal your wounds."

"Oowoo..."

Dragonite sighed softly, declined the human's gesture, and closed its eyes again.

D wasn't disheartened; he sat down next to Dragonite with Koraidon, watching the moon alongside it, "Did something upset you?"

"Oowoo..."

At these words, Dragonite lowered its head.

"Grooa?" (Did you come here because that Dragonite dismissed you as weak?)

At this, Koraidon suddenly asked a question.

Upon hearing this, Dragonite quickly raised its head, a look of surprise on its face.

"Oowoo?" (How did you know?)

"Grooa." (I just know.)

Koraidon tilted its head, playfully withholding an explanation.

Dragonite sighed again, "Oowoo..."

Indeed, it was a very weak Dragonite. Due to natural clumsiness, while other Dragonite could learn many powerful moves during their growth, it couldn't learn any.

During its growth, Dragonite gradually accepted this reality, but not long ago, it fell for another Dragonite.

However, that Dragonite rejected it because it couldn't use Dragon Tail.

Dragonite was very heartbroken and came here every day alone, dazed.

Today during the day, it attempted to train by battling other Dragonite, but ended up injured instead of getting stronger.

Thinking about this, Dragonite couldn't help but sigh again.

Beside him, D looked curiously at Koraidon conversing with Dragonite.

As a human, he couldn't understand Pokemon speech, but judging by Koraidon's demeanor, the dialogue was going smoothly.

Then, Koraidon asked, "Grooa?" (Have you thought about finding a human Trainer to help you become stronger?)

Upon hearing this, Dragonite blinked, looking bewildered, "Oowoo?" (Human Trainer? What's that?)

Koraidon: "Grooa." (It's a human. They can help Pokemon become stronger, evolve, and learn moves.)

Dragonite's eyes widened, and it couldn't believe it, asking, "Oowoo?!" (I can, too?)

Koraidon nodded confidently, "Grooa!"

Dragonite, somewhat excited, quickly asked, "Oowoo?" (Where can I find them?)

This guy is still so dumb... Koraidon felt a bit exasperated and turned its gaze to D beside it.

Dragonite followed its gaze and immediately looked hopeful, "Oowoo?" (Is he the Trainer?)

Koraidon nodded.

In the next moment, Dragonite stood excitedly.

D was stunned for a second. Then, Koraidon pulled a Poke Ball from his pocket with its paw.

D: "?"

He was confused, receiving the Poke Ball handed over by Koraidon, before he could ask, Koraidon said something to Dragonite.

Then, Dragonite reached out its paw.

Upon touching the Poke Ball's button, its body was instantly absorbed by a red light.

Watching this scene, Koraidon felt very satisfied, "Grooa!"

Finally, it could go back and have a good sleep!

D still looked bewildered. He looked at the Poke Ball in his hand, then at Koraidon, and couldn't help but ask, "What did you say to it? Why did it suddenly change like that?"

"Grooa..." Koraidon shook its head, indicating it didn't say much, then pointed at the Poke Ball and flexed its arms, showing its muscles.

D paused, guessing, "It wants to become stronger?"

"Grooa!" Koraidon happily nodded.

What the heck...

Wants to be stronger, so it needs a Trainer?

This Dragonite is like a clueless fool!

But I'm not even a Trainer!

D looked at the Poke Ball in his hand.

This... does this count as capturing it?

# 45: Chapter 45: Goat Milk Delivery Arrived

After returning to the ranch, D was still in a state of confusion.

Judging from Koraidon's actions and cries, it seemed that Koraidon first learned from Dragonite about its intention to become stronger, and Dragonite didn't know what a Trainer was.

Then, Koraidon recommended him to Dragonite, telling it that he could help Pokemon become stronger.

Although saying so isn't wrong...

So, Dragonite excitedly entered the Poke Ball, without realizing whether the other party might be a fraud.

Should it be called naive or silly?

D couldn't judge.

Koraidon landed in the ranch, and D released Dragonite from the Poke Ball.

Seeing the wounds on it, D took out a Berry once again.

This time, Dragonite accepted it without refusal, grabbed it with its claw, and ate the Berry excitedly.

As long as it becomes stronger and learns more moves, it can return to the island and prove itself to the Dragonite that thought it was weak!

So, how could it become stronger?

Dragonite looked expectantly at the human in front of it.

D: "..."

He couldn't help but hold his forehead, "Let's call it a day, it's getting late, tomorrow I'll find a way to make you stronger."

"Oowu..." Dragonite immediately felt a bit disappointed.

Since there wasn't enough room in the cabin, D put Dragonite back into the Poke Ball for the night, allowing it to sleep inside.

The next morning, the sky was bright.

D got up and began working on the ranch with Cyclizar and Koraidon.

Dragonite followed eagerly behind them, waiting for someone to help it become stronger.

D put feed and water in the Chicken House's feeding trough, then turned around to see Dragonite closely following behind.

He immediately felt a bit speechless.

D returned to the cabin and quickly fried a steak that could boost strength and stamina in a short time, and fed it to Dragonite.

After eating it, Dragonite's eyes lit up, and it immediately felt a surge of power in its body, as if it had endless strength.

This made it more confident in front of the human.

D: "You stay on the ranch, I have some errands to run, we'll talk in the afternoon."

Ranch work is very busy, after feeding the chickens and watering, D also had to go to town to buy some consumed daily necessities.

"Oowu..." Alright.

D left Dragonite in Koraidon's care, gave some simple instructions, and then rode Cyclizar to Porto Marinada Town.

This time, he mainly intended to buy some milking tools and ingredients.

The first stop, D went to Happy Ranch.

Upon opening the door, he saw the girl who had previously delivered Alcremie and Smoliv to the ranch.

She was wearing an apron and holding a cloth, doing a thorough cleaning. Seeing D, she immediately stood up and said, "Mr. D, good... good morning!"

"Hello." D looked around and asked, "Where's your grandpa?"

The girl replied, "Grandpa had some business and went out today. If there's anything you need, I can help too."

Ah... D didn't ask more, straightforwardly stating his purpose.

The girl looked surprised, "Did Mr. D catch a milk-producing Pokemon?"

D nodded slightly, "That's right, because the ranch is just starting up and there's not enough funds to buy everything, I thought I'd just catch one myself."

"I see, Mr. D must have been a remarkable Trainer before!" The girl said while fetching an item from the back shelf, "This is a milking machine, and besides this, there are milk cans and glass bottles."

Usually, a Skiddo produces milk every two days, yielding about 2-3 kilograms of goat milk each time.

The rancher uses the milking machine to extract the milk into the milk cans, then bottles it in glass jars.

This way, the milk can be stored for personal consumption or to make cheese and other products, or it can be sold.

Depending on the quality, the price varies—the better the milk quality, the higher the price.

Normally, a small 300 ml bottle of goat milk is sold at around 200 League Coins.

Of course, the rancher can also sell it by the can, with the buyer paying according to the milk quantity.

D had understood this beforehand.

He promptly purchased a milking machine, three milk cans, and 200 glass bottles meant for packaging.

These cost a total of 4,600 League Coins.

After paying, the girl said, "Alright, I'll deliver these items to your ranch later, Mr. D."

"Thanks a lot."

D thanked her politely, then rode Cyclizar to Porto Marinada Market to purchase a large amount of glutinous rice flour, white sugar, and many ingredients Pokemon usually eat.

Glutinous rice flour and white sugar combined with grass can make grass balls, which Skiddo loves, and he plans to make a lot of them as feed for Skiddo.

After finishing his errands, D didn't stay in town long.

When he rode Cyclizar back to the ranch, Keri's construction team had already arrived to start working.

Seeing D, Keri's gaze fell on the nearby dazed Dragonite, revealing a look of surprise, "I didn't expect you to have caught such a rare Pokemon like Dragonite!"

D: "..." Could he say this Dragonite just followed him?

But Keri didn't ask more, she was just making a casual remark before continuing her work.

D turned and placed the purchased ingredients into the cupboard, and when he came out, the milking machine, milk cans, and glass bottles had also arrived.

After receiving the items from the girl, D took them to the location of the three Skiddo.

The three Skiddo stood up obediently.

D wrapped their milking organs with the milking machine, placed the milk can underneath, and with a gentle squeeze, a stream of sweet-smelling goat milk flowed into the can.

"Did I squeeze too hard? Do you feel uncomfortable anywhere?" D asked.

"Baa~"

This Skiddo shook its head, indicating everything was fine.

"That's good."

D breathed a sigh of relief and continued working.

After milking all three Skiddo, it barely filled one milk can, about fifteen pounds.

D glanced at the specification, 7.5 liters.

"The glass bottle specification is 300 ml, priced at 200 League Coins each. 7.5 liters of goat milk can sell for about 5,000 League Coins."

D roughly calculated the profit from the goat milk in his heart.

With three Skiddo producing 5,000 every two days, that's 75,000 a month, which translates to being able to buy seven Nacli, then money can make more money.

Finally struck it rich!

D felt very happy.

The ranch finally has a stable income source!

He immediately carried the milk can back to the cabin, recalling the elderly lady who had come to order milk a few days ago.

He then took out the ledger from the drawer, found her phone number, and dialed it via a video phone.

After a few beeps, the call connected, and D asked, "Hello, Mrs. Dantes, this is D Ranch. Fresh goat milk has arrived. Do you need any?"

Hearing this, Boji·Dantes's voice over the phone revealed a hint of joy, and she quickly said, "I do! Thank you so much! I'm very grateful!"

After a brief chat, D learned about her requirements and address.

Customer Name: Boji·Dantes

Requirement: Daily morning delivery of two bottles of fresh goat milk, willing to pay 500 League Coins per bottle.

Address: 308 Blue Sea Street, Porto Marinada Town

"500 per bottle, that's 1,000 for two, the normal market price is 400 for two, that's a markup of 600. Such a price is outrageous..." Even as the seller, D felt it was a bit absurd; perhaps they really needed it.

After thinking it over, he decided to sell it at the normal price, at most charging an additional 100 League Coins for delivery.

Besides, there's another issue.

Who would deliver the milk to town every day?

D pondered deeply.

Just then, a sound came from behind.

D turned and saw Dragonite eagerly flying over, seemingly asking when it could begin helping to become stronger.

D: "..."

Alright, it's decided that you'll do it!

A smile appeared on his face, "I've figured out a way to help you train!"

Dragonite's eyes immediately lit up, "Oowu?"

"Help me deliver milk!" D patted its shoulder, explaining the job details to it.

After listening to D's explanation, Dragonite looked puzzled.

Can this really make me stronger?

D persuaded, "No, no, delivering isn't as simple as you think. While delivering, you need to master flight skills, sustain efforts over a long time, which is beneficial for training your physical strength, making you stronger, and tempering your Guts."

Dragonite: "!"

---

---

---

---

# 46: Chapter 46: Goat Milk Quality Up Up!

At noon, after finishing lunch for the Pokémon, D began making green grass dumplings using the glutinous rice flour he bought and the grass cut from the pasture.

He wore a gray, dirt-resistant apron, and in front of him, on the cutting board, were washed and cut grass along with prepared syrup.

Under D's hands, the glutinous rice dough was constantly being rolled out into circular cakes.

The green grass dumplings, unlike traditional feed, cannot be stored for long, so D planned to make about a week's worth at a time.

Once the Skiddos have eaten them all, he'll start making a new batch.

The afternoon sunlight streamed in through the window, and D's hands were covered with glutinous rice flour.

Outside in the pasture, Alcremie and Smoliv were playing around with the Nacli, while the Skiddos lounged leisurely on the grass. Koraidon and Dragonite seemed to be whispering about something, and Cyclizar dozed off in the shadow under the eaves.

Hearing the sounds from outside, D suddenly felt this lifestyle was quite pleasant.

Stretching with a big yawn, D began wrapping the dough around the grass, then put them all in the steamer, working until three-thirty in the afternoon to finish everything.

Each green dumpling looked like a newborn chubby boy, piled up in a large stainless-steel basin.

D looked down, and the information about these dumplings appeared in his mind.

[Green Grass Dumplings (C): Made with glutinous rice flour as the skin and filled with tender grass that grows in spring and a small amount of white sugar as the filling to create this special dish.

Cooking Effect: Slightly improves the probability of Grass Type Pokémon comprehending Grass Type Moves after eating.

Additional Effect: Slightly increases the affection of Grass Type Pokémon

Evaluation: A creatively unconventional dish!]

"Able to slightly improve the probability of Pokémon comprehending Grass Type Moves..." D pondered slightly, "Eating this three times a day as a meal shouldn't be an issue, right?"

Probably?

D didn't think too much about it, considering all the ingredients used in the green grass dumplings were quite normal.

Setting the green grass dumplings aside to cool, D took rice and MooMoo Milk from the cupboard and began making milk porridge to enhance milk quality and nutrition.

This time he was smart, not cutting the Black Truffle directly into pieces but grating it into the dish using a grater.

Soon, a simple milk porridge was ready.

The effect was exactly the same as last time.

[Black Truffle Milk Porridge (S): A milk porridge added with Black Truffle, White Sugar, and MooMoo Milk. The addition of Black Truffle has greatly enhanced the quality of the dish.

Cooking Effect: Better Milk lv3, exclusive to milk-producing Pokémon; after eating it, it will greatly enhance the quality of the milk produced by the eater, making it taste better and more nutritious, while also having a rapid stamina recovery effect.

Evaluation: An exquisite milk porridge whose nutritional value is suitable for the growth of all Pokémon, a rare delicacy.]

D served three bowls, bringing them to the Skiddos, and said with a smile, "Drink up!"

The Skiddos immediately caught the rich aroma of milk; they usually consume some grains, so they do not resist foods like rice and milk.

Perhaps the flavor was truly exceptional, as the three Skiddos quickly finished the milk porridge, leaving their bowls spotless and even showing expressions of longing for more.

"What a pity they have to wait two days before producing milk again," D thought to himself.

Today, D had already milked the Skiddos dry, so there was no immediate way to check the changes in their milk.

Before evolving, Skiddos produce milk every two days. Only after evolving into Gogoats do they produce milk daily, with an increase in both quantity and quality.

After feeding the Skiddos, D went back to the wooden cabin and washed the bowls clean, then went to find Keri to discuss building a goat pen.

"A goat pen? That's simple. For Skiddos, you just need to fence them in with wooden rails," Keri said with a smile. "That's easier than building the Salt House. Fortunately, the Salt House will be completed by tomorrow morning, so I'll bring the materials over then to help you build it."

"Alright," D nodded.

The money earned from the last fishing trip left him with about fifteen thousand League currency. After building a goat pen, only five thousand would remain.

D couldn't help but marvel, "It's spent so quickly!" He barely felt it.

But thankfully, with Skiddos and Nacli on the farm, there's a continuous source of income.

"Once the Salt House is completed tomorrow, the project of farming Nacli will officially begin. Combined with the milk from the Skiddos, within a month, the money will be earned back."

"Hmm, the crops in the field are also about to mature, and they could bring in some money as well."

D pondered the to-do list for the farm.

But before that, he had something else to take care of, which was familiarizing Dragonite with the delivery route.

With this in mind, D found Dragonite and said to it, "Let's go!"

"Roar!" Dragonite let out a joyful cry.

D instructed Koraidon to stay behind and guard the farm while he rode on Dragonite's back to fly to the town.

It seemed Dragonite didn't mind being ridden at all.

Or rather, its mind was fully occupied with the desire to get stronger, not caring about such things.

The feeling was completely different from riding Koraidon; Dragonite was soft and cushy, making the touch feel very pleasant.

It's just a pity he didn't have a chance to touch its big belly.

D lamented a little.

Dragonite flew in the sky, feeling a bit worried inside.

Flying like this didn't seem much different from when it flew over the sea, would this really make it stronger?

Dragonite let out a questioning call.

Although they couldn't communicate in words, based on Dragonite's expression and tone, D could roughly guess its meaning.

It was doubtful whether delivering goods would really make it stronger!

How could that be possible?

D immediately said to Dragonite, "Delivering goods isn't just about the delivery; you need to feel everything during the process, the flow of the wind, the formation of air currents, or even the grass and trees by the roadside."

"Use your eyes to see, in the process of constantly delivering goods, gradually get to know everything around you, understand, imitate, and finally master them."

"You can learn to flap your wings by sensing the flow of the wind, imitating the process, or even merge yourself into the air currents."

"No Dragonite is born knowing how to use those powerful moves; everyone needs a lot of practice and unyielding effort to skillfully master the moves they have learned."

"Roar!"

What profound words!

Yet, why were they somewhat hard for Dragonite to understand?

However, they sure sounded impressive!

It must learn those powerful moves and become stronger!

Dragonite immediately became full of fighting spirit.

# 47: Chapter 47: Deliveryman

Seeing this scene, D breathed a sigh of relief.

Man and dragon arrived in the town, and D found Blue Sea Street by following the directions on the road signs.

This was a residential area located in the northeastern corner of the town. The nearby houses were typical Japanese-style architecture, neatly situated along the road, surrounded by artificially planted flowers and plants.

The road was very clean, and along with its distance from the market area, the environment nearby was very quiet.

"Number 308..."

D and Dragonite made a round in the vicinity and finally found No. 308 at a fork in the road.

He walked into the courtyard filled with flowers and plants and knocked on the door: "Excuse me, is Mrs. Dantes in?"

After waiting for a while, no one came to the door.

D suspected that the other party might have gone out.

"This address should be correct." He looked at the note in his hand, confirmed the house number, and didn't wait there any longer.

Instead, he turned around and said to Dragonite, "This is the place, can you remember the route?"

Dragonite nodded its head: "Oowoo!"

D: "Very good."

He nodded in satisfaction, and since it was getting late, he left the place with Dragonite.

However, D did not immediately return to the ranch but went to the town's grocery store and bought a small red satchel.

He equipped Dragonite with the satchel, and suddenly, a Dragonite with a red satchel in a deliveryman image appeared in front of him.

"Not bad at all."

D nodded in satisfaction; after circling Dragonite twice, he rode on it and returned to the ranch.

The next day.

D got up early to prepare breakfast for several Pokémon.

After eating, he placed two bottles of prepared goat's milk into the red satchel that Dragonite was carrying.

"Do you still remember the route?" D asked.

Dragonite looked at the two bottles of milky white goat's milk and innocently nodded: "Oowoo."

D: "Then let's set off! Deliver these two bottles of goat's milk to the owner of that house!"

After giving the instructions, Dragonite flew into the sky with the goat's milk, heading towards the town.

D watched its back and thought to himself that he couldn't fool Dragonite forever. After all, Dragonite is a very smart Pokémon, and if it found out it had been deceived, it would probably be very angry.

So D turned back to the wooden cabin and secretly started preparing dishes that could help Dragonite get stronger and learn moves.

Now, the question was.

What kind of dish could have this effect? D fell into thought.

---

Porto Marinada Town, Blue Sea Street, Number 308.

Boji Dantes got up early to help her seriously ill bedridden husband with his daily washing routine.

The old man on the bed had gray hair, a thin body, and looked very frail.

Seeing the old man's face looking unwell, Boji Dantes comforted, "I've found a ranch with goat milk. Don't worry, everything will get better."

The old man coughed twice, took a deep breath, and with a moving expression said, "You've worked hard all these years."

Boji Dantes gently shook her head, "Don't talk about hardships or not..."

She wanted to say more but suddenly heard a knock on the door from outside.

Boji Dantes' eyes lit up, and she said to her husband, "It must be the person delivering the goat milk!"

She quickly got up to open the door, and as the door opened, a Dragonite about two meters tall, with a round body carrying a small red satchel, appeared in front of her.

Boji Dantes was stunned on the spot.

Dragonite stood at the door, looking at the elderly woman with its big, bright black eyes.

Remembering D's instructions, it took out two bottles of goat milk from the satchel with its large claw and handed them over, "Oowoo!"

It was only then that Boji Dantes came back to her senses.

She was shocked as she looked at Dragonite in front of her, trembling as she said, "Did you come from D Ranch to deliver goat milk?"

"Oowoo~" Dragonite nodded lightly.

Boji Dantes couldn't believe her eyes.

My God! Someone is using a Dragonite to deliver milk!

That is an extremely rare Pokémon!

Normally, people use Pelipper as delivery personnel.

This is too extravagant!

The word "extravagant" was all that remained in Boji Dantes' mind.

She dazedly took the goat milk from Dragonite's claw, and as she watched Dragonite about to turn and fly away, she finally came to her senses and shouted, "Wait a minute!"

Dragonite turned its head in puzzlement: "Oowoo?"

"You haven't been paid yet!" Boji Dantes took out the prepared deposit from her pocket.

She originally intended to place it in Dragonite's claw, but recalling how it was about to fly off without waiting for money, she placed the money in its red satchel and carefully zipped it up.

"Make sure you keep the money safe!"

"Oowoo..."

Dragonite then remembered it seemed to have forgotten about this matter.

Just on the way over, it was thinking about the things D said, observing the flowers and trees along the road, wanting to become stronger through them.

Nonetheless, since the delivery was made, it was time to leave.

Dragonite turned around, soared into the sky, and started heading back to the ranch.

Until Dragonite's figure disappeared from sight, Boji Dantes turned and went back inside to tell her husband about it.

"It was really surprising, the newly reopened D Ranch actually sent a Dragonite to deliver milk!"

The old man lying on the bed showed a surprised expression upon hearing this.

When they were young, both were trainers traveling around, and naturally knew how rare a Pokémon Dragonite was.

They're mighty, adorable in appearance, good-natured, capable of carrying a trainer into the sky, crossing mountains and seas.

Decades ago, there was even a Dragonite craze in the Paldea Region.

Countless people wanted to capture a Dragonite, but due to its rarity, even traveling throughout the Paldea Region, it was hard to encounter one.

And now, this kind of Pokémon is used to deliver milk!

"If those trainers knew about this, they'd be green with envy," the old man said with a look of envy.

Even though he was old and not young anymore, deep down inside, he still held the original dreams of a trainer.

He encountered his Pokémon partner and also met a girl who was also a trainer during his travels.

That girl is now his wife.

"Alright, I should go prepare breakfast." Boji Dantes stood up, a faint smile on her face, and she said with hope, "After having this herbal meal, your body will get better."

Meanwhile, at the ranch.

D and Keri stood in front of a newly constructed building.

Keri: "The Salt House is finished; you should go in and take a look!"

D nodded. Just as he was developing a new dish, Keri called him out to inspect the Salt House, so he paused what he was doing to come over and check.

The exterior of the Salt House was distinct from the Chicken House.

The Chicken House had an entirely wooden structure, whereas the Salt House was built with stone and cement.

The interior design simulated a cave environment, with some stones serving as decorations in the corners.

Upon entering, D immediately noticed a layer of transparent film on the ground that looked like plastic but felt somewhat hard to the touch.

Keri explained, "This is storage film. When the Naclis move around in here, the rock salt that falls off their bodies from friction will be collected on it. All you have to do is remove the storage film to get a bunch of rock salt."

D nodded, indicating he understood.

Keri smiled slightly and continued, "Now I will go build the sheep pen for you, and during this time, you can bring the Naclis here to familiarize them with the environment."

# 48: Chapter 48: Skiddo's Explosive Debut

Compared to the Salt House and Chicken House, building the sheep pen was much simpler.

Since Skiddo are very smart Pokemon and know what to eat and what not to eat, there's no need to pen them up like ordinary cattle and sheep.

Therefore, the sheep pen was designed with a semi-open structure, specifically leaving an entrance without a door for the Skiddo to go in and out.

Inside the fence, an open rain shelter was built.

The shelter is very spacious, and it could accommodate another three Skiddo aside from the current three.

Inside the shelter, there are feed troughs and water troughs, with a thick layer of straw on the floor. When it rains, the Skiddo can enjoy eating Grassy Seed while watching the rain.

Soon, the construction of the sheep pen was completed. Keri put away her tools, stood up, and said, "It's done! Are you satisfied? If not, I can help you rebuild it."

"It's fine, looks pretty good." D nodded and turned to the three observing Skiddo beside him, "What do you think?"

"Baa~"

The Skiddo seemed quite pleased too.

Compared to the open and exposed wilderness, this semi-enclosed shelter and fence give them a strong sense of security.

No need to worry about being attacked from all sides by other Pokemon like in the wild.

The three of them bleated happily as they walked forward, took a round under the shelter, and finally lay down on the straw, looking very content.

Keri walked over to D, smiling, and said, "It seems they are pretty satisfied with their new home."

"Indeed," D nodded in agreement.

No matter what Pokemon, they more or less have some sense of territory. Unless they are very familiar, they rarely enter other Pokemon's territories.

Keri pointed to the patch of grass in the sheep pen and said, "When the Skiddo finish eating this grass, you can buy some pasture seeds from the town. Just scatter them in the soil, and they'll grow even without watering."

The construction work ended, and Keri left the ranch with her team.

D took some cooled Grassy Seed from the house and poured it into the Skiddo's feed trough for dinner, then filled up the water trough beside it.

After doing this, he found Nacli and took them to their new home.

Inside the Salt House, D smiled at the five Nacli and said, "How is it? This is where you'll live. Are you satisfied?"

"Nacl~"

Just like the Skiddo, the Nacli were also very pleased with their new home.

They swarmed into the room, curiously wandering around to get familiar with the environment.

Just then, hearing about the Nacli's new home, Alcremie, who was playing outside, hurriedly brought Smoliv to celebrate.

Over time, the Nacli were no longer afraid of Alcremie; they even seemed to have become good friends.

D referred to this relationship as the "Caramel Cream and Rock Salt Friendship."

Needless to say, the taste of the new cream made by blending the two was quite nice, reminiscent of a cheese rock salt Assam milk tea.

"You all play here, I'm going to see if Dragonite is back."

"Nacl~"

The group of Pokemon nodded their heads.

Seeing this, D smiled slightly and turned to head outside.

He saw that Koraidon was waiting for him outside and asked, "Has Dragonite returned?"

"Raaaaa." Koraidon shook its head.

"Not back yet?" D said while walking forward, looking towards the town.

As he passed the sheep pen, he suddenly noticed the three Skiddo lazily lying on the straw, their bellies all round and full.

D: "?"

He instinctively looked towards the feed trough and sure enough, saw that all the Grassy Seed inside had been eaten.

D: "…Hey! That wasn't meant to be eaten as a snack!"

Upon hearing D's tone, the three Skiddo thought they had done something wrong and their Growl became timid.

"Aw, baa…"

D: "…"

Forget it, just let them eat! It's just a few Grassy Seeds, he can afford it.

"It's okay, go ahead and eat! More food is a blessing!" D instead comforted the three Skiddo.

Next, he planned to head back home to try out a new recipe.

But before he could turn around, one of the Skiddo suddenly erupted in a burst of green light.

The light radiated from its body into the ground, and soon the surrounding area lit up with vibrant green glimmers of life.

In the light, countless blades of grass shimmered and swayed gently, displaying robust vitality.

A big question mark appeared over D's head.

"?"

Is that... a Grassy Terrain?

Grassy Terrain: A Grass Type Move, which turns the surroundings into a grassy field for a short time. Pokemon on it will regain health, and Grass Type Moves' power will increase.

So, the Skiddo has grasped Grassy Terrain?

D was initially surprised, but considering the three Skiddo had just eaten dozens of Grassy Seeds, it wasn't so shocking after all.

Because that's the effect of Grassy Seed.

Learning Grassy Terrain in a day—if they keep eating for another ten years, D could hardly imagine what moves they might learn!

After all, Grass Type Moves aren't just limited to Vine Whip, Razor Leaf, Seed Flare, and the like.

There are also Frenzy Plant, Grassy Glide, Seed Flare... the list goes on.

---

At that time, he should have been considering whether Skiddo was legal, rather than whether Skiddo would eat the feed.

Passersby might be so scared they'd wet themselves.

"It's okay, it's okay, it's okay."

D repeated in his heart. For him, as long as Skiddo could continue to produce milk, that was enough.

Thinking of this, D turned around.

But before he could take two steps, he heard a shout of exertion from Skiddo behind him.

He turned around abruptly and saw a Skiddo gathering a bright ball of light in its mouth.

The next second, that light transformed into a beam that shot out, instantly demolishing the newly constructed wooden fence.

D: "!!!"

Where the Solar Beam passed, not a blade of grass was left, leaving a clear burn mark on the ground, as if the hot sunlight had scorched the earth.

The Skiddos looked innocent.

D took a deep breath and decided not to leave. He crossed his arms and watched them perform, saying with amusement:

"Go ahead! Let me see what other moves you haven't used!"

"Baa..."

The Skiddos had a cute and clueless expression as if they had no idea what had happened.

What was up with that? How did they suddenly comprehend a new move?

D helplessly said, "At this rate, you could form a group and debut."

One Skiddo used Grassy Terrain, another Skiddo acted cute to distract the opponent, and the last Skiddo used Solar Beam on the Grassy Terrain to attack.

The Skiddos didn't understand what forming a group and debuting meant.

D didn't bother to explain further. He found an axe, saw, hammer, wooden planks, and nails from the storeroom and began to repair the fence that had been destroyed by Solar Beam.

Just after finishing the fence repairs, Dragonite finally returned late to the ranch.

It had a small red satchel around its waist and slowly landed beside D.

D quickly got up and asked, "How did it go?"

"Roar..." Dragonite seemed a bit disappointed; it hadn't learned any new moves.

But it had successfully delivered the goat milk!

Dragonite took out the money Mrs. Dantes paid from its satchel and handed it to D.

D reached out to take it, feeling a bit soft-hearted at Dragonite's pitiful appearance.

But the meal that could help Dragonite grow stronger hadn't been prepared yet...

After pondering for a moment, D asked, "Can you show me the moves you currently know?"

A demonstration? Dragonite thought blankly for a moment, then began to demonstrate.

It flew high into the air, spread its wings wide, and dove toward the ground.

"This should be the Flying Type's Wing Attack," D thought.

The second move, Dragonite opened its eyes wide, and its gaze turned sharp.

"Leer?" D raised an eyebrow.

This is a move that frightens opponents with its gaze, thereby lowering their defense.

But with Dragonite's cute and round appearance, it lacked any intimidation. At least D and Koraidon didn't feel scared; instead, they found it adorable, almost melting from the cuteness.

The third move, Dragonite whipped up a Twister on the grass, but the Twister wasn't very large in scale.

"This move should be Twister."

The fourth move, Dragonite opened its mouth and unleashed a purple shockwave forward.

"Dragon Rage," D thought to himself.

He stood there watching, but Dragonite didn't continue.

D was slightly taken aback: "That's it?"

Dragonite lowered its head, crestfallen: "Roar..."

Well, it seemed this Dragonite's intuition wasn't that high.

D took a deep breath, pondering in his heart how he could help a Pokemon grasp moves if he were a trainer.

Scenes flashed through D's mind: Ash teaching Dragonite Dragon Dance, Greninja teaching Lucario, Mrs. Wilma in the Sinnoh Region teaching various Pokemon Draco Meteor.

Most of Pokemon's moves are about mastering the energy within their bodies, transforming it into beams, hurricanes, shock waves, bubbles, and other forms to project it outward.

That's right; it's about energy control.

Koraidon is also a Dragon Type Pokemon; although he hadn't seen it with his own eyes, the Dragon Type moves it mastered should be quite a few.

Draco Meteor and Outrage are quite challenging for Dragonite; comparatively, Dragon Tail is much simpler.

After pondering for a moment, D looked at Koraidon beside him and asked, "Koraidon, can you use Dragon Tail?"

"Garrr!" Koraidon nodded.

D's eyes lit up: "Then can you teach Dragonite?"

Koraidon was a bit hesitant; it knew how to use it but hadn't taught anyone before.

Seeing this, D said, "It's okay, just demonstrate it for him." He would explain it.

That shouldn't be a problem, thought Koraidon.

So, D looked at Dragonite and said, "We're not in a rush for deliveries now; I have another move to teach you."

Upon hearing this, Dragonite's eyes lit up: "Roar?"

D set aside the fence repair work and led Koraidon and Dragonite to an open area.

"Watch Koraidon's demonstration carefully, Dragonite!" D shouted.

On the open ground, Koraidon was already prepared.

In its battle form, its thick tail was enveloped in a layer of purple light.

The next moment, Koraidon spun its body, and that strong, flexible tail swept forward.

The surrounding airflow was stirred, bending the grass on the ground in a messy fashion.

Dragonite focused intently while watching.

After the move ended, Koraidon stepped aside.

D said to Dragonite: "Want to give it a try?"

"Roar..."

Dragonite hesitantly stepped forward, attempting to imitate Koraidon's tail-whipping motion but failed to use it successfully.

This made Dragonite feel very disheartened.

D stood to the side with his arms crossed, comparing the state of the two Pokemon while using the move in his mind, then said: "Can you mobilize the Dragon Type energy in your body?"

"Roar?" Dragonite blinked its eyes, then nodded.

"Dragon Tail isn't just about swinging your tail; Koraidon's Dragon Tail also has a copious amount of Dragon Type energy covering it," D explained. "So, you can try to concentrate the Dragon Type energy onto your tail, then whip it."

Dragonite listened, seemingly understanding yet not fully comprehending.

Concentrate the Dragon Type energy onto the tail...

Dragonite tried again, but still failed.

This made it feel somewhat dejected.

Seeing this, D encouraged, "Don't lose heart; you need perseverance and determination. Remember what I told you before?"

Dragonite lifted its head: "Roar..."

D continued, "No Dragonite's move is learned in one go. Since you can't use Dragon Tail now, we'll take it step by step."

"Break down the entire move, start by practicing mobilizing the energy within your body, gathering it to your tail, and once you're proficient, practice the next step. I see that you can mobilize energy, and with diligent practice, you'll definitely be able to use Dragon Tail successfully."

Under D's encouragement, Dragonite's fighting spirit was reignited: "Roar!"

It stood still, focusing its mind and began to mobilize the Dragon Type energy within.

D watched for a while, then said to Koraidon: "Stay here and keep it company while practicing; there's something I need to do."

"Garrr!" Koraidon nodded its big head in agreement.

D then turned around and headed toward the wooden cabin.

In front of the cabin, Cyclizar was lounging under the eaves, which had almost become its exclusive spot.

Upon seeing D approaching, Cyclizar stood up with a soft cry.

D patted Cyclizar's head, and Cyclizar followed him into the room.

The two of them came to the kitchen area. D looked at the table full of prepared ingredients and seasonings, feeling bewildered.

After a bit of thought, he asked Cyclizar next to him:

"Cyclizar, is there any food you particularly love to eat? The kind you really enjoy."

Cyclizar, like Dragonite, is also a Dragon Type Pokemon, so its preferences might provide valuable insights.

---

---

---

---

# 49: Chapter 49: Cyclizar and Dragon Tail

"Gawoo~"

Cyclizar had a joyful expression, showing that it liked anything D made.

Even though he couldn't understand it, based on Cyclizar's expressions and actions, D could guess its meaning.

He deliberately asked, "What about hot noodle soup then?"

Cyclizar immediately drooped and lowered its head, "Gawoo..."

"Alright, just kidding with you!" D reached out to pat Cyclizar's head and then began to seriously ponder what kind of dishes to make.

Fried pork chops, sandwiches, steamed sea bass, sticky rice dumplings, steamed egg, creamy mushroom soup... he'd already made all of these.

Plus, with limited ingredients at home, D could only create new dishes by changing ingredient combinations.

Thinking of this, D began a new attempt.

He randomly picked a few ingredients from the cutting board: fresh tender pork loin, eggs, and Rock Salt produced by Nacli.

For example, coating the pork loin in egg batter and deep-frying it, using Alcremie's cream and Rock Salt from Nacli as stuffing for sticky rice dumplings, using well-prepared sea bass to make a Lemon Sole Sandwich.

However, after busying himself all day, none of these dishes could make Dragonite stronger.

[Fried Pork Loin (C): Cooking Effect, restores health.]

[Xue Mei Niang (B): Cooking Effect, greatly increases the fondness of dessert-loving Pokemon, has a great attraction to Alcremie.]

[Lemon Sole Sandwich (C+): Cooking Effect, attracts Water Type Pokemon when carried.]

D: "..."

He couldn't help taking a deep breath, turning around to feed the fried pork loin to Cyclizar beside him.

Cyclizar happily ate it.

D fell into thought, "This is a bit challenging!"

His gaze swept across the various ingredients on the table, and when he saw a few Berries in a basket, he suddenly thought of a dish he'd made before.

Berry Platter!

Yes, it was the Berry Platter!

It was a special dish that could randomize a Pokemon's moves. If it was that one...

With that thought, D immediately took out those Berries, cut them into pieces with a fruit knife, and placed them on a white porcelain plate.

Soon, a fruit platter was ready.

D looked down, and the information about the dish emerged from his mind.

[Berry Platter (C-): A delicacy made of various Berries, blending the flavors of different Berries, favored by some Pokemon.

Cooking Effect: Skill Refinement, after consumption, a certain move of the Pokemon will randomly change.

Evaluation: A basic dish, easy to make, a good choice for home and travel.]

If he gave it to Dragonite to eat...

In D's mind, a scene of Dragonite mastering a lot of moves appeared, with a happy smile on its face, excitedly charming him.

But then, another scene appeared of Dragonite forgetting all four of its moves, then leaving home heartbroken.

It's a gamble on luck...

D was a bit hesitant, knowing well the probability and quirks of these trash games, he didn't dare to gamble for a moment.

The worst situation after feeding it to Dragonite would be forgetting all four moves, and at that time, eating it again would surely comprehend a new move.

If extremely lucky, it might learn powerful moves like Precipice Blades, Origin Pulse, Roar of Time, Spacial Rend.

But if it learned something like Tackle, it wouldn't be worth it.

To him, it's just sunk cost, but Dragonite doesn't understand that.

If it's unlucky, it might even forget the four moves it painstakingly learned.

Then, losing faith in him, it would run away heartbroken.

"No, I can't feed it yet."

After thinking it over, D decided to wait a while to see if Dragonite could learn Dragon Tail, first building trust with it, then use the Berry Platter according to the situation.

Anyway, he's not in a hurry, there's plenty of time.

With this in mind, D packed up the remaining ingredients and took the Xue Mei Niang and Lemon Sole Sandwich to the Salt House, where Alcremie and Smoliv were playing with the Naclis, sharing the two with them.

Once they finished eating, D collected the empty plates and returned to the log cabin.

Upon reaching the kitchen area, after placing the tableware neatly, D suddenly noticed that the Berry Platter he placed here was empty.

"?"

D looked back at Cyclizar, not far behind him, and asked, "Did you eat the Berries here?"

"Gawoo..."

Cyclizar nodded, and seeing D's expression, it was a bit worried if it had done something wrong.

D said helplessly, "No, I don't mean to blame you, I'm just asking."

Then he asked, "Do you feel any difference in your body compared to before? Like learning a new move, or forgetting any old ones."

"Gawoo?" Hearing this, Cyclizar was initially puzzled, but soon its eyes brightened. It stood up and demonstrated the newly learned move to D.

He saw a purple Dragon Type energy gathering on Cyclizar's tail, and when the energy gathered to a certain extent, Cyclizar moved.

It swung its tail vigorously, making a "whoosh" sound in the air. Although it hit nothing, from its movements, it seemed to be quite a powerful move.

"Dragon Tail?" D slightly raised an eyebrow, "Is this the move you just learned?"

"Gawoo!" Cyclizar nodded.

"You didn't forget the other moves?" D asked again.

Cyclizar nodded again.

It seems Cyclizar's luck is quite good!

D nodded, "Got it."

————

In the spring of Union Year 198, at the end of January, the temperature in the Paldea Region gradually warmed up.

Today marks the fifth day since the construction of the Salt House, and after a five-day interval, the raising of Nacli and Skiddo officially got on track.

That morning, after feeding the chickens, D went to the Salt House.

After five days of continuous movement by the Naclis, a fine layer of Rock Salt granules had accumulated on the storage membrane of the Salt House.

Seeing this, D's face beamed with the joy of harvest.

With five Naclis nestled with Koraidon on one side, five Naclis were stacked together, snuggling obediently in Koraidon's arms.

D, along with Cyclizar, worked together to lift the storage membrane and collect the Rock Salt from it, storing it into sealed cloth bags.

The Naclis stayed in Koraidon's embrace, curiously watching this scene.

"Salt-salt..."

They suddenly realized that D seemed to really like the salt grains from their bodies!

This made the Naclis very happy. To them, D not only took them from the dangerous wild and cared for them.

He also created a very safe new home for them, bringing delicious food every day.

They made many new friends here as well, like the big sister-like Alcremie, the quietly gentle Smoliv.

This filled the Naclis' hearts with gratitude and emotion.

Upon seeing this scene, one of the Naclis happily jumped down from Koraidon, and under its lead, the other four swiftly followed suit.

They ran to D, making "Salt-salt" sounds while rubbing their bodies, causing more Rock Salt to fall off them.

It was like a snowfall, with snowflakes fluttering down and covering the ground.

# 50: Chapter 50: Steady Progress

Seeing the actions of the Nacli, D was slightly taken aback. "What are you doing?"

"Salty salt~"

The Nacli displayed happy smiles.

They really liked D and wanted to share the rock salt from their bodies with him.

That was what this group of Nacli were thinking in their hearts.

D had a general idea of the Nacli's intentions and remembered that it was a part of Nacli's ecological habits.

—They were willing to share their precious salt with those they liked, hence they were highly valued in the past.

So, were these five Nacli offering it to him because they saw him collecting salt grains and thought he really liked it?

D felt touched and commented with a wry smile, "Enough, enough! Moderation is key!"

The Nacli's bodies would wear down when they left salt traces.

Though the records didn't mention it, excessive wear all at once could surely be burdensome to their bodies.

Upon hearing this, the Nacli blinked innocently, "Salty?"

Is this much enough?

D crouched down, gently touching each of their little heads, saying, "I understand your goodwill, but it's okay, take it slow. You're still small, so you should take care of your bodies to grow healthy and evolve into mighty Garganacl."

"Salty…" The Nacli seemed to understand somewhat.

They knew that every Nacli would evolve into Garganacl when they grew up.

When that time came, the Garganacl could produce rock salt far exceeding the amount they could as Nacli.

They understood!

Lightbulbs lit up above the heads of the Nacli.

Evolve into Garganacl to produce more and better salt for the best D!

"Off you go!"

D had no idea what the Nacli were thinking.

He picked them up, handed them back to Koraidon, and continued collecting the rock salt spread on the membrane with Cyclizar.

Soon, a bag full of rock salt was collected.

D laid out the membrane again, then fed and filled the Naclis' water troughs, before heading back to the cabin.

He placed the bag on a scale to weigh it, and soon a line of numbers appeared on the screen.

It was the weight of this bag of rock salt: 3,250 grams.

"Three thousand two hundred and fifteen grams…" D calculated silently, "At an export price of 3,000 League Coins per 500 grams, this bag of rock salt will bring me 19,500 incomes. Wow, that's a bit beyond my expectations!"

"If this continues, soon I can expand my home, buy a refrigerator and a TV. Yes, a cellar has to be built too. In summer, I can grow hops and certain fruits, then make wine in the cellar."

The beer and fruit wine brewed would also amount to considerable income.

D felt joyful, securing the rock salt, preparing to sell it to Zack when he came to pick up the goods later in the evening.

However, before that, he had to set aside some for his own use.

Compared to normal salt, the rock salt from Nacli was more beneficial for body growth. If consumed over an extended period, not only him but even the Pokemon at the ranch would grow healthier and stronger.

Setting aside the rock salt, D headed to the sheep pen.

Today was milking day once again.

The three Skiddo had already been prepared. Seeing D approaching with a milk bucket, they immediately lined up obediently.

D casually asked, "Did you learn any new moves again?"

"Baa~!"

The Skiddo in the middle nodded its head.

It was the one that had battled Koraidon.

"Really?" D was a bit surprised, so as he squatted down to milk, he queried, "What move was it?"

The Skiddo then began to demonstrate.

Its body emitted a faint glow, but thereafter, nothing seemed to happen.

"Is that it?" D froze for a moment.

"Baa~" The Skiddo nodded joyfully.

It discovered that this place is really wonderful, with abundant grass to eat every day and no dangers.

Even more delightful was that here, it could learn new moves just by lying down!

"Show me again?" D hadn't quite caught it.

The Skiddo happily obliged, using the move once more.

This time, D realized.

"It's Synthesis!"

Synthesis: A Grass Type move, it restores its own stamina, with the recovery amount varying according to the weather.

D said, "That's a pretty good move."

Skiddo: "Baa!"

"Keep it up!"

D mentioned it casually. He didn't intend to send the Skiddo to fight.

However, becoming stronger is second nature to Pokemon. For the Skiddo, this life where it becomes stronger by resting must be quite wonderful.

Soon, the milk bucket was full, and D carried it back to the cabin.

His eyes scanned it, and the information on the goat milk appeared in his mind.

[Goat Milk (Top Quality): Goat milk at the pinnacle of quality and nutrition, extremely beneficial when consumed, can also be made into top-quality cheese, top-quality butter, and other foods.]

D didn't sell the first batch of goat milk but kept it for himself.

He only started selling after the second batch.

The benefits of that milk porridge were so noticeable that when Zack came to pick up goods a few days ago, he exclaimed "Good milk" at first sight of the goat milk.

Generally, buyers would price based on the milk's quality and nutritional value.

After a series of tests, he offered 600 League Coins for this top-quality goat milk, triple the previous 200.

D was very pleased with this.

The gentle spring breeze was pleasant.

After finishing the day's work, D carried a small stool to the field to check the crops' growth and pull some weeds.

After a month, the crops with shorter growth cycles like turnips, potatoes, garlic, and radishes were close to maturity.

Their roots dangled full and heavy fruits.

Of course, that's D's imagination, as these crops' fruits were buried in the ground and not visible.

As for cabbages, cucumbers, and tomatoes, they'd need more time to mature.

The few Berry trees planted at the field's edge were also growing.

But their growth was slow; they'd transform into big trees and bear fruit likely by autumn.

Just then, Dragonite, who had delivered the milk, returned.

Its face radiated excitement, and after landing, it looked around and spotted D in the field.

Dragonite raced over excitedly, extending its two plump paws.

Before D could react, he was enveloped in a big hug.

"Whoa~"

Maybe due to a strong desire to grow stronger, Dragonite had never had the kind of close bond with him like the Nacli and Alcremie had after joining the ranch.

Therefore, D was very surprised at Dragonite's sudden hug attack. "What's this about?"

Dragonite seemed very happy, "Roar!"

D had an inkling, "Did you master Dragon Tail?"

"Roar!"

Dragonite nodded repeatedly with joy.

Following D's guidance, it diligently practiced Dragon Tail every day, even while delivering goods.

It had just successfully executed it, causing Dragonite to have greater faith in D's advice.

Overcome with excitement, it dashed back and hugged D, who had helped it learn a new move.

"Congratulations!"

D was truly happy for Dragonite, thinking that this might just represent the essence of being a trainer?

Remembering this, he seized the chance to give Dragonite's plump belly a few pats.

It was so soft! What a nice feel!

D grinned.

The bond between person and dragon became stronger.
